# EFFECTS_QUICK_REFERENCE

- `SkipDrawReplacementEffect` — capability for a global static replacement that skips every card draw
- `PossessedPortalDrawReplacementEffect()` — every player's draw is replaced by skipping that draw; used by Possessed Portal
- `EachPlayerSacrificesPermanentUnlessDiscardEffect()` — each player in APNAP order sacrifices a permanent unless they discard a card; a single available action resolves automatically, while both actions open a choice
- `ExileTargetCreatureCardCreateTokenForOwnerEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` - activated ability: exile a target creature card from any graveyard, then create one copy of `tokenTemplate` under that card's owner's control. No token is created if the target leaves the graveyard before resolution (Pharika, God of Affliction)

- `RevealFirstDrawEffect(CardEffect onLand, CardEffect onNonland)` - controller-draw marker: reveal the first card drawn on each controller turn and route a land or nonland result through the normal draw-trigger pipeline (Keranos, God of Storms)

- `RevealTopCardDealManaValueDamageToAnyTargetEffect()` - reveal the top card of the controller's library, then deal damage equal to its mana value to the chosen any-target; the card stays on top (Riddle of Lightning, after `ScryEffect(3)`)

- `RevealTopCardDrawManaValueEffect()` - reveal the top card of the controller's library; draw cards equal to its mana value; the card stays on top and is the first card drawn when the mana value is greater than zero (Interpret the Signs, after `ScryEffect(3)`)

- `TopCardOfLibraryManaValue()` - `DynamicAmount` for the mana value of the controller's current top library card, or zero for an empty library; pair with `RevealTopCardOfLibraryEffect` and `BoostSelfEffect` when a revealed top card determines a self-boost (Stormchaser Chimera)

- `PreventAllDamageToSelfFromTargetCreatureEffect()` — activated ability: prevent all damage from the targeted creature to the ability's source permanent this turn (Stonewise Fortifier). Uses a turn-scoped target/source shield, so the creature can still damage other recipients and other creatures can still damage the source.

- `ExtraTurnSkipReplacementEffect` — capability for a static replacement that skips an extra turn as it would begin while the source remains on the battlefield
- `ExileAndTakeExtraTurnReplacementEffect` — capability for a self-replacement that exiles the source from the battlefield instead of putting it into a graveyard and queues an extra turn for its last controller
- `UginNexusReplacementEffect()` — combines the two Ugin's Nexus replacement capabilities
- `PayXManaDrawXCardsEffect()` — life-gain trigger: controller chooses X up to the life gained and available mana, pays generic mana, and draws X cards. The life-gain amount is snapshotted when the trigger is collected; X=0 declines. Well of Lost Dreams (`ON_CONTROLLER_GAINS_LIFE`)

- `AllCardsAreColorlessEffect()` — STATIC global color-setting effect: every permanent, spell, and card not on the battlefield is colorless. Battlefield permanents use the layered static pass; spells and other-zone cards use `GameQueryService.getEffectiveCardColors` (Mycosynth Lattice)
- `SpendManaAsAnyColorEffect()` — STATIC global mana permission: players may spend mana as though it were mana of any color. Reflected into each player's `ManaPool` and honored by `ManaCost` affordability/payment paths (Mycosynth Lattice)

- `PutCreatureFromHandWithManaValueEqualToSourceCountersEffect(CounterType)` — activated ability: you may put a creature card from hand onto the battlefield when its mana value equals the number of the given counter type on the source permanent (Aether Vial)

- `JeweledBirdAnteEffect()` — activated ability: ante the source artifact, move the controller's other anted cards to their owners' graveyards, then draw a card. The ante zone is represented by exile plus `GameData.antedCardIds`, so unrelated exile entries are untouched. Jeweled Bird

- `CreateTokenAndLinkToSourceEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` — creates the wrapped token(s), records them under the source in `GameData.sourceCreatedTokens`, and gives each token a back-reference to the source for a paired leaves-the-battlefield trigger. If the source has already left before resolution, the token is still created without any link. Stangg
- `ExileTokensCreatedWithSourceEffect()` — `ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD`: exiles every surviving token tracked as created by the source, clearing each token's back-reference first so its reciprocal trigger cannot return to the departed source. Stangg

- `GrantChosenLandwalkEffect(GrantScope scope)` — prompts for a basic land type, then grants the matching plain landwalk until end of turn to the source (`SELF`) or target (`TARGET`) creature. Uses the basic-land-type choice protocol. Giant Slug uses the self form from its delayed upkeep trigger.
- `RegisterChosenLandwalkAtNextUpkeepEffect()` — activated-ability registrar that queues a one-shot `GrantChosenLandwalkAtNextUpkeep` for the activating player's next upkeep. The delayed trigger carries the source permanent id and pushes `GrantChosenLandwalkEffect(SELF)` onto the stack; it is discarded if the source has left the battlefield. Giant Slug.
- `GrantTargetingRestrictionToTargetUntilEndOfTurnEffect(TargetingRestrictionEffect)` — target permanent gains the supplied spells/abilities targeting restriction until end of turn. Use `TargetingRestrictionEffect.opponentSpellsAndAbilities()` for Vines of Vastwood, where the restriction is not the hexproof keyword and is not bypassed by hexproof-ignoring effects.

- `PutCountersOnSourceCardEffect(CounterType[, int count])` — counters on the battlefield permanent represented by the ability's source card; use for a resolution-time may trigger collected from another permanent's event when the text says to put counters on the ability source (Beastmaster Ascension)
- `ReduceColoredCastCostForMatchingSpellsEffect(CardPredicate, ManaCost, CostModificationScope)` — matching spells lose only matching colored mana components from their cast cost; unmatched colored reduction does not reduce generic mana (Ragemonger uses `new ManaCost("{B}{R}")`)

- `PayXManaPutCountersOnSelfEffect(CounterType counterType)` — resolution-time "you may pay {X}. If you do, put X counters on this": prompts X up to available mana, pays it, and puts X counters of the specified type on the source permanent (X=0 declines). Hero of Leina Tower.

- `ExchangeControlOfSourceAndTriggeringSpellEffect()` — exchanges control of the source permanent and the spell that caused its spell-cast trigger; preserves the spell's owner for zone changes and offers new target choices after the exchange when applicable (Perplexing Chimera)

- `TributeEffect(int counterCount)` — opponent's as-enters tribute choice; the entering permanent records whether the required +1/+1 counters were actually placed.
- `TributeNotPaidEffect(CardEffect wrapped)` — ETB intervening-if wrapper; resolves to `wrapped` only when tribute was not paid.
- `AdditionalDamageFromColorSpellsEffect(Set<CardColor>, int)` — STATIC: a spell of any listed color deals that much damage plus N to a permanent or player instead (Sulfuric Vapors). Global across controllers, spells only, and applies to both damage destinations; stacks additively and only when damage > 0. Queried by `GameQueryService.getSpellDamageBonus`
- `ExileCardsFromGraveyardEffect(int maxTargets, int lifeGain, boolean singleGraveyard)` — activated-ability/hand-ability form that requires all directly supplied targets to share one graveyard; Carrion Beetles uses `(3, 0, true)`
- `CopySpellForEachPriorInstantOrSorceryEffect()` — `ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL`: when the controller casts an instant or sorcery, queues a `StormCopyEffect` with one copy for each other instant or sorcery that controller cast earlier in the turn. The count and spell snapshot are fixed when the trigger is collected (Thousand-Year Storm)

- `ReduceBuybackCostEffect(int)` — static battlefield effect: reduces the generic mana component of every mana buyback cost by N while the source remains on the battlefield (Memory Crystal). It affects all players' buyback costs, not the spell's normal cast cost or non-mana buyback costs

- `FlipCoinForEachBlockingCreatureEffect()` — for each blocking creature, flip independently; on a win, prevent all combat damage that creature would deal this turn (Fighting Chance). Resolves against the blockers currently on the battlefield and adds winning blockers to `GameData.creaturesPreventedFromDealingCombatDamage`, cleared at turn cleanup

- `MoveCountersFromAllCreaturesToSourceEffect(CounterType)` — moves all counters of the given kind from every creature on every battlefield onto the source permanent, including the source when it is a creature. Resolves from the source permanent id and is untargeted; the source must still be on the battlefield. Uses shared counter placement for counter locks, replacement effects, and counter triggers. Spike Cannibal (`PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`)
`CantAttackUnlessReturnToHandEffect(count, PermanentPredicate, description)` is a STATIC additional attack cost: return `count` matching permanents to their owners' hands as attackers are declared. It is read by `AttackReturnToHandCostService`; pair it with a `CantAttackUnlessEffect(new ControlsPermanentCount(count, filter), …)` legality gate (Floodtide Serpent).
- `GrantEffectToSourceEffect(EffectSlot slot, CardEffect grantedEffect)` — when resolved, grants the source permanent an indefinite triggered ability. Use for ETB effects such as “it gains ‘When this creature dies, …’”; persistent source grants are included in self-death trigger collection.
Thought Prison uses `ChooseCardsFromTargetHandEffect(..., HandChoiceDestination.EXILE, true)` to store the selected exiled card on the source permanent for imprint-based trigger predicates.

- `KrarksThumbEffect()` — static replacement effect: each logical coin flip made by its controller flips two coins and keeps the preferred result. Count all copies controlled by the flipping player; the replacements apply once each, so every logical flip resolves to one chosen result.
- `ExileAllOpponentsHandsEffect()` — non-targeting exile of every card from every opponent's hand; it is not a discard.
- `ExileTopCardsToSourceEffect(count, false, false, TARGET_OPPONENT, true)` — target opponent exiles the top cards of their library, tracked with the source.
- `ReturnCardExiledWithSourceToBattlefieldEffect(filter, requiresManaValueEqualsX, grantedSubtype)` — returns a matching source-exiled card under the ability controller's control, optionally filtering by the loyalty X and granting a persistent subtype.

- `EachTargetCreatureDealsPowerDamageToSourceEffect()` — every creature in the bound target group deals damage equal to its power to the ability's source permanent; used by Polukranos, World Eater.
- `MonstrosityEffect(DynamicAmount amount)` / `MonstrosityEffect(int amount)` — resolves monstrosity once, evaluating the amount from the stack entry (including X).
- `DealDividedDamageEffect.xAmongTargetCreaturesAtResolution()` — keeps a target-creature group on a trigger and prompts for each positive damage allocation while the ability resolves.
- `CastMatchingInstantOrSorceryFromGraveyardWithoutPayingManaCostEffect(CardPredicate)` — offer each matching instant or sorcery in the controller's graveyard for a free cast; non-targeting and reuses the normal graveyard-cast flow

- `PutSourceAndBlockingCreaturesOnTopOfLibraryEffect([includeCreaturesBlockingSource])` - put the source permanent and creatures it is blocking on top of their owners' libraries, optionally also including creatures blocking it, then shuffle each affected owner's library (Gomazoa, Vortex Elemental)
- `AllowPlayMatchingCardsFromGraveyardThisTurnEffect(CardPredicate filter)` — grants play permission to matching cards currently in the controller's graveyard until end of turn; covers both casting spells and playing lands (Kethis, the Hidden Hand)
- `PlayLandsFromTopOfLibraryEffect()` — static permission to play lands from the top of your library; normal land-play timing, land-per-turn limits, and land-play restrictions still apply (Courser of Kruphix)
- `ExileOwnCardsInsteadOfGraveyardUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — turn-scoped replacement that exiles the controller's cards instead of putting them into that player's graveyard; used by Yawgmoth's Will

- `ReturnEnchantedCreatureToBattlefieldOnDeathEffect(boolean underAuraControllersControl[, CounterType enterWithCounter])` — Aura death trigger returning the enchanted creature to the battlefield, optionally with one counter as it enters; Unholy Indenture uses `PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`.

- `GainActivatedAbilitiesOfTargetCreatureUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — source gains the target creature's currently effective activated abilities until end of turn
- `SpendBlueManaAsAnyColorForActivatedAbilitiesEffect()` — source-scoped static permission for a creature's activated ability costs; blue mana may pay colored requirements of those costs
- `EquipAbilitiesCanBeActivatedAtInstantSpeedEffect()` — controller-scoped static permission that lets equip abilities be activated at instant speed; queried by `GameQueryService` during activation legality and auto-pass checks (Leonin Shikari)
- `DealDamageToTargetPlayerEqualToChosenColorCardsInHandEffect()` — controller chooses a color, then the target player reveals their hand and takes damage equal to the number of cards of that color. The effect pauses for the resolution-time color choice and routes damage through `DamageSupport` (Darigaaz, the Igniter).

- `VoidEffect()` — controller chooses a number, then all artifacts and creatures with that mana value are destroyed and a target player reveals their hand and discards all nonland cards with that mana value. The number is chosen at resolution and stored on `GameData` while the effect is rerun.

- `ChooseColorThenDiscardFromTargetHandEffect()` — controller chooses a color, then target player reveals their hand and the controller chooses one card of that color for the target to discard. Pair with `target(PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(ANY))`; player-targeting spec (Addle)

Protective Sphere uses `PreventDamageFromChosenSourceEffect.allDamageToYouOfActivationManaColor()`. Its source-choice filter is snapshotted from the colored mana spent on the activation; colorless mana yields no legal source choice.

Compact lookup: effect name + constructor signature, organized by category.

- `OpponentDrewAtLeastCardsThisTurn(n)` — an opponent drew at least `n` cards this turn; use as an alternate-cast availability condition for Runeflare Trap.

- `DealDamageToEachOpponentEqualToCardsInHandEffect()` — deal damage to each opponent equal to the number of cards that opponent has in hand; evaluates the amount independently for each opponent. Stormbreath Dragon
- `BoostCreatedTokensEffect(powerBoost, toughnessBoost)` — gives every token created earlier in the same spell or ability resolution a +P/+T modifier until end of turn. Put it after the token-creation effect; the handler reads `StackEntry.createdPermanentIds`. Wrap it in `ConditionalEffect(new Kicked(), …)` for a kicked-only token boost (Elemental Appeal).

- `DestroyEnchantedCreatureOnLeaveEffect()` / `(UUID enchantedPermanentId, boolean cannotBeRegenerated)` — `ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD`: destroy the creature the source Aura enchanted. The collector captures the host's permanent ID before the Aura disappears; use the card-definition constructor for the default no-regeneration form
- `DistributeCountersAmongTargetsEffect.chosenAmongTargetCreaturesEtb(CounterType, int)` — fixed chosen counter distribution for an ETB ability; target-to-counter assignments come from the shared ETB assignment buffer (Wurmskin Forger)
For detailed descriptions, targeting info, and examples, see EFFECTS_INDEX.md.

`SacrificeCreatureThenMassDamageEqualToPowerEffect()` — resolution-time sacrifice, then damage equal to the sacrificed creature's power to each creature without flying and each player (Rupture); no creature means no damage.

`EachPlayerExilesFromHandEffect(N)` — each player exiles N cards from hand (their choice), non-targeting, APNAP (Mind Swords). Fewer than N means the whole hand. Reuses `EXILE_FROM_HAND_CHOICE` with a remaining-choosers queue.
- `DrawCardsForEachOpponentOfTriggeringPlayerEffect(DynamicAmount|int)` — each opponent of the player who caused a spell-cast trigger draws the evaluated amount. Standstill uses `(3)` after sacrificing its source.
- `RedirectChosenColorSpellDamageToControllerEffect()` — static replacement; an instant or sorcery spell of the source permanent's chosen color that would deal damage to its controller instead deals that damage to the spell's controller (Harsh Judgment).

`EachPlayerSacrificesOneOfEachTypeEffect()` — each player chooses an artifact, creature, enchantment, land, and planeswalker in active-player order; a multi-typed permanent may satisfy more than one type, and choosing it more than once has the same result as choosing it once. All chosen permanents are sacrificed together. Used by Release (Catch // Release)
`RegisterEchoAtNextUpkeepEffect(String manaCost)` registers a one-shot echo pay-or-sacrifice trigger for the source permanent's next applicable controller upkeep. The `EchoAtNextUpkeep` action follows the permanent's current controller and is discarded if the permanent leaves; the upkeep handler pushes `ForcedCostOrElseEffect(PayManaCost, [SacrificeSelfEffect], true)`.

- `ChooseOpponentGainsControlOfSourceEffect()` — non-targeting (`ControlStealingEffect`, PERMANENT): the ability controller chooses one of their opponents; that player gains permanent control of the source permanent. With one opponent the choice is automatic; with multiple opponents it uses the shared player-choice prompt. Goblin Festival
`PlayerWithLowestLifeGainsControlOfSourceCreatureEffect()` - Loxodon Peacekeeper: `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`; the player with the lowest life gains permanent control of this creature. If players are tied for lowest life, the source's controller chooses one of them.

`EachPlayerSacrificesOneOfEachTypeEffect()` - each player chooses an artifact, creature, enchantment, land, and planeswalker in active-player order; a multi-typed permanent may satisfy more than one type, and choosing it more than once has the same result as choosing it once. All chosen permanents are sacrificed together. Used by Release (Catch // Release)

`StrongholdGambitEffect()` — each player makes a mandatory hidden card choice from hand; all chosen cards are then revealed, and every creature card tied for the lowest mana value enters under its owner's control. Choices are collected in APNAP order, and tied winners enter as one simultaneous batch.
- `RevealTopCardsOfTargetPlayerUntilInstantOrSorceryAndCastEffect()` — target player exiles from the top of their library until an instant or sorcery is found; the controller may cast it for free, then the remaining revealed cards are put on the bottom of that library in a random order (Chaos Wand). Reuses the `CAST_WITHOUT_PAYING` library-search flow.

`EachPlayerChoosesLandOfEachBasicTypeThenSacrificeRestEffect()` — each player chooses one land with each basic land type they control in active-player order; a land with multiple basic land types may satisfy multiple choices, and all other lands are sacrificed after every player chooses. Used by Global Ruin

`ChooseLandOfEachBasicTypeThenDestroyEffect()` — the controller chooses one land with each basic land type from among all battlefields; a land with multiple basic land types may satisfy multiple choices, and the chosen lands are destroyed simultaneously. Used by Sundering Titan

`BendOrBreakEffect()` — each player separates their nontoken lands into two piles, chooses an opponent to choose one of those piles, destroys the chosen piles, and taps the other piles. Uses a queued multi-player interaction flow; chosen lands are destroyed with normal regeneration rules and the other lands are tapped. Used by Bend or Break

`WhimsOfTheFatesEffect()` — starting with the spell's controller, each player separates all permanents they control into three piles by choosing Pile 1 and Pile 2; the remaining permanents form Pile 3. One pile is selected uniformly at random for each player, and all selected permanents are sacrificed together. Uses a queued multi-player interaction flow.

`PutAttackingCreaturesOnTopOrBottomOfLibraryEffect()` — for each attacking creature, its owner chooses top or bottom of their library; resolution prompts each owner through the shared multi-permanent choice flow (Aetherspouts).

`PutMatchingPermanentsOnTopOfOwnersLibrariesEffect(filter)` — puts every matching permanent on top of its owner's library; each owner chooses the order when they have multiple matching permanents (Harmonic Convergence).

- `PreventAllDamageToTargetFromChosenColorEffect()` — activated ability: on resolution, choose a color, then prevent all damage from that color to the target creature, player, or planeswalker until end of turn (Avacyn, Guardian Angel). Uses a target-specific turn-scoped shield and does not grant protection.

Protection from multicolored sources uses `ProtectionFromMulticoloredEffect(GrantScope scope)`;
`GrantScope.ENCHANTED_CREATURE` is the Aura form used by Guildscorn Ward.

`CantBeEquippedEffect()` is a static marker that prevents Equipment from attaching to the permanent carrying it; equipment attachment helpers and ongoing attachment legality checks read it.

**How to use:** Search this file for keywords from the card text (e.g. "graveyard", "life", "shuffle", "destroy"). Once you find a candidate effect, grep EFFECTS_INDEX.md for its name to get full details.

Implementation note: `ExileTargetPermanentUntilSourceLeavesEffect(boolean imprint, PermanentPredicate targetPredicate)` supports narrowed harmful permanent targets; `GrantTriggeredAbilityEffect` grants `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD` abilities from the entering permanent's current static grants.

- `RegisterDelayedReturnSourceTransformedEffect()` — ON_DEATH effect that registers a delayed end-step return from the source card's owner's graveyard to the battlefield transformed. Used by Loyal Cathar-style "When this dies, return it transformed at the beginning of the next end step." `(true, true)` = only on sacrifice + under owner's control (Biolume Egg).
- `ReturnSourceTransformedFromGraveyardEffect()` — ON_DEATH effect that returns the source card from its owner's graveyard to the battlefield transformed **immediately on resolution**, under the trigger controller's control. Harvest Hand // Scrounged Scythe. Immediate sibling of `RegisterDelayedReturnSourceTransformedEffect` (no end-step wait); both share `GraveyardTransformedReturnService`. Fizzles if the card is no longer in a graveyard.
- `RegisterDelayedSelfReturnFromGraveyardEffect(CounterType, int)` — registers a delayed end-step return of the SOURCE CARD from its owner's graveyard to the battlefield under that owner's control, entering with the given counters (`()` = no counters). Sand Golem = `ON_SELF_DISCARDED_BY_OPPONENT` + `(PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE, 1)`. Fizzles if the card isn't in a graveyard when the trigger resolves; the delayed return itself is skipped if the card has left by the end step. Untransformed sibling of `RegisterDelayedReturnSourceTransformedEffect` (queues `DelayedGraveyardToBattlefieldSelfReturn`, drained in `StepTriggerService.handleEndStepTriggers`). `RegisterDelayedSelfReturnFromGraveyardEffect.tappedAtOwnersNextUpkeep()` instead returns it **tapped** at the beginning of the **owner's** next upkeep (`atNextUpkeep`/`tapped` flags on the action; drained in `handleUpkeepTriggers` only when the owner is the active player) — Phytotitan `ON_DEATH`.
- `RegisterDelayedSelfReturnFromGraveyardWithOneFewerCounterEffect(CounterType)` — `ON_DEATH` marker that fires only when the dying source had at least one counter of the given type, snapshots that count, and registers the ordinary delayed self-return with one fewer counter. Nine-Lives Familiar uses `REVIVAL`.
- `RegisterDelayedReturnTargetAndSacrificedCardsEffect()` — spell effect for Rescue from the Underworld: queues the targeted creature card and the creature sacrificed as the additional cost in one `DelayedGraveyardCardsToBattlefieldUnderControl` action for the controller's next upkeep. Cards still in graveyards enter simultaneously under the controller's control; cards that left their graveyards are skipped.
- `RegisterDelayedCreateTokenEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenEffect)` — registers a delayed trigger that resolves `tokenEffect` at the beginning of the next end step, creating the token(s) under the resolving controller's control. Used by Rukh Egg-style "When this dies, create a ... token at the beginning of the next end step."
- `RegisterDelayedLoseLifeAndReturnFromGraveyardToHandEffect(int lifeLoss)` — registers a delayed trigger that at the next end step pushes `LoseLifeEffect(lifeLoss)` + self-return `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect` onto the stack as one ability. Brood of Cockroaches (`1`).
- `NecropotenceSetAsideTopCardEffect()` — `CardDrawingEffect` (1). Activated-ability effect (Necropotence `Pay 1 life:`): exile the top library card face down, then return it to hand at the **controller's own** next end step via a `ReturnExiledCardToHandAtEndStep` delayed action (drained in `StepTriggerService.handleEndStepTriggers`, `activePlayerId == ownerId`). Pair with `PayLifeCost(1)` in the ability's effect list.
- `ExileDiscardedCardFromGraveyardEffect([boolean trackWithSource])` — discard-trigger marker: "whenever you discard a card, exile that card from your graveyard" (Necropotence). The default is collected inline; `trackWithSource=true` queues the trigger and tracks the discarded card with the triggering permanent (Bag of Holding). `withStashCounter()` queues the trigger and marks the card with a stash counter without source tracking (Tinybones, Bauble Burglar). The tracked forms recheck the card's graveyard presence at resolution.
- `AllowCastFromCardsExiledWithSourceEffect.forStashCounters(boolean)` — static permission for the controller to cast cards with stash counters that they don't own during their turn; the boolean allows mana of any type to be spent (Tinybones, Bauble Burglar).
- `RegisterDelayedReturnDamagedCreatureUnderControlEffect()` — `ON_DAMAGED_CREATURE_DIES` effect (Seraph). Returns the creature that died onto the battlefield under your control at the next end step (via `DelayedGraveyardToBattlefieldUnderControl`, drained in `StepTriggerService`), with a PERMANENT control effect so a stolen opponent's creature stays yours yet dies to its owner's graveyard; linked creatures are sacrificed when their controller loses control of the still-present Seraph (`StateBasedActionService`). Reads the dying card id from `StackEntry.triggeringCardId`. The real class name is `RegisterDelayedReturnDyingCreatureUnderControlEffect`; its `(boolean requireSourceOnBattlefield)` overload is Shirei, Shizo's Caretaker's "if Shirei is still on the battlefield" — on `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DIES` wrapped in a `MayEffect`, where the collector binds the dying card id via `DyingCreatureCardAwareEffect`. `forOpponent()` is the targeted-opponent variant: the chosen opponent becomes the delayed return controller (Endless Whispers).
- `ReturnDyingDamagedCreatureUnderControlEffect(CardColor grantColor, CardSubtype grantSubtype)` — `ON_DAMAGED_CREATURE_DIES` effect (Dread Slaver). Returns the creature that died from its owner's graveyard to the battlefield under your control immediately, granting it the (nullable) colour/subtype persistently — "that creature is a black Zombie in addition to its other colors and types". No end-step delay and no control-loss sacrifice, unlike Seraph/Krovikan Vampire.
- `ReturnDyingCreatureToOwnerBattlefieldEffect(CounterType, int, CardSubtype, Set<Keyword>)` — `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DIES` effect (Valkyrie's Call). Returns the bound nontoken, non-Angel dying creature from its owner's graveyard under its owner's control, applying persistent subtype/keyword grants and the configured enter-with counters. Fizzles if the card left the graveyard or is blocked from entering.
- `ReturnDamagedCreaturesThatDiedUnderControlEffect()` — `ControlStealingEffect` / end-step (Krovikan Vampire). Wrap in `ConditionalEffect(new SourceDamagedCreatureDiedThisTurn(), …)` on `END_STEP_TRIGGERED`. Puts each still-in-graveyard creature card this source damaged that died this turn onto the battlefield under your control; same `seraphReturnedCreatures` control-loss sacrifice linkage as Seraph. Intervening-if requires the source on the battlefield at the beginning of the end step.
- `DealDamageToLastRedSpellDamagerEffect(int amount)` — deals N damage to the controller of the last red instant/sorcery spell that damaged this effect's controller this turn (untargeted; reads `GameData.lastRedSpellDamagerThisTurn`). Gate the cast with `setCastCondition(new DealtDamageByRedSpellThisTurn())`. Suffocation.
- `RegisterDrawCardsAtNextUpkeepEffect(int count)` / `()` — `CardDrawingEffect`; schedules the resolving controller to draw `count` cards (default 1) at the beginning of the next turn's upkeep via a `DrawCardsAtNextUpkeep` delayed action. Used by Blessed Wine "Draw a card at the beginning of the next turn's upkeep." `RegisterDrawCardsAtNextUpkeepEffect.targetPlayer(count)` schedules it for the **targeted** player instead (benign `PLAYER` spec) — Sapphire Charm "Target player draws a card at the beginning of the next turn's upkeep." `RegisterDrawCardsAtNextUpkeepEffect.targetSpellControllerMayDrawUpTo(count)` schedules an optional "up to count" draw for the controller of the **targeted spell** (read off the stack, so list it before the counter) — Arcane Denial. `RegisterDrawCardsAtNextUpkeepEffect.targetGraveyardOwner(count)` schedules it for the owner of the graveyard holding the entry's targeted cards (read out of the graveyards, so list it before the effect that moves them) — Lodestone Bauble "That player draws a card at the beginning of the next turn's upkeep."
- `DrawUpToNCardsEffect(int max)` — `CardDrawingEffect`; the resolving controller picks 0..`max` via an `XValueChoice` prompt and draws that many. Used as the delayed upkeep trigger pushed by `DrawCardsAtNextUpkeep` with `upTo=true` (Arcane Denial).
- `MakeTargetAttackingCreatureBlockedEffect()` — "Target unblocked attacking creature becomes blocked" (CR 509.1h). Sets `Permanent.blockedWithoutBlockers`; the creature is blocked with no blockers, so it assigns no combat damage (CR 510.1c). Works on creatures that can't be blocked. Fires the non-per-blocker `ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED` / aura / ally becomes-blocked triggers via `CombatBlockService.fireBecomesBlockedTriggersWithoutBlockers`. Target: `TargetFilters.unblockedAttackingCreature()`, or an X-scaled `targetX(TargetFilters.attackingCreature(), cap)` group (the handler fans over `getTargetIds()` and skips targets already blocked by a creature). Dazzling Beauty, Choking Vines
- `MakeAllAttackingCreaturesBlockedEffect()` — makes every current attacking creature blocked without adding blockers. Reuses the same no-blocker state and non-per-blocker becomes-blocked trigger path as `MakeTargetAttackingCreatureBlockedEffect`. Fog Patch.
- `RegisterTransformSourceAtNextUpkeepEffect()` — ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DIES registrar (Archangel Avacyn). Queues a `TransformSourceAtNextUpkeep` delayed action; at the next upkeep (any player) pushes `TransformToBackFaceEffect` onto the stack (no-op if already transformed / gone).
- `TransformToBackFaceEffect()` — transform source to back face only if still front-face; no-op if already transformed
- `MeldWithNamedCreatureEffect(String partnerName)` — exile source + owned/controlled creature named `partnerName`, meld into source `backFaceCard` (Gisela → Brisela). Track components on `Permanent.meldComponentCards`.
- `RegisterDamageAtNextUpkeepUnlessPaysEffect(int damage, String manaCost)` — SPELL registrar (Quenchable Fire). Piggybacks on the accompanying `DealDamageToTargetPlayerOrPlaneswalkerEffect`'s target (declares no target of its own) and schedules a `DamageAtNextUpkeepUnlessPays` delayed action, drained in `StepTriggerService.handleUpkeepTriggers` at the **spell controller's** next upkeep. The paying party (targeted player, or targeted planeswalker's controller) may pay `manaCost` or the target takes `damage` (pushed as a `DamageTargetPlayerOrPlaneswalkerUnlessPaysEffect` may-pay trigger). Fizzles if the target is gone. Pair with `DealDamageToTargetPlayerOrPlaneswalkerEffect(damage)` in the same SPELL slot for the immediate damage.
- `RegisterCombatDamageReflectionEffect()` — "This turn, whenever an attacking creature deals combat damage to you, it deals that much damage to its controller" (Harsh Justice). Registers a `DelayedCombatDamageReflection` for the rest of the turn; `CombatDamageService` reflects each attacking creature's player-damage back to its controller. Pair with `setSpellCastTimingRestriction(DECLARE_ATTACKERS_IF_ATTACKED)`.
- `RegisterDelayedControllerSpellCastTriggerEffect(CardPredicate spellFilter, List<CardEffect> resolvedEffects)` — "Until end of turn, whenever you cast a [filter] spell, [effects]" (Mountain Titan: `CardColorPredicate(BLACK)` + `PutCountersOnSourceEffect(1, 1, 1)`). Registers a `DelayedControllerSpellCastTrigger` for the rest of the turn; `TriggerCollectionService` fires one trigger per matching spell the registering controller casts, carrying `sourcePermanentId` so source-referencing effects resolve. Skipped once the source permanent leaves the battlefield; cleared at turn cleanup. `spellFilter = null` = any spell.
- `RegisterDelayedCreatureDeathTriggerEffect(CardEffect effect)` — "Whenever a creature dies this turn, [effect]". Registers a `CreatureDeathTriggerWatcher` in `GameData`; `TriggerCollectionService` pushes one non-targeting trigger per creature death, including tokens. The watcher outlives the source card and is cleared at turn cleanup. Death Frenzy uses `new GainLifeEffect(1)`.
- `RegisterDelayedBlockerBoostEffect(int power, int toughness)` — "Whenever a creature blocks this turn, it gets +X/+Y until end of turn" (Battle Cry). Registers a `DelayedBlockerBoost` for the rest of the turn; `CombatBlockService` fires one `BoostSelfEffect` trigger per unique blocker declared. Cleared at turn cleanup.
- `MillAndRegisterDelayedAttackerBoostEffect(int millCount, int powerPerCreature, int toughnessPerCreature)` — mill N from controller, then "whenever a creature attacks this turn, it gets +P/+T until end of turn for each creature card put into your graveyard this way" (Song of Blood: `(4, 1, 0)`). Registers a `DelayedAttackerBoost` with the snapshotted creature-milled total; `CombatAttackService` fires one `BoostSelfEffect` per attacker. Cleared at turn cleanup. Skips registration when the total boost is +0/+0.
- `RegisterDelayedUnblockedAttackerPowerDamageEffect()` — "Until end of turn, whenever a creature you control attacks and isn't blocked, you may have it deal damage equal to its power to a target creature. If you do, it assigns no combat damage this turn" (Gaze of Pain). Registers a `DelayedUnblockedAttackerPowerDamage`; `CombatBlockService` queues a CR 603.5 may (`DealDamageToTargetCreatureEffect(SourcePower())` + `AssignNoCombatDamageEffect`) per unblocked attacker. Cleared at turn cleanup.
- `RegisterDelayedUnblockedAttackerUntapRemoveFromCombatEffect()` — "Whenever a creature attacks and isn't blocked this combat, untap it and remove it from combat" (Melee). Registers a `DelayedUnblockedAttackerUntapRemoveFromCombat`; `CombatBlockService` pushes `UntapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.TARGET)` + `RemoveTargetFromCombatEffect()` per unblocked attacker (any player's). Cleared at end of combat.
- `ChooseBlockersThisCombatEffect()` — "You choose which creatures block this combat and how those creatures block" (Melee; Odric, Master Tactician via `ON_ATTACK` + `MinimumAttackers(4)`). Registers a `DelayedBlockerDeclarationControl`; the declare-blockers `PendingInteraction` gets `chooserId` = the resolving controller. Cleared at end of combat.
- `RegisterDelayedSacrificeSourceWhenTargetLeavesEffect()` — "When that creature leaves the battlefield this turn, sacrifice this creature" (Kjeldoran Elite Guard). Pair with `BoostTargetCreatureEffect` on the shared creature target; registers a `DelayedSacrificeSourceWhenTargetLeaves`. Fired from every leave path in `PermanentRemovalService`. Cleared at turn cleanup.
- `RegisterDelayedDestroyCreatureDamagedByTargetEffect()` — "Whenever target creature deals combat damage to a non-Wall creature this turn, destroy that non-Wall creature" (Acidic Dagger). Registers a `DelayedDestroyCreatureDamagedByWatchedCreature`; `CombatDamageService` fires one non-targeting `DestroyTargetPermanentEffect` trigger per damaged non-Wall creature, in every combat damage step of the turn. Cleared at turn cleanup.
- `RegisterDelayedCombatDamageDrawEffect()` — "Whenever a creature you control deals combat damage to a player or planeswalker this turn, draw a card." Registers a `DelayedCombatDamageDraw`; `CombatDamageService` fires one non-targeting draw trigger per qualifying creature per combat damage step, including damage to planeswalkers. Cleared at turn cleanup.
- `RegisterDelayedWatchedCreaturesCombatDamageEffect(List<CardEffect> effects)` — "Choose up to two target creatures. Until your next turn, whenever either of those creatures deals combat damage, <effects>" (Tamiyo, Field Researcher +1, with `DrawCardEffect(1)`). Registers a `DelayedWatchedCreaturesCombatDamage` keyed by the chosen permanent ids; `CombatDamageService` fires one non-targeting trigger per watched creature that dealt positive combat damage to anything (player, planeswalker, or creature). The trigger is controlled by the player who activated the ability, so "you draw" draws for them even when the watched creature is an opponent's. Expires at that controller's next turn start.
- `RegisterDelayedWatchedCreatureDealsDamageEffect(List<CardEffect> effects)` — "Until end of turn, whenever target creature deals damage, <effects>" (Spiritualize). Registers a `DelayedWatchedCreatureDealsDamage` keyed by the target permanent id; combat and noncombat damage paths fire one controller-owned trigger with the summed damage amount. Cleared during end-of-turn cleanup.
- `RegisterDelayedDestroyCreatureDealingCombatDamageToPlaneswalkerEffect()` — "Until your next turn, whenever a creature deals combat damage to [this planeswalker], destroy that creature" (Vraska the Unseen). Registers a `DelayedDestroyCreatureDealingCombatDamageToPlaneswalker` keyed to the source permanent; `CombatDamageService` fires one non-targeting `DestroyTargetPermanentEffect` trigger per creature that dealt positive combat damage to that planeswalker. Expires at the ability controller's next turn start.
- `RegisterDelayedSacrificeTargetWhenSourceLeavesEffect()` — "When this creature leaves the battlefield this turn, sacrifice that creature" (Phantasmal Mount). Pair with `BoostTargetCreatureEffect` + `RegisterDelayedSacrificeSourceWhenTargetLeavesEffect` on the shared target; registers a `DelayedSacrificeTargetWhenSourceLeaves`. Fired from every leave path in `PermanentRemovalService`. Cleared at turn cleanup.

## Targeting rules (summary)

- An effect declares targeting by overriding `targetSpec()` to a non-NONE `TargetSpec` (a `TargetPredicates` factory + `harmful` flag + optional `PermanentPredicate`); the deleted legacy `canTarget*` booleans derived from it. See `EFFECTS_INDEX.md` § "Effect targeting declarations".
- Effects in SPELL slot whose `targetSpec()` admits players force player targeting at cast time; those admitting permanents force permanent targeting (and the declared target/predicate type-check it).
- ETB/triggered/saga slots: targeting declarations don't force spell-level targeting.
- `CostEffect` subtypes are excluded from targeting computation.
- Targeting is computed by `EffectResolution.needsTarget(card)` / `needsSpellCastTarget(card)`.
- Never call `setNeedsTarget`/`setNeedsSpellTarget` directly.

## Capability / marker interfaces

DESCRIPTIVE interfaces in `model/effect/` that a family of effects implements so the AI
(`SpellEvaluator`, `InstantCategoryClassifier`, `AiTargetSelector`, `HardAiDecisionEngine`,
`GameSimulator`, `AiManaManager`) can read a FACT about the effect instead of `instanceof`-ing each
concrete type. **When you add a new effect that fits one of these families, implement the interface**
- `TargetPlayerGainsControlOfTargetPermanentEffect()` â€” targeted `ControlStealingEffect`: the first target player gains permanent control of the second target permanent. Pair with a player target followed by `TargetFilters.permanentYouControl()`. Used by Donate.
(return existing record components — the impl is purely additive) so the AI scores/targets it without
a code change. Branching on one of these interfaces is fine anywhere — it is the *concrete*-effect
`instanceof` they exist to replace.

- `GraveyardCardChoosingEffect` — effects that pick "up to N target cards" from a graveyard as the
  ability goes on the stack, a multi-card selection a single `TargetSpec` cannot describe:
  `graveyardChoiceMaxTargets()` + `graveyardChoiceFilter()`. Read by trigger collectors (e.g.
  `CombatBlockService` on `ON_ATTACKS_UNBLOCKED`) to route to `GraveyardTargetingService`.
  Implemented by `ExileCardsFromGraveyardEffect`. Its conditional-rider form can require one
  graveyard and apply a life-loss/life-gain rider when an actually exiled card matches a predicate.
- `AttachedPermanentSelfTargetingEffect` — a **self-targeting** effect on an activated ability of an
  Aura/Equipment that must resolve against the *attached* permanent, not the source.
  `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService` captures `Permanent.getAttachedTo()` onto the stack entry before
  costs are paid, so a cost that detaches the source (sacrifice — Carapace; return to hand — Krasis
  Incubation) still leaves the effect hitting the creature it had been attached to (CR 608.2h).
  Implemented by `RegenerateEffect` and `PutCountersOnEnchantedCreatureEffect`. Only honoured when
  `targetSpec().selfTargeting()` is true.
- `CostEffect` — additional costs (sacrifice, discard, exile, counter removal, tap creature). AI
  cost-valuation facets describe the resource paying gives up (default to neutral; override only the
  one that matches the record): `consumedPermanentFilter()` (`PermanentPredicate` selecting a
  payer-chosen battlefield permanent to sacrifice/return/put a counter on — creature/artifact/filtered;
  MANDATORY for every spell cost paid via `PlayCardRequest.sacrificePermanentId`, or the AI sends a
  null id and the engine rejects the cast — guarded by `CostEffectClassificationTest`),
  `consumesSourcePermanent()` (sacrifice this permanent), `sacrificesChosenCreature()` (plain
  "sacrifice a creature"), `lifePaid(currentLife)`, `sourceCountersRemoved()`,
  `consumedGraveyardCardCount()` + `consumedGraveyardCardType()`. Read by `GameSimulator`
  (payment-planning: find a sacrifice target / graveyard cards to exile) and
  `HardAiDecisionEngine.evaluateAbilityCosts` (score the cost). Overridden by `SacrificeCreatureCost`,
  `SacrificePermanentCost`, `SacrificeCreatureOrPayManaCost`,
  `ReturnCreatureToHandCost`, `PutCounterOnControlledCreatureCost`, `SacrificeSelfCost`, `PayLifeCost`,
  `RemoveCounterFromSourceCost`, `ExileNCardsFromGraveyardCost`; all other cost records inherit
  the neutral defaults (the AI never reasoned about them). Payment EXECUTION stays concrete in
  `AbilityActivationService`.
- `ManaProducingEffect` — mana abilities (CR 605.1a). AI-estimator facets (default to neutral;
  override only if the AI should model the mana): `estimatedManaColor()`, `estimatedManaAmount()`,
  `estimatedCountsAllColors()`, `estimatedWildcardMana()`, `modeledByManaEstimator()`
- `DamageDealingEffect` — deals a `DynamicAmount` to one target category; `damageAmount()`,
  `canDamageCreatures()`, `canDamagePlayers()`. Impl `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect`,
  `DealDamageToTargetCreatureEffect`, `DealDamageToTargetCreatureAndAllWithSameNameEffect`,
  `DealDamageToTargetCreatureOrPlaneswalkerEffect`,
  `DealDamageToPlayersEffect`
- `GlobalDamageMultiplyingEffect` — static permanent that scales ALL damage in the game regardless of
  source/target/controller; `damageMultiplierFactor()` (2 for a doubler, stacks multiplicatively). Read
  by `GameQueryService.getDamageMultiplier` and the Myr Battlesphere damage path. Impl `DoubleDamageEffect`
  (Furnace of Rath). NOT for the selective doublers (`DoubleControllerDamageEffect`,
  `DoubleDamageToEnchantedPlayerEffect`, `DoubleEquippedCreatureCombatDamageEffect`) — each selects a
  different permanent subset and stays concrete in its own dedicated query method
- `SourceDamageMultiplyingEffect` — static permanent capability for a multiplier restricted to matching
  damage sources controlled by the effect's controller; `damageMultiplier()` and `sourceFilter()`.
  Queried by `GameQueryService` for stack and combat damage, and by `DamageSupport` when an effect
  supplies an explicit creature source
- `ControllerRecipientDamageMultiplyingEffect` — static permanent capability for a multiplier when
  the effect controller's source deals damage to an opponent or an opponent's permanent;
  `damageMultiplier()`. Queried with both source controller and recipient controller at every damage
  entry point. Implemented by `DoubleControllerDamageToOpponentsAndTheirPermanentsEffect`
- `RemovalEffect` — single-target destroy/exile/bounce; `removalKind()` returns `RemovalKind`
  (`DESTROY`/`EXILE`/`BOUNCE`) or `null` when not single-target removal (e.g. mass bounce).
  Impl `DestroyTargetPermanentEffect`, `ExileTargetPermanentEffect`,
  `ExileTargetCreaturesAndControllersGainLifeEqualToPowerEffect`,
  `ExileTargetCreatureAndSameNameFromHandIfGodEffect` (Hour of Glory),
  `ReturnTargetPermanentToHandWithManaValueConditionalEffect`, `ReturnToHandEffect` (TARGET scope),
  `ReturnTargetPermanentAndAllWithSameNameToHandEffect`,
  `ReturnTargetAndAttachedMatchingToHandEffect`, `ExileTargetAndAttachedMatchingEffect`
- `BoardWipeEffect` — recognition-only fact for board sweeps; `sweepsBoard()` (true when the config is
  a mass sweep). Lets the AI's phase/pressure multipliers detect a wipe without naming the type; the
  per-type wipe SCORING stays concrete (each sweep is valued differently). Impl `MassDamageEffect`
  (always), `DestroyAllPermanentsEffect` (always), `DestroyOtherPermanentsWithEnteringNameEffect` (always),
  `DestroyOtherCreaturesSharingColorWithEnteringCreatureEffect` (always), `ReturnToHandEffect` (only `BounceScope.ALL_MATCHING`)
- `CardDrawingEffect` — `drawnCardAmount()` (`DynamicAmount`). Impl `DrawCardEffect`, `DrawCardUnlessPaysEffect`
- `LifeGainEffect` — `lifeGainAmount()` (`DynamicAmount`) + `gainsNoLife()` (default: amount is `Fixed(0)`; ask this instead of bare `instanceof` when the question is "does this card gain life"). Impl `GainLifeEffect`, `GainLifeEqualToTargetCreatureStatEffect`
- `ExileTargetCreaturesAndControllersGainLifeEqualToPowerEffect()` — multi-target exile that grants each
  target's controller life equal to that target's effective power captured before exile
- `ExileEachTargetSpellEffect()` — exiles every still-legal spell in its multi-target spell group;
  spells leave the stack without being countered, and copies cease to exist
- `TokenCreatingEffect` — `tokenAmount()`, `tokenType()`, `tokenPower()`, `tokenToughness()`.
  Impl `CreateTokenEffect`, `CreateTokenAndLinkToSourceEffect`, `CreateTokenThenEffect`
- `CreatureBoostEffect` — targeted P/T pump; `powerBoost()`, `toughnessBoost()` (`DynamicAmount`).
  Impl `BoostTargetCreatureEffect`
- `StaticCreatureBoostEffect` — continuous anthem/aura boost; `powerBoost()`, `toughnessBoost()`
  (int), `grantedKeywords()`, `scope()`, `filter()`. Impl `StaticBoostEffect`. The
  `StaticBoostEffect(int, int, GrantScope, CounterType scalingCounter)` ctor multiplies the flat
  boost by the count of that counter on the source ("All creatures get +1/+0 for each time counter
  on this" — Infinite Hourglass, `GrantScope.ALL_CREATURES`, `CounterType.TIME`)
- `KeywordGrantingEffect` — `keywords()`, `scope()`, `filter()` (predicate narrowing the scope, or
  `null`). Impl `GrantKeywordEffect`
- `ControlStealingEffect` — `controlDuration()`. Impl `GainControlOfTargetEffect`, `StealDyingOpponentPermanentUnlessPaysLifeEffect`
- `CulturalExchangeEffect()` — targets two players; the spell's controller chooses equal numbers of creatures from their battlefields in two resolution-time multi-permanent choices, then exchanges permanent control of all chosen creatures simultaneously. Completion uses `MultiPermanentChoiceContext.CulturalExchange` and `CulturalExchangeSupport`. Used by Cultural Exchange
- `CounterSpellingEffect` — marker for "counter target spell". Impl `CounterSpellEffect`,
  `CounterUnlessPaysEffect`, `CounterUnlessTakesDamageEffect`, `CounterEachTargetSpellEffect`
- `CounterUnlessEffect` — "counter unless controller pays a ransom" family; `ransomKind()`
  (`PAY_MANA`/`DISCARD_CARD`) + `ransomMagnitude()` (mana amount, or 1 for a discard). Lets the
  trigger-collection and may-ability choice-flow services recognise the family and route on kind
  without `instanceof`-ing each variant (the pay/discard orchestration itself stays in the engine).
  Impl `CounterUnlessPaysEffect`, `CounterUnlessDiscardsEffect`
- `ClashOutcomeConditionalEffect` — clash-trigger effect applied only on one outcome; `wrapped()` +
  `appliesOnWin()`. Read by `TriggerCollectionService.fireClashTriggers` to resolve the won/lost
  clause without branching on the two wrapper types. Impl `IfWonClashEffect`, `IfLostClashEffect`
- `RegenerationEffect` — marker for regeneration. Impl `RegenerateEffect`
- `ProtectionGrantingEffect` — static "protection from …" facts read by the engine query layer
  (`GameQueryService`), not the AI: `protectionFromColors()` + `protectionScope()`
  (`GrantScope` null = self / `EQUIPPED_CREATURE`), `protectionFromCardTypes()`,
  `protectionFromSubtypes()`, `protectionFromManaValueAtLeast()` (`OptionalInt`),
  `protectsFromEverything()` (Progenitus — protection from every source; checked in
  `hasProtectionFromSourceCardTypes` since every source has a card type). A record fills only
  its facet and inherits the empty/self defaults. Impl `ProtectionFromColorsEffect`,
  `ProtectionFromCardTypesEffect`, `ProtectionFromSubtypesEffect`, `ProtectionFromManaValueEffect`,
  `ProtectionFromEverythingEffect`.
  NOT for runtime-only protection: chosen-color (`ProtectionFromChosenColorEffect`, a
  `ChooseColorEffect`), "protection from non-[subtype] creatures" (tracked on the `Permanent`), or
  `ProtectionFromColorsOfPermanentsYouControlEffect()` — STATIC self-scoped Empty-Shrine Kannushi
  shape whose colour set is recomputed from the controller's battlefield inside
  `GameQueryService.hasProtectionFrom`
- `ProtectionFromImprintedCardTypesEffect()` — STATIC self-scoped protection from every card type
  of the card imprinted on the source permanent; the static handler derives a normal
  `ProtectionFromCardTypesEffect` from the still-exiled imprint (Mirror Golem)
- `TargetingRestrictionEffect.hexproofFromCardTypes(Set<CardType>)` — STATIC self restriction:
  opponents' spells with one of the given card types can't target the permanent; targeting-only,
  unlike protection (Elenda, Saint of Dusk)
  `GameQueryService.hasProtectionFrom`; `ProtectionFromColorsOfPermanentsYouControlEffect(ENCHANTED_CREATURE)`
  is the Aura-granted Pledge of Loyalty shape, evaluated from the Aura controller's battlefield
  by its static handler
- `BlockabilityRestrictionEffect` — attacker-side evasion facts read by the engine query layer
  (`GameQueryService`): `cantBeBlocked()`, `unblockableIfDefenderControls()` (`PermanentPredicate`),
  `unblockableIfControllerCastHistoricSpellThisTurn()`, `unblockableWhileAttackingAlone()`,
  `blockableOnlyBy()` + `blockableOnlyByDescription()`, `cantBeBlockedByCreaturesMatching()`,
  `cantBeBlockedByCreaturesMatchingOnlyIfDefenderControls()`. The engine
  evaluates the predicates and supplies engine-computed facts (attacking-alone, historic-spell). Impl
  `CantBeBlockedEffect`, `CanBeBlockedOnlyByFilterEffect`, `CantBeBlockedByCreaturesMatchingPredicateEffect`,
  `CantBeBlockedByCreaturesWithLessPowerEffect`,
  `CantBeBlockedIfDefenderControlsMatchingPermanentEffect`,
  `CantBeBlockedIfControllerCastHistoricSpellThisTurnEffect`, `CantBeBlockedIfAttackingAloneEffect`
- `BlockingRestrictionEffect` — blocker-side restriction facts (`GameQueryService`): `cantBlock()`,
  `canBlockOnlyAttackersMatching()` + `canBlockOnlyAttackersDescription()`, and the board-wide
  `globalCantBlockBlockerMatcher()` / `globalCantBlockAttackerMatcher()` / `globalCantBlockDescription()`.
  Impl `CantBlockEffect`, `CanBlockOnlyIfAttackerMatchesPredicateEffect`,
  `MatchingCreaturesCantBlockMatchingCreaturesEffect`
- `CanBlockCreaturesWithShadowEffect()` — STATIC; carrier can block creatures with shadow as though it
  had shadow, without gaining shadow itself (Wall of Diffusion)
- `BlockCostEffect` — per-block mana tax fact (`CombatBlockService.declareBlockers`):
  `blockCost(Permanent blocker, int attackerPower)` = generic mana to declare the block (0 = free). Impl
  `CantBlockHighPowerCreaturesUnlessPaysEffect`
- `AttackCostEffect` — per-attacker mana tax fact on the creature itself
  (`CombatAttackService` via `GameQueryService.getCreatureAttackTax`): `attackCost(Permanent)` =
  generic mana to declare the attack (0 = free). Impl `CantAttackUnlessPaysPerCounterEffect`
- `GlobalBlockLifeCostEffect` — board-wide per-blocker life tax
  (`CombatBlockService` / `GameQueryService.getGlobalBlockLifeTax`): `lifeCostBlockerMatcher()`,
  `lifeCostAttackerMatcher()`, `lifePerBlocker()`. Impl
  `MatchingCreaturesCantBlockMatchingCreaturesUnlessPaysLifeEffect`
- `AttackOrBlockRestrictionEffect` — combined "can't attack or block" facts (attack side in
  `CombatAttackService`, block side in `GameQueryService`): `globallyCantAttackOrBlock()`
  (`PermanentPredicate`), `cantAttackOrBlockUnless()` (`Condition`), `restrictionDescription()`. Impl
  `MatchingCreaturesCantAttackOrBlockEffect`, `CantAttackOrBlockUnlessEffect`
- `PermanentLockEffect` — per-permanent, duration-scoped lock facts read off a `FloatingContinuousEffect`
  whose `affectedPermanentId` is the locked permanent: `locksAttacking()` (`CombatAttackService`),
  `locksBlocking()` (`GameQueryService.findBlockDenial`), `locksActivatedAbilities()`
  (`AbilityActivationService.validateNotBlockedByStaticAbilityLock`; mana abilities included, triggered
  abilities unaffected). Queried via `GameQueryService.isLockedFrom{Attacking,Blocking,ActivatingAbilities}`.
  Impl `LockTargetPermanentEffect(locksAttacking, locksBlocking, locksActivatedAbilities, duration)` —
  the resolved effect that stamps the floating lock on its target creature (Detain / "can't attack this
  turn", Edifice of Authority). `UNTIL_END_OF_TURN` expires at cleanup, `UNTIL_YOUR_NEXT_TURN` at the
  ability controller's next turn start.
- `LockMatchingPermanentsEffect(predicate, locksAttacking, locksBlocking, locksActivatedAbilities, duration)` —
  non-targeted mass lock; stamps the floating `LockTargetPermanentEffect` on every matching permanent
  (Lavinia of the Tenth's ETB mass Detain).
- `AttackWithoutTappingPermissionEffect` — floating combat permission fact read by
  `GameQueryService` during attacker declaration; while its source is untapped, creatures controlled
  by the effect controller skip the normal tap caused by attacking. Impl `JohanCombatEffect`.
- `OtherCreaturesCantAttackThisTurnEffect()` — SPELL one-shot (Intimidation Bolt): every creature
  except the accompanying damage spell's target can't attack this turn. Appends the target
  (`entry.getTargetId()`) to the turn-scoped `GameData.otherCreaturesCantAttackExemptCreatureIds`, read
  in `CombatAttackService.canCreatureAttack` (so it also bars creatures entering later; a dead target
  ⇒ nobody attacks). Cleared at each turn transition. Pair with `DealDamageToTargetCreatureEffect(N)`
- `PeaceTalksEffect()` — SPELL one-shot (Peace Talks): this turn and next turn, creatures can't attack,
  and players and permanents can't be the targets of spells or activated abilities. Sets
  `GameData.peaceTalksTurnsRemaining` to at least 2; decremented at each turn advance; active while
  `> 0`. Attack side in `AttackLegalityService`; targeting in `TargetLegalityService` /
  `ValidTargetService` (triggered abilities unaffected). Untargeted
- `NoDefenderAttackPermissionEffect` — "attack as though no defender" facts
  (`GameQueryService.canAttackDespiteDefender`): `grantsCarrierAttackAsThoughNoDefender()` (self grant,
  incl. `ConditionalEffect`-wrapped and floating grants) and `noDefenderAttackMatcher()`
  (`PermanentPredicate`, board-wide). Impl `CanAttackAsThoughNoDefenderEffect`,
  `MatchingCreaturesCanAttackAsThoughNoDefenderEffect`
- `LandwalkIgnoredForBlockingEffect()` — board-wide STATIC: creatures with landwalk abilities
  (CR 702.14a) can be blocked as though they didn't have them. Suppresses landwalk `Keyword`s, the
  landwalk-flagged `CantBeBlockedIfDefenderControlsMatchingPermanentEffect(pred, true)` (snow
  landwalk), and `Permanent.unblockableIfDefenderControlsUntilEndOfTurn`; leaves non-landwalk
  defender-condition evasion (Scrapdiver Serpent) alone. Staff of the Ages
- `CombatDamageTriggerContextEffect` — how `CombatDamageService` should populate a fired
  `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER` trigger's stack entry: `combatDamageTriggerContext()` returns a
  `TriggerContext` — `DAMAGED_PLAYER_WITH_DAMAGE_AMOUNT` (xValue = damage dealt, targetId = damaged
  player, no source; Balefire Dragon), `SOURCE_SELF` (source = the dealing creature, no target;
  explore / self-counter), `DAMAGED_PLAYER` (targetId = damaged player, source = the dealing creature;
  "that player discards/mills/…") — or `null` for the plain no-context entry (also the default for
  effects that don't implement it). Recipient-dependent effects return `DAMAGED_PLAYER` only when their
  recipient is the target player and `null` otherwise. Impl `ReturnPermanentsOnCombatDamageToPlayerEffect`,
  `DealDamageToEachCreatureDamagedPlayerControlsEffect`,
  `LookAtTopXCardsPermanentsToBattlefieldRestToGraveyardEffect`, `PutCountersOnSourceEffect`,
  `RemoveAllCountersEffect`, `ExploreEffect`, `ExileTopCardsRepeatOnDuplicateEffect`,
  `TargetPlayerRandomDiscardOrControllerDrawsEffect`, `RevealRandomCardFromTargetPlayerHandEffect`,
  `RevealRandomCardFromTargetPlayerHandLoseLifeEqualToManaValueEffect` (reveal random + lose life = its mana value, Singe-Mind Ogre),
  `LookAtRandomCardInTargetPlayerHandEffect`,
  `SphinxAmbassadorEffect`, `TargetPlayerExilesFromHandEffect`, `ChooseCardsFromTargetHandEffect`,
  `SkipNextEffect`, `TargetPlayerCantGainLifeRestOfGameEffect`, `DiscardEffect` /
  `MillEffect` / `DealDamageToPlayersEffect` (only when recipient is the target player). NOT for the
  bespoke `MayEffect`-wrapped or destroy/sacrifice "damaged player controls" flows, which stay in
  `CombatDamageService`
- `GraveyardAbilityGrantingEffect` — static "each creature card in your graveyard has '[graveyard
  activated ability]'" facts read by `GameQueryService.computeGrantedGraveyardAbilitiesForOwnedCreatureCard`
  (and appended to the effective graveyard abilities by `AbilityActivationService` + the graveyard
  `CardView`): `grantedGraveyardAbilityFor(Card)` — per card, so a grant may derive its cost from the
  card and may return `null` to skip it. Impls `GrantGraveyardAbilityToCreatureCardsEffect` (Sedris,
  the Traitor King — a fixed unearth {2}{B}) and `GrantScavengeEqualToManaCostToCreatureCardsEffect`
  (Varolz, the Scar-Striped — scavenge for a cost equal to each card's mana cost)
- `MadnessGrantingEffect` — static "[filter] cards you own that aren't on the battlefield have madness
  (cost = mana cost)" facts read by `GameQueryService.findGrantedMadnessCost` at discard time:
  `madnessGrantFilter()`. Impl `GrantMadnessEqualToManaCostEffect` (Falkenrath Gorger — Vampire creatures)
- `SpellCastingAbilityGrantingEffect` — `filter()` and `grantedAbility()` facts for static abilities
  granted to matching spells. Read by the spell-casting and action-availability gates. Impl
  `GrantSpellCastingAbilityToSpellsEffect`
- `CombatOpponentReferencingEffect` — marker for an effect that, when fired from an **Aura/Equipment**
  combat trigger (`ON_BLOCK` / `ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED PER_BLOCKER`), acts on the enchanted/equipped
  creature's combat opponent. `CombatTriggerService` bakes that opponent in as the trigger's
  non-targeting `targetId` (so it can't fizzle) instead of prompting the controller for a target. Impl
  `DestroyCombatOpponentAtEndOfCombatEffect` (Venom — Basilisk-style Aura, non-Wall filter),
  `SkipNextUntapEffect(TapUntapScope.TARGET)` (Shield of the Righteous — equipped creature blocks →
  blocked attacker skips next untap)
- `ExileOneOfCombatOpponentsAndTrackWithSourceEffect` — non-targeting resolution-time choice for
  Godsend: chooses one creature blocking or blocked by the equipped creature, exiles it, and tracks
  the card with the Equipment. Uses `PermanentChoiceContext.ExileCombatOpponent` when multiple
  combat opponents remain.
- `FreeCyclingEffect` — static "you may pay {0} rather than pay cycling costs" fact read by
  `AbilityActivationService` at cycling activation (CR 118.9 alt cost; only the mana cost is replaced,
  the discard is still paid): `minCardsInHand()` cards the controller must hold, counting the card
  being cycled. Impl `FreeCyclingWhileHandSizeEffect` (New Perspectives, `EffectSlot.STATIC`, min 7)
| `TappedBlockPermissionEffect` | `CardEffect` | `tappedBlockMatcher()` returns a `PermanentPredicate` selecting creatures that may block while tapped, or `null`. Evaluated by `BlockLegalityService` relative to the permanent carrying the effect, so the permission stays correct after control changes. Impl `ControlledCreaturesCanBlockAsThoughUntappedEffect`. |

## Wrapper / modifier effects

- `FlipUntilLoseOrStopEffect(List<CardEffect> rewards)` — flip until a loss or the controller stops; if a loss occurs, no reward resolves, otherwise reward `i` resolves when at least `i + 1` flips were won. Each win queues a may-choice continuation. Fiery Gambit uses damage, opponent damage, and a draw-plus-untap sequence.

Core wrappers (all take `CardEffect wrapped` as first/only effect arg):
- `SequenceEffect.of(step1, step2, …)` (>=2 steps) — resolve the steps strictly in order, as one effect (engine splices them into the entry's live effect list; NO "if you do" contingency, a no-op step doesn't stop later steps). Use ONLY to gate several steps under a single-effect wrapper (`MayEffect(SequenceEffect.of(a, b))`, `ConditionalEffect(cond, SequenceEffect.of(a, b))`, `FlipCoinWinEffect(SequenceEffect.of(a, b))`) or to keep several steps one atomic triggered ability on a trigger slot. Do NOT wrap a plain spell/ability effect list — use flat `addEffect(...)` calls instead (Act of Treason / Drain Life pattern). Single target is shared by every targeting step; multi-target groups inside a sequence are unsupported
- `MayEffect(CardEffect, String prompt[, CardEffect elseEffect, MayChoicePlayer choicePlayer])` — "you may"; the choice defaults to the controller, while `choicePlayer=ACTIVE_PLAYER` makes the player whose upkeep caused an each-upkeep trigger choose (Oath of Lieges), and `TARGET_SPELL_CONTROLLER` makes the controller of the targeted spell choose (Vex)
- `OncePerTurnTriggerEffect(CardEffect)` — "This ability triggers only once each turn"; marks the source permanent in `GameData.oncePerTurnTriggersFiredThisTurn` on first fire (Ghoulish Procession, Morbid Opportunist); in `ON_ATTACK` it means "attacks for the first time each turn" (Aurelia, the Warleader) and uses `GameData.onceEachTurnAttackTriggersFiredThisTurn`
- `EnteringCreatureFromGraveyardConditionalEffect(CardEffect)` — gates an ally-creature-enters trigger on the entering creature having entered from a graveyard or having been cast from a graveyard
- `MayPayManaEffect(String manaCost, CardEffect, String prompt[, MayPayPayer payer])` — "you may pay {X}". The `(manaCost, int lifeCost, wrapped, prompt)` overload is "you may pay {M} **and** N life" (Purgatory) — both halves must be payable or neither is paid; not to be confused with the "{M} **or** N life" choice of `TargetPlayerMayPayManaOrLifeEffect`. `payer` (default `CONTROLLER`) redirects the prompt: `ENCHANTED_CONTROLLER` = the enchanted permanent's controller on the stack `targetId` (Paralyze, Dance of the Dead), `DEFENDING_PLAYER` = the attacked player / attacked planeswalker's controller read from an `ON_ATTACK` trigger's `attackedTargetId` (Mtenda Lion). The `(manaCost, wrapped, prompt, elseEffect)` overload is the punisher "If you don't, [elseEffect]" half (Preferred Selection; Knight of the Mists with `wrapped=null` + `DestroyTargetPermanentEffect(true)`). On `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`, targeting is deferred to trigger time so the entering permanent can be a legal target.
- `MayPayTapPermanentsEffect(TapMultiplePermanentsCost, CardEffect, String prompt[, CardEffect elseEffect])` — "you may tap N permanents". `wrapped` may be `null` when tapping is the whole payment; `elseEffect` is the "if you don't" half (Koskun Falls: upkeep tap-a-creature else `SacrificeSelfEffect`)
- `ConditionalEffect(new Metalcraft(), CardEffect)` — 3+ artifacts
- `ConditionalEffect(new NotCondition(new DevotionToColorAtLeast(ManaColor.WHITE, 5)), new SetCardTypesEffect(Set.of(CardType.ENCHANTMENT), GrantScope.SELF))` — a devotion-gated card-type override for Theros Gods; devotion counts that color's mana symbols among all permanents the controller controls, including the source
- `SetCardTypesEffect(Set.of(CardType.ENCHANTMENT), GrantScope.SELF)` — layer-4 replacement of a permanent's card types; use inside a condition for Theros God-style devotion type changes
- `SetNameEffect(String name, GrantScope scope)` — layer-3 replacement of a permanent's name; use for continuous effects such as Witness Protection's enchanted creature name change
- `ConditionalEffect(new Coven(), CardEffect)` — 3+ creatures with different powers
- `ConditionalEffect(new ControlsDistinctPermanentNamesCount(minCount, filter), CardEffect)` — at least `minCount` matching permanents with different names (Maze's End)
- `ConditionalEffect(new SpellManaSpentAtLeast(minMana), wrapped)` — mana spent to cast triggering spell >= N
- `ConditionalEffect(new SpellXAtLeast(minX), wrapped)` — resolving spell/ability's chosen X (entry `xValue`) >= N (Martial Coup "If X is 5 or more")
- `ConditionalEffect(new Morbid(), CardEffect)` — creature died this turn
- `ConditionalEffect(new Delirium(), CardEffect)` — 4+ card types among cards in your graveyard
- `ConditionalEffect(new Raid(), CardEffect)` — attacked this turn
- `ConditionalEffect(new AttackedWithCreaturesOfSubtypeThisTurn(N, CardSubtype.X), CardEffect)` — attacked with N or more creatures of a subtype this turn; the subtype count is cumulative across combats and recorded when attackers are declared
- `ConditionalEffect(new ControllerCastAnotherSpellThisTurn(filter), wrapped)` — another spell matching filter cast this turn (excludes resolving spell)
- `ControllerCastSpellThisTurn(filter)` — you cast a spell matching filter this turn, INCLUDING the source card itself (Mogg Conscripts: `CantAttackUnlessEffect(new ControllerCastSpellThisTurn(new CardTypePredicate(CREATURE)), ...)`)
- `ConditionalEffect(new OpponentCastSpellThisTurn(filter), wrapped)` — an opponent cast a spell matching filter this turn
- `OpponentCastThreeOrMoreSpellsThisTurn()` — one opponent cast at least three spells this turn; use as an alternate-cast availability condition for Mindbreak Trap
- `GrantControllerAndPermanentsHexproofFromColorsEffect(Set<CardColor>)` — controller and permanents they control can't be targeted by spells or abilities of the given colors controlled by opponents until end of turn; targeting-only, unlike protection
- `ConditionalEffect(new NotCondition(inner), wrapped)` — logical negation of any condition ("unless …"), e.g. Hotheaded Giant enters with -1/-1 counters `new NotCondition(new ControllerCastAnotherSpellThisTurn(new CardColorPredicate(RED)))`
- `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect(CardPredicate, CardEffect)` — triggering card matches predicate; in `ON_ANY_CREATURE_BECOMES_BLOCKED`, it is evaluated globally and queues the wrapped effect once per matching blocked attacker (Spined Sliver)
- `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect(PermanentPredicate, CardEffect)` — triggering permanent matches predicate (ON_ALLY_PERMANENT_SACRIFICED, ON_ALLY_CREATURE_ATTACKS, and `ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED` + `TriggerMode.PER_BLOCKER` where the subject is each blocker — Catacomb Dragon)
- `ConditionalEffect(new ControlsAnotherPermanent(filter), wrapped)` — controls another matching permanent
- `ConditionalEffect(new ControllerLifeAtLeast(threshold), wrapped)` — life >= N
- `ConditionalEffect(new AnOpponentLifeAtMost(threshold), wrapped)` — some opponent's life <= N
- `ConditionalEffect(new ChosenColorStrictlyMostCommonAmongOpponentNontokens(), wrapped)` — Call to Arms: chosen color strictly most common among opponent nontoken permanents
- `ConditionalEffect(new ColorMostCommonAmongAllPermanents(color), wrapped)` — color is most common among all permanents or tied; multicolored permanents count once for each color, and colorless permanents do not count
- `ConditionalEffect(new ControllerHasMoreLifeThanAnOpponent(), wrapped)` — you have strictly more life than at least one opponent (Feudkiller's Verdict)
- `ConditionalEffect(new AnOpponentHasMoreLifeThanController(), wrapped)` — at least one opponent has strictly more life than you, i.e. "if you have less life than an opponent" (Timely Reinforcements). Pair with `OpponentControlsMoreCreatures(1)` for "if you control fewer creatures than an opponent"
- `ConditionalEffect(new TargetPermanentMatches(PermanentPredicate), wrapped)` — the targeted permanent matches the predicate at resolution (e.g. "destroy target permanent if it's red": wrap `DestroyTargetPermanentEffect` with `PermanentColorInPredicate(Set.of(RED))`). Targeting stays unrestricted (any permanent); the color check is a resolution-time intervening-if. Hydroblast mode 1
- `ConditionalEffect(new TargetPlayerLifeTotalEquals(n), wrapped)` — the targeted player's life total is exactly `n` at resolution; the player remains a legal target when the condition is false (Hidetsugu's Second Rite)
- `ConditionalEffect(new TargetPermanentManaValueEqualsControllerUnspentMana(), wrapped)` — the targeted permanent's mana value equals the ability controller's total unspent mana at resolution (Glissa Sunseeker; combine with `TargetPermanentMatches(PermanentIsArtifactPredicate)` and an artifact target filter)
- `ConditionalEffect(new TargetToughnessAtMostControllerGraveyardCount(), DestroyTargetPermanentEffect())` — destroy the target if its toughness is at most the resolving spell's controller's graveyard size (Ghastly Demise); target selection remains restricted only by the card's nonblack-creature filter
- `ConditionalEffect(new TargetSpellMatches(StackEntryPredicate), wrapped)` — the targeted spell on the stack matches the predicate at resolution (e.g. "counter target spell if it's red": wrap `CounterSpellEffect` with `StackEntryColorInPredicate(Set.of(RED))`). Targeting stays unrestricted (any spell); the color check is a resolution-time intervening-if. Hydroblast mode 0
- `ConditionalEffect(new TargetSpellCanBeCountered(), wrapped)` — the targeted spell is still on the stack **and** the source spell can actually counter it (not uncounterable, no Autumn's Veil-style colour protection). Gates the rider half of "counter target spell … [do something with] the spell countered this way" so the rider is skipped when the counter would do nothing. Psychic Rebuttal

Metalcraft / Morbid / Raid / ControlsAnotherPermanent / OpponentControlsMoreLands are ETB trigger gates
(`Condition.isEtbTriggerGate()`): a targeted ETB wrapped in one never asks for its target at cast
time — the target is chosen as the trigger goes on the stack (see TRIGGER_SLOT_TARGETING.md,
"ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD targeted triggers"). Override `isEtbTriggerGate()` when adding a new
intervening-if condition used to gate a targeted ETB.
- `ConditionalEffect(new ControllerDealtDamageThisTurn(n), CardEffect)` — the controller was dealt at least `n` damage this turn (any source, combat or not; `GameData.damageDealtToPlayersThisTurn`). Controller-relative counterpart of `OpponentDealtDamageThisTurn`; a real intervening-if on `END_STEP_TRIGGERED` (Boarded Window with 4)
- `ConditionalEffect(new AnyPlayerLostLifeThisTurn(n), CardEffect)` — any player lost at least `n` life this turn from any source, including damage; use for wording that says “a player,” such as Knight of the Ebon Legion
- `ConditionalEffect(new GainedLifeThisTurn(n), CardEffect)` — the controller gained at least `n` life this turn (`GameData.lifeGainedThisTurn`); `new GainedLifeThisTurn()` = threshold 1 ("if you gained life this turn", the SOS Infusion condition). On `END_STEP_TRIGGERED` it is a real intervening-if: checked against the permanent's controller at trigger time and re-checked at resolution (Crested Sunmare with 1, Angelic Accord with 4)
- `ConditionalEffect(new PutCounterOnCreatureThisTurn(), CardEffect)` — the controller put at least one counter on a creature this turn; use for end-step intervening-if text such as Lasting Tarfire
- `ConditionalEffect(new ControllerTurn(), CardEffect)` — during your turn
- `ConditionalEffect(new NotControllerTurn(), CardEffect)` — during turns other than yours; also an `END_STEP_TRIGGERED` intervening-if gate ("if it's an opponent's turn" — Discordant Spirit)
- `ExtraTurn()` — the current turn was taken from the extra-turn queue; combine with `NotCondition` for attack restrictions such as Medomai the Ageless
- `ControllerOwnTurnCountAtMost(n)` — controller is active player and has taken ≤ `n` turns this game (`GameData.turnsTakenByPlayer`); `setCastCondition(new NotCondition(...))` gives Serra Avenger's first-three-turns cast ban
- `ConditionalEffect(new ControlsPermanent(filter), wrapped)` — controls matching
- `EnchantedPermanentConditionalEffect(PermanentPredicate, CardEffect ifMatch, CardEffect ifNotMatch)` — aura active branch based on enchanted permanent predicate
- `ConditionalEffect(new OpponentControlsPermanent(filter), wrapped)` — opponent controls matching
- `ConditionalEffect(new OpponentControlsPermanentCount(N, filter), wrapped)` — an opponent controls at least N matching permanents; Walking Dream uses this with `DoesntUntapEffect.self()` and a creature filter
- `ConditionalEffect(new OpponentControlsMoreLands(), wrapped)` — an opponent controls strictly more lands than you (Gift of Estates)
- `ConditionalEffect(new HasAttacker(predicate), wrapped)` — one or more matching attackers
- `CantAttackUnlessEffect(Condition, "unless clause")` — STATIC attack restriction; condition = `ControlsPermanentCount(1, filter)` / `DefendingPlayerControlsPermanent(filter)` / `ControlsMoreCreaturesThanOpponent()` / `AnyPlayerControlsPermanentCount(N, filter)` / `DefendingPlayerPoisoned()` / `OpponentDealtDamageThisTurn(minAmount)` / `NotCondition(new ExtraTurn())`
- `CantBlockUnlessEffect(Condition, "unless clause")` — STATIC block-only restriction (Marauding Boneslasher: `new ControlsOtherPermanentCount(1, new PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(ZOMBIE))`, "you control another Zombie"). `ControlsMoreCreaturesThanOpponent()` compares the blocker controller with the attacking player. Block counterpart of `CantAttackUnlessEffect`; leaves attacking unrestricted, unlike `CantAttackOrBlockUnlessEffect`. Evaluated in `GameQueryService.canBlock`
  - `Equipped()` — "can't attack or block unless it's equipped" (Training Drone: `CantAttackOrBlockUnlessEffect(new Equipped(), "it's equipped")`)
  - `ControlsOtherPermanentCount(N, filter)` — controller controls at least N matching permanents **excluding the source** (count analogue of `ControlsAnotherPermanent`; e.g. Amonkhet gods' `CantAttackOrBlockUnlessEffect(new ControlsOtherPermanentCount(3, new PermanentIsCreaturePredicate()), "you control at least three other creatures")`)
  - `ControlsOtherThanTriggeringPermanentCount(N, filter)` — on an enter trigger, controller controls at least N matching permanents excluding both the source and the permanent that caused the trigger (Valakut's five other Mountains).
- `CantAttackOrBlockUnlessGreaterPowerAlsoDoesEffect()` — STATIC combat-set restriction (Okk): can't attack unless another declared attacker has strictly greater power; can't block unless another declared blocker has strictly greater power. Validated against the current combat's declared set in `CombatAttackService`/`CombatBlockService`, not via `Condition`
- `CantAttackOrBlockUnlessCountAlsoDoesEffect(int otherCount)` — STATIC combat-set restriction (Orcish Conscripts, `otherCount = 2`): can't attack unless at least N other creatures are declared as attackers; can't block unless at least N other creatures are declared as blockers. Validated against the current combat's declared set in `CombatAttackService`/`CombatBlockService`, not via `Condition`
- `MaximumCombatCreaturesEffect(int maxAttackers, int maxBlockers)` — STATIC global combat-set restriction: caps the number of attackers and distinct blockers declared in each combat. Read through the `CombatCreatureLimitEffect` capability by `CombatAttackService`/`CombatBlockService` (Dueling Grounds uses `(1, 1)`)
- `CantAttackUnlessMatchingCreatureAlsoAttacksEffect(PermanentPredicate, String)` — STATIC attack-only combat-set restriction: can't attack unless another declared attacker matches the predicate (Scarred Puma). Validated against the current combat's declared attackers in `CombatAttackService`, not via `Condition`
- `CreaturesCantAttackUnlessPredicateEffect(PermanentPredicate exemption)` — STATIC global: no creature can attack unless it matches exemption (Stormtide Leviathan)
- `CreaturesCantAttackControllerUnlessPredicateEffect(PermanentPredicate exemption[, boolean protectsPlaneswalkers])` — STATIC defender-scoped: creatures not matching exemption can't attack THIS controller only ("creatures without flying can't attack you", Form of the Dragon → exemption `PermanentHasKeywordPredicate(Keyword.FLYING)`). Pass `protectsPlaneswalkers=true` to also bar attacks on the controller's planeswalkers ("can't attack you or planeswalkers you control", Sandwurm Convergence → exemption `PermanentNotPredicate(PermanentHasKeywordPredicate(Keyword.FLYING))`); the single-arg overload defaults to player-only
- `RequirePaymentToAttackEffect(int amountPerAttacker)` — STATIC uniform attack tax read from the DEFENDER's battlefield; controller pays N per attacker (Windborn Muse / Ghostly Prison). Optional trailing `Condition activeCondition` turns the tax on and off with a source-permanent state (Archangel of Tithes — `SourceUntapped`)
- `RequirePaymentToBlockEffect(int amountPerBlocker[, Condition activeCondition])` — board-wide STATIC block tax: creatures can't block unless their controller pays {N} per declared blocker, charged once per blocker in `CombatBlockService` (Archangel of Tithes — `SourceIsAttacking`)
