- `RequirePhyrexianPaymentToAttackEffect(ManaColor color)` — STATIC defender-side `{color/P}` tax per attacker (Norn's Annex). `CombatAttackService` validates the complete declaration before paying anything, preserves matching colored mana when combined with generic attack taxes, and pays 2 life for each symbol not paid with mana. A player may pay their exact remaining life, then loses at the immediate post-declaration SBA check; they may not pay more life than they have or pay life while their life total can't change. AI tax planning uses colored mana first, never spends its final life point, and declines nonlethal life payments whose life cost exceeds the attackers' total power.
- `EnchantedCreatureCombatTaxEffect(int amount, CombatTaxKind kind)` — Aura STATIC: the enchanted creature can't take the combat action named by `kind` unless {amount} generic is paid to declare it; the declaration stays legal, only the cost gates it. `ATTACK` (Brainwash, Oppressive Rays) and `BLOCK_WITH` (Oppressive Rays) are paid by the creature's controller per declared attack/block; `BE_BLOCKED_BY` (Awesome Presence) is paid by the **defending** player per creature blocking it. Contrast the uniform, defender-side `RequirePaymentToAttackEffect`
- `CantAttackUnlessPaysPerCounterEffect(CounterType, int amountPerCounter)` — STATIC: this creature can't attack unless its controller pays `{amountPerCounter}` generic for each counter of that type on it (Phyrexian Marauder — `PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`, 1). Via `AttackCostEffect` / `GameQueryService.getCreatureAttackTax`; zero counters ⇒ free
- `CantAttackUnlessSacrificeEffect(count, PermanentPredicate, description)` — STATIC additional attack cost: this creature can't attack unless its controller sacrifices `count` matching permanents (paid as attackers declared). Marker read by `AttackSacrificeCostService` (no dispatch handler). **Pair with** a `CantAttackUnlessEffect(new ControlsPermanentCount(count, filter), …)` legality gate so the declaration is only allowed when the cost is payable (Leviathan — `(2, Island)`, "can't attack unless you sacrifice two Islands")
- `CreaturesCantAttackUnlessSacrificeEffect(attackerPredicate, countPerAttacker, sacrificeFilter, description)` — global STATIC attack cost applied from any permanent to every matching creature: they can't attack unless their controller sacrifices `countPerAttacker` matching permanents for EACH matching attacker. Legality gate is derived from the same fields (no paired `CantAttackUnlessEffect` needed); read by `AttackLegalityService` + `AttackSacrificeCostService` (Flooded Woodlands — green creatures, one land each)
- `CantBlockHighPowerCreaturesUnlessPaysEffect(int minAttackerPower, int amount)` — STATIC block-side tax (Hipparion, `(3, 1)`): this creature can't block a creature with power ≥ `minAttackerPower` unless its controller pays {amount} generic. Charged per qualifying block in `CombatBlockService.declareBlockers` via the `BlockCostEffect` capability interface; block stays legal, only the cost gates it
- `CantBlockCreaturesWithPowerAtLeastEffect(int minAttackerPower)` — STATIC hard restriction (Ironclaw Orcs, `(2)`): this creature can't block an attacker with effective power ≥ `minAttackerPower`. No cost, not self-referential; compared in `GameQueryService.findBlockDenial`. Facet `cantBlockCreaturesWithPowerAtLeast()` on `BlockingRestrictionEffect`
- `ControlledCreaturesMatchingCantBeBlockedEffect(PermanentPredicate filter)` — STATIC controller-scoped: creatures the source's controller controls that match the filter can't be blocked (source included if it matches). "Creatures you control with power or toughness 1 or less can't be blocked", Tetsuko Umezawa, Fugitive → `PermanentAnyOfPredicate(PowerAtMost(1), ToughnessAtMost(1))`. CR 613.11 rules-modifier, not a layer-6 grant — prefer it over `GrantEffectEffect(CantBeBlockedEffect, ALL_OWN_CREATURES, filter)` when the filter reads P/T
- `ControlledCreaturesCantAttackUnlessPredicateEffect(PermanentPredicate exemption)` — STATIC controller-scoped: creatures the source's controller controls that don't match exemption can't attack (own team only; source exempt if it matches). "Non-Eye creatures you control can't attack", Evil Eye of Orms-by-Gore → exemption `PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(EYE)`
- `CreaturesWithPowerGreaterThanAmountCantAttackEffect(DynamicAmount amount)` — STATIC global: any creature (either player's) whose effective power is strictly greater than `amount` can't attack. `amount` is evaluated from the source's controller (`AmountContext.forStaticEffect`). Ensnaring Bridge → `new CardsInHand(CountScope.CONTROLLER)` ("power greater than the number of cards in your hand can't attack")
- `ConditionalEffect(new GraveyardCardThreshold(threshold, filter), wrapped)` — graveyard threshold
- `ConditionalEffect(new CardsInLibraryAtLeast(threshold), wrapped)` — controller has N+ cards in library (Battle of Wits: upkeep + WinGameEffect)
- `ConditionalEffect(new AnyGraveyardAtLeast(threshold), wrapped)` — some player's graveyard has N or more cards in it (Visions of Beyond: SPELL `ConditionalEffect(AnyGraveyardAtLeast(20), DrawCardEffect(3))` + `ConditionalEffect(NotCondition(same), DrawCardEffect(1))` for "draw three instead")
- `ConditionalEffect(new OpponentGraveyardAtLeast(threshold), wrapped)` — an OPPONENT of the controller has N or more cards in their graveyard; the controller's own graveyard never counts (Jace's Phantasm: STATIC `ConditionalEffect(OpponentGraveyardAtLeast(10), StaticBoostEffect(4, 4, SELF))`)
- `ConditionalEffect(new OpponentOwnsCardInExile(), wrapped)` — an opponent of the controller owns a card in exile; the controller's own exiled cards never count (Warden of the Beyond: STATIC `ConditionalEffect(OpponentOwnsCardInExile(), StaticBoostEffect(2, 2, SELF))`)
- `ConditionalEffect(new AnyLibraryAtMost(threshold), wrapped)` — some player's library has N or fewer cards (Shelldock Isle: `{U}, {T}` + PlayImprintedCardWithoutPayingManaCostEffect, threshold 20)
- `ConditionalEffect(new CardsInHandAtLeast(threshold), wrapped)` — controller has N+ cards in hand (Imaginary Pet: upkeep + ReturnToHandEffect.self())
- `ConditionalEffect(new ActivePlayerHandAtLeast(threshold), wrapped)` — the active player (whose upkeep it is) has N+ cards in hand (Misers' Cage: OPPONENT_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED + `DealDamageToPlayersEffect(2, TARGET_PLAYER)`)
- `ConditionalEffect(new CardsInHandAtMost(threshold), wrapped)` — controller has N or fewer cards in hand (Thresher Lizard: STATIC StaticBoostEffect(1, 2, SELF), threshold 1 = "one or fewer cards in hand")
- `ConditionalEffect(new AnOpponentHasMoreCardsInHandThanController(), wrapped)` — at least one opponent has more cards in hand than the controller (Pulse of the Grid)
- `ConditionalEffect(new SourceCounterThreshold(threshold, counterType), wrapped)` — source counter threshold (e.g. 5+ growth counters)
- `ConditionalEffect(new DevouredCreature(), wrapped)` — source devoured at least one creature (CR 702.82). STATIC self-grant use: "has flying and trample if it devoured a creature" (Hellkite Hatchling: two `ConditionalEffect(new DevouredCreature(), new GrantKeywordEffect(Keyword.X, GrantScope.SELF))`, paired with `DevourEffect` on ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD)
- `ConditionalEffect(new SourceIsTapped(), wrapped)` — source permanent is tapped (Mana Vault draw-step self-damage)
- `ConditionalEffect(new NotCondition(new SourceIsToken()), wrapped)` — source permanent isn't a token (Progenitor Mimic upkeep trigger)
- `ConditionalEffect(new SourceIsAttacking(), wrapped)` — source permanent is currently attacking (Thorned Moloch: STATIC GrantKeywordEffect(FIRST_STRIKE, SELF))
- `ConditionalEffect(new NotCondition(new SourceHasDealtDamage()), wrapped)` — source permanent has not dealt damage since becoming this game object; persistent source-damage tracking makes this suitable for static conditional abilities (Palladia-Mors, the Ruiner's hexproof)
- `SourceBlockedOrWasBlockedByColorThisTurn(CardColor)` — source blocked or was blocked by a creature of that color this turn. Turn-scoped, recorded at declare-blockers into `GameData.combatBlockOpponentColorsThisTurn` (color judged at the moment of the block), so it stays true after combat ends. Sea Troll uses it as an `withActivationCondition` gate on its `{U}: Regenerate` ability
- `SourceWasBlockedThisTurn()` — source attacked and became blocked this turn (attacker direction only; blocking does not count). Turn-scoped `GameData.creaturesBlockedThisTurn`, keyed by permanent ID so it still answers after the source has died — use it as the intervening-"if" of a dies trigger (Fyndhorn Druid: `ON_DEATH ConditionalEffect(new SourceWasBlockedThisTurn(), new GainLifeEffect(4))`)
- `SourceAttackedOrBlockedThisTurn()` — source was declared as an attacker or blocker this turn; use it as an end-step intervening-if before a self-shuffle effect (Inferno Hellion)
- `ConditionalEffect(new ControlledCreaturesTotalPowerAtLeast(threshold), wrapped)` — total power of creatures you control >= N (Mosswort Bridge)
- `ConditionalEffect(new ControlsCreatureWithGreatestPower(), wrapped)` — you control a creature whose effective power ties or beats every creature on the battlefield; never met with no creatures (Triumph of Cruelty)
- `ConditionalEffect(new ControlsEachCreatureWithGreatestPower(), wrapped)` — stricter sibling: **every** creature tied for the greatest effective power on the battlefield is yours; vacuously true with no creatures (Might Makes Right). Listed in `StepTriggerService.queueBeginningOfCombatTriggers` as a trigger-time intervening-if gate
- `EnteringCreatureMinPowerConditionalEffect(int, CardEffect)` — entering power >= N
- `EnteringCreatureMaxPowerConditionalEffect(int, CardEffect)` — entering power <= N
- `EnteringCreatureExactStatsConditionalEffect(int power, int toughness, CardEffect)` — entering creature's printed P/T both equal the given values ("if that creature is 1/1" — Sigil Captain `(1, 1, …)`)
- `PutCountersOnEnteringCreatureEffect(int count[, boolean optional])` — ON_ALLY_CREATURE_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD: put `count` +1/+1 counters on the entering creature. `optional` defaults to `true` ("you may": collector bakes the entering permanent id into a `MayEffect(PutCounterOnTargetPermanentEffect(PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE, count))`); `optional=false` bakes the mandatory counter placement directly onto the stack. Wrap in `EnteringCreatureMinPowerConditionalEffect` for the "with power N or greater" gate (Mighty Emergence: `(5, new PutCountersOnEnteringCreatureEffect(2))`) or `EnteringCreatureExactStatsConditionalEffect` for "is X/Y" (Sigil Captain: `(1, 1, new PutCountersOnEnteringCreatureEffect(2, false))`)
- `EvolveTriggerEffect()` — keyword-driven ally-creature entry trigger: compares the entering creature's power and toughness to the source at trigger and resolution, using the entering creature's saved stats if it has left before resolution, then puts one +1/+1 counter on the source, then fires the source's `ON_SELF_EVOLVES` triggers if the counter was actually placed
- `BoostEnteringCreatureEffect(int powerBoost, int toughnessBoost[, Set<Keyword> keywords])` — ON_ALLY_CREATURE_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD: the creature that just entered gets +X/+Y and gains the keywords until end of turn (Ogre Battledriver `(2, 0, Set.of(Keyword.HASTE))`). Non-targeting marker; the collector bakes a mandatory `BoostTargetCreatureEffect` (+ `GrantKeywordEffect(TARGET)`) onto the stack with the entering permanent as `targetId`
- `TransformEnteringCreatureEffect([boolean optional])` — ON_ALLY_CREATURE_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD: transform the entering creature ("it"). `optional` defaults to `true` ("you may": collector bakes the entering permanent id into a `MayEffect(TransformTargetPermanentEffect)`); `optional=false` bakes the mandatory transform directly. Gate subtype with `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect(CardSubtypePredicate(WEREWOLF), …)` (Vildin-Pack Alpha)
- `TransformTargetPermanentEffect()` — transform the permanent referenced by stack `targetId` (other face). Non-targeting "it" resolution companion to `TransformEnteringCreatureEffect`; no-op if no back face / transform prevented

Replacement wrappers (pick between base/upgraded at resolution):
- `ConditionalReplacementEffect(new Metalcraft(), baseEffect, upgradedEffect)(CardEffect base, CardEffect metalcraft)`
- `ConditionalReplacementEffect(new Morbid(), baseEffect, upgradedEffect)(CardEffect base, CardEffect morbid)`
- `ConditionalReplacementEffect(new Delirium(), baseEffect, upgradedEffect)(CardEffect base, CardEffect delirium)`
- `ConditionalReplacementEffect(new Raid(), baseEffect, upgradedEffect)(CardEffect base, CardEffect raid)`
- `ConditionalReplacementEffect(new Kicked(), baseEffect, upgradedEffect)(CardEffect base, CardEffect kicked)`
- `ConditionalReplacementEffect(new ControlsPermanent(filter), baseEffect, upgradedEffect)(PermanentPredicate, CardEffect base, CardEffect upgraded)`
- `ConditionalReplacementEffect(new TargetPermanentMatches(filter), baseEffect, upgradedEffect)(PermanentPredicate, CardEffect base, CardEffect upgraded)` — target permanent predicate

Other wrappers:
- `ChooseOneForTargetCreatureEffect(List<ChooseOneOption>)` — modal *activated ability* whose every mode applies to the same target creature (Nature's Blessing). Unlike a raw `ChooseOneEffect` (target spec `NONE`, so the ability is never offered a target), this declares a benign `CREATURE` spec: the target is chosen at activation, the mode as the ability resolves (`handleListChoice`), and the spliced mode effects read the entry's target
- `ChooseOneEffect(List<ChooseOneOption>)` — modal spell; `(options, n)` = choose exactly n; `ChooseOneEffect.oneOrMore(options)` = choose 1..N
- `ChooseModeNotYetChosenEffect(List<ChooseOneOption>)` — "choose one that hasn't been chosen" modal UPKEEP trigger (Demonic Pact). Mode AND its targets are picked as the trigger goes on the stack; chosen labels are consumed on `Permanent.chosenModeLabels`, so the last mode is forced. Per-mode `targetFilter` overrides the card's
- `EscalateDiscardCost()` — escalate: discard one card per mode beyond the first
- `EscalateManaCost("{3}")` — escalate: pay mana cost once per mode beyond the first
- `EscalateSacrificeCost(int, PermanentPredicate)` — sacrifice the matching permanents once per mode beyond the first; selections use `additionalCostSacrificePermanentIds`
- `FlipCoinWinEffect(CardEffect win)` or `(CardEffect win, CardEffect lost)` — flip a coin; run `win` if won, `lost` if lost (`lost` optional, defaults to nothing). Bottle of Suleiman: `(CreateTokenEffect(5/5 Djinn), DealDamageToPlayersEffect(5, CONTROLLER))`. Delegates `targetSpec()` to whichever branch targets (`win` first, else `lost`), so a targeted branch works on a spell/activated ability — Goblin Lyre: `(DealDamageToTargetPlayerOrPlaneswalkerEffect(PermanentCount(IsCreature, CONTROLLER)), DealDamageToPlayersEffect(PermanentCount(IsCreature, TARGET_PLAYER), CONTROLLER))`
- `ChooseOneEffect(List<ChooseOneOption>)` — modal spell; `(options, n)` = choose exactly n; `ChooseOneEffect.oneOrMore(options)` = choose 1..N. `option.withManaCost("{2}{B}")` gives one mode its own **total** cost, replacing the card's printed cost when that mode is chosen — this is how a **split card with fuse** is modelled (Far // Away: a mode per half plus a fuse mode costing both halves combined; CR 709.3, CR 702.102c). Playability accepts the card when any mode's cost is affordable and the cast re-checks the mode actually chosen
- `EscalateDiscardCost()` — escalate: discard one card per mode beyond the first
- `EscalateManaCost("{3}")` — escalate: pay mana cost once per mode beyond the first
- `FlipCoinWinEffect(CardEffect win)` or `(CardEffect win, CardEffect lost)` — flip a coin; run `win` if won, `lost` if lost (`lost` optional, defaults to nothing). Bottle of Suleiman: `(CreateTokenEffect(5/5 Djinn), DealDamageToPlayersEffect(5, CONTROLLER))`. Delegates `targetSpec()` to whichever branch targets (`win` first, else `lost`), so a targeted branch works on a spell/activated ability — Goblin Lyre: `(DealDamageToTargetOpponentOrPlaneswalkerEffect(PermanentCount(IsCreature, CONTROLLER)), DealDamageToPlayersEffect(PermanentCount(IsCreature, TARGET_PLAYER), CONTROLLER))`
- `FlipCoinsPerHeadsEffect(int coins, CardEffect perHeads)` — flip N coins, resolve `perHeads` once per heads (multi-step payload = `SequenceEffect` of synchronous steps; `targetSpec()` delegates to `perHeads`). Ral Zarek −7: `(5, new ControllerExtraTurnEffect(1))`
- `ManaClashEffect()` — repeat: you + target opponent flip coins, 1 dmg per tails, until both heads
- `GameOfChaosEffect()` — target opponent; flip a coin, on a win you gain / opp loses the stake life (opp gains / you lose on a loss), the flip's winner may flip again, stakes double each flip (Game of Chaos). Self-driven loop via `GameOfChaosFlipAgainEffect` + the may-ability yes/no prompt
- `NthSpellCastTriggerEffect(int, List<CardEffect>)` — Nth spell trigger
- `GrantBloodthirstToCastSpellEffect(int amount)` — "it gains bloodthirst N" (Bloodlord of Vaasgoth). Place inside a `SpellCastTriggerEffect` in `ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL`; resolves by stamping `StackEntry.grantedBloodthirst` on the triggering spell (grants accumulate, CR 702.54c), which `StackResolutionService` carries to `Permanent.grantedBloodthirst` and `BattlefieldEntryService.applyGrantedBloodthirst` turns into +1/+1 counters as an as-enters replacement gated on `OpponentDealtDamageThisTurn(1)` (CR 702.54a). Printed bloodthirst N is just `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD ConditionalEffect(new OpponentDealtDamageThisTurn(1), new EnterWithCountersEffect(PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE, new Fixed(N)))` — no new effect needed
- `ConditionalEffect(new NoSpellsCastLastTurn(), CardEffect)` — werewolf front
- `ConditionalEffect(new TwoOrMoreSpellsCastLastTurn(), CardEffect)` — werewolf back
- `ConditionalEffect(new OpponentLostLifeLastTurn(), CardEffect)` — an opponent lost life during the immediately preceding turn (Feast on the Fallen)
- `ConditionalEffect(new ControllerDidntLoseLifeThisTurn(), CardEffect)` — the source's controller lost no life this turn; damage counts as life loss (Luminarch Ascension)
- `ConditionalEffect(new CastFromZone(sourceZone), wrapped)` — resolves wrapped effect only if cast from that zone (`Zone.HAND` / `Zone.GRAVEYARD`)
- `ConditionalEffect(new CastNotFromHand(), CardEffect)` — resolves wrapped effect only if cast from anywhere other than hand (e.g. flashback)
- `ConditionalEffect(new WasCast(), CardEffect)` — resolves wrapped effect only when the permanent entered by resolving a spell that was cast, including casts from zones other than hand
- `ConditionalEffect(new Kicked(), CardEffect)` — kicked adds effect

See EFFECTS_INDEX.md for 20+ additional conditional wrappers (poison, blocker count, etc.)

## Damage

> **Power-based damage convention.** Any effect that deals damage equal to a creature's power
> (fight, bite, Pack Hunt, Berserker, Arc-Lightning-style source damage, planeswalker
> power-to-loyalty, `TargetDealsPowerDamageToTargetEffect`,
> `FightTargetsEffect`, `MassFightTargetCreatureEffect`,
> `SourceFightsTargetCreatureEffect`, the `SourcePower` dynamic amount,
> `PackHuntEffect`, `ControlledCreaturesDealPowerDamageToTargetEffect`) must read the amount via
> `gameQueryService.getPowerBasedDamage(gameData, source)` — **never** via
> `getEffectivePower` with a manual `> 0` guard. The helper clamps negative power to 0 per
> CR 510.1a so the damage primitives never see negative values.

- `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect(DynamicAmount, boolean cantRegenerate, boolean exileInsteadOfDie)`; `(int)`, `(int, boolean)`, `(DynamicAmount)` — any target. Amounts: `Fixed`, `XValue` (X spells / cost-snapshotted power), `SourcePower`, `CountersOnSource(CHARGE)`, …; `sacrificeOnly(int)` is the `ON_DEATH` form for "when you sacrifice this, it deals N damage to any target" and skips non-sacrifice deaths
- `DealDamageToRandomAnyTargetEffect(DynamicAmount)`; `(int)` — deals the damage to one "any target" (creature, planeswalker or player) picked uniformly at random **at resolution**. Nothing targets, so no target is declared on activation (Goblin Test Pilot)
- `DealDamageToSourceEffect(DynamicAmount)`; `(int)` — the source permanent deals damage to itself (no target). Pair with `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect` for "deals X to any target and X to itself" (Sunflare Shaman)
- `DealDamageToAttackedTargetEffect(int damage)` — damage to the player or planeswalker attacked by the creature that caused the attack trigger
- `EquippedCreatureDealsPowerToAttackedTargetEffect()` — Equipment `ON_ATTACK`: equipped creature deals damage equal to its power to the player/planeswalker it's attacking (Mage Slayer); non-targeting, equipped creature is the damage source
- `DealDamageToTriggeringAttackerEffect(int damage, PermanentPredicate attackerCondition)` — `ON_CREATURE_ATTACKS_YOU`: damage to the attacking creature; `attackerCondition` restricts which attackers trigger it (Raking Canopy: flyers, 4)
- `SourceFightsTargetCreatureEffect()` — source fights target
- `SourceFightsRandomOpponentCreatureEffect()` — source fights a creature an opponent controls chosen uniformly at random at resolution; nothing is targeted. Scab-Clan Giant (`ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`, no `target(...)` on the card)
- `EnteringCreatureFightsTargetCreatureEffect()` — the creature that just entered ("that creature") fights target creature; targeted harmful CREATURE, narrowed by the card's `TargetFilter`. Gruul Ragebeast (`ON_SELF_OR_ALLY_CREATURE_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD` + `TargetFilters.creatureAnOpponentControls()`)
- `EnchantedCreatureFightsTargetCreatureEffect()` — the Aura's enchanted creature (re-derived from the source Aura's `attachedTo`, NOT the Aura permanent) fights target creature; targeted harmful CREATURE. Cartouche of Strength (ETB `MayEffect`, opponent-creature target group)
- `PackHuntEffect(CardSubtype)` — pack hunt
- `DealDamageToTargetAndTheirCreaturesEffect(DynamicAmount)` / `(int)` — target player or planeswalker + each creature that player/pw's controller controls (`XValue` for Lavalanche, `Fixed` for Flame Wave / Chandra Nalaar ultimate). Doubles as a `CumulativeUpkeepEffect` unpaid effect (Heart of Bogardan) — the fallback path has no target, so it hits the source controller's opponent
- `DealDamageToEachCreatureDamagedPlayerControlsEffect()` — damage to damaged player's creatures
- `DealDamageToDefendingPlayerCreaturesEffect(int damage[, PermanentPredicate filter])` — ON_ATTACK, non-targeting: the source deals fixed damage to each matching creature controlled by the defending player. The attacked player or attacked planeswalker's controller is carried as `attackedTargetId`; use inside `MayEffect` for Ronin Cliffrider and Scalding Salamander rather than a player-targeting effect
- `DestroyPermanentDamagedPlayerControlsEffect(PermanentPredicate, int minimumDamage)` — ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER, mandatory: destroy target matching permanent (e.g. `PermanentIsLandPredicate`) the damaged player controls, only when `minimumDamage`+ dealt (Deus of Calamity). Destroy analog of `ExilePermanentDamagedPlayerControlsEffect`
- `SacrificePermanentDamagedPlayerControlsEffect(PermanentPredicate, int minimumDamage)` — ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER, mandatory: controller chooses a target matching permanent (e.g. `PermanentIsCreaturePredicate`) the damaged player controls, that player **sacrifices** it (only when `minimumDamage`+ dealt). Ashling, the Extinguisher (minimumDamage=0). Sacrifice analog of `DestroyPermanentDamagedPlayerControlsEffect`
- `DealDamageToTargetCreatureEffect(DynamicAmount, boolean unpreventable)`; `(int)`, `(int, boolean)`, `(DynamicAmount)` — target creature. Amounts: `Fixed`, `XValue`, `SourceToughness`, `PermanentCount` (subtype counts), `ManaSpentToCast`
- `EnchantedCreatureDealsDamageToTargetCreatureEffect(DynamicAmount, PermanentPredicate)`; `(int, PermanentPredicate)` — the creature attached to the source Aura deals damage to the targeted creature, using the enchanted creature as the damage source for protection, prevention, and damage triggers. Used by Dizzying Gaze.
- `DealDamageToTargetCreatureEffect(DynamicAmount, boolean unpreventable)`; `(int)`, `(int, boolean)`, `(DynamicAmount)` — target creature. Amounts: `Fixed`, `XValue`, `SourceToughness`, `PermanentCount` (subtype counts), `ManaSpentToCast`, `DamageDealtToTargetPermanentThisTurn`
- `DealDamageToTargetCreatureDamagedPlayerControlsEffect(DynamicAmount)`; convenience `(int)` — ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER may trigger or ON_ALLY_INSTANT_OR_SORCERY_DEALS_DAMAGE trigger; after any may choice, the controller chooses exactly one creature controlled by the damaged player and the source deals the evaluated amount through the normal damage-prevention/SBA pipeline. `EventValue` supports "that much" (Spark Mage, Satyr Firedancer)
- `DealDamageToTargetCreatureThenBoostSourceIfDamagedEffect(int damage, PermanentPredicate boostTargetPredicate, int powerBoost, int toughnessBoost)` — enter trigger: deals damage to the entering creature and boosts the source only if that creature matches the predicate and actually received damage
- `DealDamageToTargetCreatureAndAllWithSameNameEffect(DynamicAmount)` / `(int)` — target creature + each other creature with the same name across all battlefields; only the named target is targeted (hexproof same-name still hit). Fizzles if target leaves. Izzet Staticaster
- `DealDamageEqualToChosenTypeCountEffect(TargetPredicates.creature())` / `(TargetPredicates.anyTarget())` — "Choose a creature type. Deals damage to target creature / any target equal to the number of permanents you control of the chosen type" (Coordinated Barrage / Roar of the Crowd). Resolution-time creature-type choice (`beginSpellCreatureTypeChoice`, stored on `GameData.chosenSpellSubtype`), Changeling-aware count; the declared target picks the creature vs any-target damage path, and nothing else is accepted. Pair the creature form with an attacking/blocking target filter
- `DealDamageToTargetCreatureOrPlaneswalkerEffect(DynamicAmount)` / `(int)` / `(int, PermanentPredicate)` — creature or planeswalker (`PermanentCount` for subtype-scaled X; Voldaren Ambusher)
- `DealDamageToEachPlayerEqualToChosenColorCreatureCountEffect()` — "Choose a color. [This] deals damage to each player equal to the number of creatures of that color that player controls" (Searing Rays). Resolution-time color choice (`beginSpellColorChoice`, stored on `GameData.chosenSpellColor`); counts effective-color creatures separately for each player and routes each damage event through `DamageSupport`
- `DealDamageToTargetPlayerOrPlaneswalkerEffect(DynamicAmount, PlayerRelation)` / `(int, PlayerRelation)` / `(DynamicAmount)` / `(int)` — target player or planeswalker; the relation-less forms mean `PlayerRelation.ANY`, i.e. any player incl. the controller (Boggart Shenanigans; Brion Stoutarm's sacrificed-power `XValue`). `PlayerRelation.OPPONENT` is the "target opponent or planeswalker" wording (Burning Sun's Avatar, Final Strike) and is the only form the ON_DEALT_DAMAGE auto-targeting handles
- `DealDamageToTargetAndUpToCreaturesThatPlayerControlsEffect(int playerDamage, int creatureDamage, int maxCreatureTargets[, boolean opponentOnly, boolean creaturesCantBlock])` — target player or planeswalker plus up to N creatures that player (or that planeswalker's controller) controls; `opponentOnly` restricts the first target to an opponent, `creaturesCantBlock` adds the can't-block rider
- `ExileTopCardsChooseSpellAndCastCopiesEffect(int count, int copies)` — exile top N, choose one instant/sorcery among them, cast that many free copies of it
- `DealDamageToPermanentsTargetControlsEffect(int)` / `(int, boolean damagedCreaturesMustAttackThisTurn)` / `(int, DamagedPermanentScope)` — damage each permanent target player controls in scope: `CREATURES` (default; Radiating Lightning, Simoon) or `CREATURES_AND_PLANESWALKERS` (Chandra, Bold Pyromancer's −7). With the flag, each creature *actually* dealt damage this way must attack this turn if able (Aggravate)
- `DealDamageDividedEvenlyAmongCreaturesTargetControlsEffect(DynamicAmount)` — `floor(amount/creatureCount)` to each creature target player controls (Dwarven Catapult); pair with card-level opponent-only `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter`
- `DealDamageToEachMatchingPermanentEffect(int, PermanentPredicate, EachPermanentScope)` — damage each matching permanent across `ALL_PLAYERS`/`TARGET_PLAYER`
- `DealDamageToEachPlayerControllingMatchingPermanentEffect(int, PermanentPredicate)` — damage each player controlling a matching permanent (Disorder)
- "If this is the Nth time this ability has resolved this turn, [X]" — `ConditionalEffect(new NthAbilityResolutionThisTurn(n), X)` on an activated ability; the engine counts resolutions per source permanent (`GameData.permanentAbilityResolutionsThisTurn`), condition is met only on the exact n-th resolution. Ashling the Pilgrim = `PutCountersOnSelfEffect(PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE)` + `ConditionalEffect(NthAbilityResolutionThisTurn(3), RemoveAllCountersEffect(PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE))` + `ConditionalEffect(NthAbilityResolutionThisTurn(3), MassDamageEffect(new EventValue(), true))`
- `InnerFlameIgniterEffect()` — **card-specific.** On the exact third resolution this turn, creatures you control gain first strike until end of turn. Pair with `BoostAllOwnCreaturesEffect(1, 0)` in the same ability for the unconditional +1/+0 (Inner-Flame Igniter)
- `DealDamageToPlayersEffect(DynamicAmount, DamageRecipient)`; `(int, recipient)`; `.enchantedAttachedCount(PermanentPredicate)` — **unified player damage.** Recipients: `TARGET_PLAYER` (only targeting one; `Fixed`/`CardsInGraveyard` Scrapyard Salvo/`CardsInHand(TARGET_PLAYER)` Sudden Impact + Sword of War and Peace), `EACH_OPPONENT` (single eval, same value; `Fixed`/`CountersOnSource` Hallar), `EACH_PLAYER` (Slagstorm), `CONTROLLER` (self/pain lands), `ENCHANTED_PLAYER` (curse upkeep; `.enchantedAttachedCount` Curse of Thirst), `TARGET_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER` (Chandra's Outrage), `TARGET_SPELL_CONTROLLER` (Refuse — pair with `TargetSpellManaValue`; Essence Backlash — pair with `TargetSpellPower`), `TRIGGERING_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER` (Magnetic Mine / Dingus Egg / Dingus Staff)
- `DealDamageToOpponentsAndPlaneswalkersDamagedBySourceEffect(int)` — non-targeting upkeep-style effect; deals damage to current opponents and currently existing planeswalkers whose UUID is in the source permanent's game-long damage-recipient history. Used by The Fallen.
- For "another [subtype] you control enters, it deals damage equal to its power to each opponent," reuse `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect(CardSubtypePredicate(subtype), DealDamageToPlayersEffect(SourcePower, EACH_OPPONENT))` on `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD`; the enter collector binds the entering permanent as the damage source.
- `DealDamageToPlayersEffect(DynamicAmount, DamageRecipient)`; `(int, recipient)`; `.enchantedAttachedCount(PermanentPredicate)` — **unified player damage.** Recipients: `TARGET_PLAYER` (only targeting one; `Fixed`/`CardsInGraveyard` Scrapyard Salvo/`CardsInHand(TARGET_PLAYER)` Sudden Impact + Sword of War and Peace), `EACH_OPPONENT` (single eval, same value; `Fixed`/`CountersOnSource` Hallar), `EACH_PLAYER` (Slagstorm), `CONTROLLER` (self/pain lands), `ENCHANTED_PLAYER` (curse upkeep; `.enchantedAttachedCount` Curse of Thirst), `TARGET_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER` (Chandra's Outrage), `TARGET_SPELL_CONTROLLER` (Refuse — pair with `TargetSpellManaValue`; Essence Backlash — pair with `TargetSpellPower`), `TRIGGERING_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER` (Magnetic Mine)
- `DealDamageToEachPlayerEqualToFractionOfLifeRoundedDownEffect(int divisor)` — damage each player for `floor(that player's life / divisor)` (Heartless Hidetsugu `2`). Per-player amounts; not `DealDamageToPlayersEffect`
- `DealDamageIfDidntCastSpellThisTurnEffect(int damage)` — `END_STEP_TRIGGERED`: deals N damage to the end-step player (`entry.getTargetId()`, baked to the active player by StepTriggerService) if that player didn't cast a spell this turn. Intervening-if checked at trigger and resolution (Impatience)
- `DealDamageToEndStepPlayerIfLifeAtMostEffect(int damage, int lifeThreshold)` — `END_STEP_TRIGGERED`: deals N damage to the end-step player (`entry.getTargetId()`, baked by StepTriggerService via the `EndStepPlayerTargetedEffect` marker) if that player's life total is `lifeThreshold` or less. Intervening-if checked at trigger and resolution (Razor Pendulum)
- `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect.forTargetGroup(int damage, int targetGroup)` — damage aimed at a target group's chosen target (Goblin Barrage kicked target)
- `MassDamageEffect(DynamicAmount, damagesPlayers, damagesPlaneswalkers, PermanentPredicate)` — mass damage; convenience ctors `(int)`, `(int, damagesPlayers)`, `(DynamicAmount, damagesPlayers)`, `(int, usesXValue, damagesPlayers, filter)` (+ planeswalker overload). The full ctor adds `boolean perCreatureAmount`: when true the amount is evaluated once **per damaged creature** with that creature as the amount's source permanent ("2 damage to each creature for each Aura attached to that creature" = `Scaled(AttachmentsOnSource(true, false), 2)` + `perCreatureAmount=true`, Baki's Curse); players are never damaged in that mode. Static factory `exilingDamageToEachCreature(int)` sets the 6th component `exileInsteadOfDie` — each creature the damage actually reaches is marked so it is exiled instead of dying this turn (Yamabushi's Storm). Static factory `cantRegenerate(int, boolean, PermanentPredicate)` sets the 7th component `cantRegenerate` — each creature that actually receives damage can't be regenerated this turn (Flamebreak)
- "deal N damage to any target and you gain M life" — **compose** `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect(dmg)` + `GainLifeEffect(life)` as two effects on the same slot. Targeting auto-derives from the damage effect; the life is gained on resolution and the single-target spell fizzles (no life) if the target becomes illegal. Works for every amount shape:
  - fixed life (Essence Drain, Dark Nourishment, Ajani Vengeant dz??dz?2): `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect(N)` + `GainLifeEffect(M)`
  - X spell (Consume Spirit, Drain Life): `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect(new XValue())` + `GainLifeEffect(new XValue())` — life = X, not damage dealt
  - dynamic count (Corrupt): `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect(new PermanentCount(pred, scope))` + `GainLifeEffect(new PermanentCount(pred, scope))` — both counts evaluate equal at resolution (deaths deferred to SBA). Corrupt's oracle "life equal to the damage dealt this way" is prevention-sensitive; this gains the full count
  - Soul Burn (life = unprevented damage, capped by {B} spent on X and target life/loyalty/toughness): `SoulBurnEffect()` + `setXColorRestrictions(BLACK, RED)` — do **not** compose damage+GainLife; the caps are prevention- and payment-sensitive
- `DealDividedDamageEffect` (unified divided/multi-target damage) — factories: `.chosenAmongAnyTargets(int)` (Fight with Fire kicked), `.chosenAmongAnyTargets(DynamicAmount)` (Jaws of Stone — dynamic total, e.g. Mountains you control, divided among any targets), `.chosenAmongTargetCreatures(int)` (Ignite Disorder), `.chosenAmongAnyTargetsEtb(int,int)` (Inferno Titan/Bogardan ETB), `.xAmongAttackingCreatures()` (Hail of Arrows), `.xAmongTargetCreatures()` (Fire Covenant), `.xAmongTargetCreaturesCantBlock()` (Huatli dz??dz?X), `.xDividedEvenly()` (Fireball), `.ordered(List<Integer>)` (Cone of Flame/Arc Trail), `.xAmongAnyTargetsTapAndLockNoncreature()` (Aurelia's Fury — taps each damaged creature and stops each damaged player casting noncreature spells this turn). Custom X/CHOSEN filters: construct with `targetRestriction` (Rock Slide — attacking/blocking without flying). `targetSpec()` is derived: `ANY_TARGET` when `canTargetPlayers`, else `CREATURE`, narrowed by `targetRestriction` — so targeting really is creatures-only / attacking-only where the oracle text says so, in enumeration and at cast alike
- `DealDamageToEachTargetEffect(DynamicAmount[, PermanentPredicate filter])` — full amount to each of multiple targets (Jaya's Immolating Inferno with `XValue`, Chandra the Firebrand's -6). Spec is `harmful(ANY_TARGET)`. Optional `filter` restricts *damage* to targeted permanents matching it (targeted players/non-matching skipped) and is NOT a targeting restriction — Winter Blast: `targetX(creatureFilter, cap)` bound to `TapPermanentsEffect(TARGET)` + `DealDamageToEachTargetEffect(new Fixed(2), new PermanentHasKeywordPredicate(FLYING))` ("tap X target creatures; 2 damage to each of those with flying")
- `TargetDealsPowerDamageToTargetEffect()` — bite (group indices `(sourceTargetGroup, victimTargetGroup)` default to 0, 1)
- `ChosenPermanentDealsPowerDamageToTargetCreatureEffect()` — the permanent recorded as chosen on the stack entry deals its power to target creature; the chosen permanent is the damage source
- `ControlledCreaturesDealPowerDamageToTargetEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — each controlled creature matching the filter deals its power to target creature (Moonlight Hunt); no tap, no damage back
- `TargetCreatureDealsPowerDamageToSelfEffect()` — target deals its power to itself
- `TargetCreatureDealsPowerDamageToEachOtherCreatureAndEachOpponentEffect()` — target creature you control deals its power to each OTHER creature (all battlefields) and to each opponent; the target is the damage source and is itself untouched (Chandra's Ignition)
- `TargetCreatureDealsPowerDamageToControllerEffect()` — target creature deals its power to that creature's controller; the creature is the damage source (Dong Zhou, the Tyrant, ETB)
- `EachCreatureDealsDamageToItsControllerEffect(int damage)` — each creature on the battlefield deals N damage to its controller; each creature is its own damage source (Rakdos Charm mode 3)
- `EachEnchantmentDealsDamageToControllerThenEachAuraToEnchantedCreatureEffect(int damage)` — each enchantment deals N to its controller, then each Aura attached to a creature deals N to that creature; each enchantment is its own damage source (Aura Barbs)
- `DealDamageToCombatOpponentControllerEffect(DynamicAmount amount)` — combat trigger: THIS creature deals `amount` to the controller of its combat opponent (the creature it blocks / that becomes blocked by it). A `CombatOpponentReferencingEffect` (block trigger bakes the opponent as the non-targeting `targetId`, and on an aura/equipment `CombatTriggerService` auto-targets the enchanted creature's opponent). Meglonoth (`ON_BLOCK`, `new SourcePower()`). Differs from `TargetCreatureDealsPowerDamageToControllerEffect` — there the *target* creature is the damage source; here the *source* creature is, and `amount` reads it via `SourcePower`
- `TargetCreatureDealsPowerDamageToAnyTargetEffect()` — group 0 creature deals its power to any target (group 1); the creature is the damage source (Soul's Fire). Groups `(sourceTargetGroup, victimTargetGroup)` default to 0, 1
- `EnchantedCreatureDealsPowerDamageToTargetCreatureEffect()` — the source Aura's enchanted creature deals damage equal to its power to target creature; the enchanted creature is the damage source for prevention, protection, triggers, and lifelink (Laccolith Rig)
- `DiscardRandomCardDealDiscardedPowerToTargetPlayerOrPlaneswalkerEffect()` — discard a card at random; if it's a creature card, source deals damage equal to that card's power to target player/planeswalker (Cragganwick Cremator ETB). Target always chosen; non-creature discard = no damage
- `DiscardRandomCardReturnCreatureUnlessAnyPlayerPaysLifeEffect(int lifeCost)` — upkeep discard at random; if the discarded card is a creature, players in APNAP order may each pay the fixed life cost, and the first payment prevents that card from returning from its owner's graveyard to the battlefield (Aether Rift)
- `FightTargetsEffect()` — fight (group indices `(firstTargetGroup, secondTargetGroup)` default to 0, 1)
- `MassFightTargetCreatureEffect()` — Alpha Brawl-style mass fight
- `PreventDamageEffect.nextToSelf(int)` — activated ability (no target): "Prevent the next N damage that would be dealt to this creature this turn" (Ethereal Champion). Shields the ability's own source permanent via its `damagePreventionShield`; next N damage from any source (combat or noncombat), then consumed. Hooked in `PreventDamageEffectHandler`
- `PreventDamageEffect.nextToEnchanted(int)` — activated ability on an Aura (no target): "Prevent the next N damage that would be dealt to enchanted creature this turn" (Fylgja). Shields the Aura's attached permanent via its `damagePreventionShield`; next N damage from any source (combat or noncombat), then consumed. Hooked in `PreventDamageEffectHandler`
- `PreventDamageEffect.nextToController(int)` — activated ability (no target): "Prevent the next N damage that would be dealt to you this turn" (Esper Battlemage). Shields the ability's controller via `GameData.playerDamagePreventionShields`; next N damage from any source (combat or noncombat), then consumed, cleared at end of turn. Hooked in `PreventDamageEffectHandler`
- `PreventDamageEffect.nextCombatToController(int)` — activated ability (no target): "Prevent the next N combat damage that would be dealt to you" (Decorated Griffin). Uses a separate player shield consumed only by combat damage; cleared at end of turn. Hooked in `PreventDamageEffectHandler`
- `PreventDamageEffect.allCombatToPlayers()` — SPELL: "Prevent all combat damage that would be dealt to players this turn" (Defend the Hearth). Combat damage to creatures and planeswalkers is unaffected; cleared at end of turn. Hooked in `PreventDamageEffectHandler`
- `PreventDamageEffect.nextToEachCreatureAndPlayer(int)` — activated ability (no target): "Prevent the next N damage that would be dealt to each creature and each player this turn" (Kitsune Palliator). Gives every creature on the battlefield as it resolves its own next-N `damagePreventionShield` and every player a `playerDamagePreventionShields` entry; creatures entering later are unaffected. Hooked in `PreventDamageEffectHandler`
- `PreventDamageEffect.nextToTargetCreature(int)` — "Prevent the next N damage that would be dealt to target creature this turn" (Soldevi Heretic). Same shield write as `nextToTarget`, but declares a creature-only `TargetSpec` so a player can never be chosen
- `PreventDamageEffect.nextToTargetCreature(int, PermanentPredicate)` — same, but the predicate narrows the creature-only `TargetSpec` further: "target legendary creature" (Eiganjo Castle) is `nextToTargetCreature(2, new PermanentHasSupertypePredicate(CardSupertype.LEGENDARY))`
- `PreventDamageEffect.nextToTargetPlayerOrPlaneswalker(int)` — "Prevent the next N damage that would be dealt to target player or planeswalker this turn" (Wandering Mage). Same shield write as `nextToTarget`, but declares a `PLAYER_OR_PLANESWALKER` `TargetSpec` so a creature can never be chosen
- `PreventDamageEffect.nextToTarget(DynamicAmount)` — "Prevent the next N damage to any target"; use `FixedIfTargetMatches(predicate, amount, otherwise)` for "… If it's a [matching permanent], prevent M instead" (Elvish Healer); use `FixedIfTargetPlayerControlsMoreLands(amount, otherwise)` as a `SearchLibraryEffect` count for Tithe (up to 2 when target has more lands, else up to 1)
- `PreventNextDamageToTargetAndAddToughnessCountersEffect(int amount)` — SPELL (benign CREATURE target): "Prevent the next N damage to target creature this turn; at the next end step put a +0/+1 counter on it for each 1 damage prevented this way" (Sacred Boon). Sets the target's `damageToCounterPreventionShield`; prevented damage is accumulated into `DelayedPlusZeroPlusOneCounters` (drained by `StepTriggerService` as a `PutCountersOnSourceEffect(0, 1, total)` trigger). +0/+1 counters use `CounterType.PLUS_ZERO_PLUS_ONE` (toughness only). Hooked in `DamagePreventionService.applyCreaturePreventionShield`
- `PreventXDamageToControllerAndPermanentsAndRedirectToAnyTargetEffect()` — SPELL (harmful `ANY_TARGET`): "Prevent the next X damage that would be dealt to you and/or permanents you control this turn. If damage is prevented this way, this deals that much damage to any target" (Divine Deflection). Adds a `DamageRedirectShield` with `coversControlledPermanents=true`; consumed by `DamagePreventionService.applyPlayerPreventionShield` and `applyCreaturePreventionShield`, the prevented damage dealt on by `DamageSupport.processPendingRedirectDamage`. Cleared at turn cleanup
- `PreventXDamageToTargetCreatureAndRedirectToAnyTargetEffect(DynamicAmount)` — SPELL with two target groups: "Prevent the next X damage that would be dealt to target creature this turn. If damage is prevented this way, this deals that much damage to any target" (Acolyte's Reward). Evaluates the amount on resolution, then adds an exact-creature `DamageRedirectShield`; the prevented damage is dealt to the second target through the normal damage pipeline. Cleared at turn cleanup
- `PreventNextDamageToTargetAndAddToughnessCountersEffect(int amount, true)` — SPELL (benign ANY_TARGET): Scars of the Veteran — same creature path as above; players get a plain `playerDamagePreventionShields` next-N shield (no counters). Pair with `AlternateHandCast(ExileCardsFromHandCastingCost(CardColorPredicate(WHITE), "white"))`
- `PreventNextDamageToTargetAndAddPlusOnePlusOneCountersEffect(DynamicAmount)` — SPELL (benign CREATURE target): "Prevent the next X damage that would be dealt to target creature this turn. For each 1 damage prevented this way, put a +1/+1 counter on that creature" (Temper). Evaluates X on resolution, stores a target-creature shield, and adds the +1/+1 counters immediately as damage is prevented; covers combat and noncombat damage and clears at turn cleanup
- `PreventDividedDamageEffect(DynamicAmount amount)` (with an `int` convenience constructor) — SPELL or activated ability (no `target()` call): "Prevent the next N damage that would be dealt this turn to any number of targets, divided as you choose" (Remedy, Serra's Hymn). Per-target shield amounts come from the cast-time or activation-time `damageAssignments` map, summing to N; each target gets a "next X damage" shield via `PreventDividedDamageEffectHandler`. Reuses the damage-distribution flow (`EffectResolution.needsDamageDistribution`). Shields expire at end of turn
- `PreventNoncombatDamageToControllerAndGainLifeEffect()` — STATIC: prevent all noncombat damage to controller; they gain life equal to the damage prevented (Purity). Hooked in `DamageSupport.dealDamageToPlayer`
- `PreventDamageToControllerPerClericEffect()` — STATIC: "If a source would deal damage to a player, you may prevent X of that damage, where X = Clerics you control" (Battletide Alchemist). Modeled controller-only; prevents up to (Clerics controlled Adz? number of these permanents) per source, combat and noncombat. Via `DamagePreventionService.applyControllerPerClericDamagePrevention`, hooked in `DamageSupport.dealDamageToPlayer` (noncombat) and `CombatDamageService.accumulatePlayerDamage` (combat, per attacker)
- `FlipCoinDoubleOrPreventNextDamageFromChosenSourceEffect()` — SPELL (no target): "Choose a source you control and flip a coin. If you win the flip, the next time that source would deal damage this turn, it deals double that damage instead. If you lose the flip, the next time it would deal damage this turn, prevent that damage" (Desperate Gambit). The source is chosen on resolution among the controller's permanents; the coin is flipped after the choice. Both branches install a `SourceNextDamageToAnyTargetShield` — `doubling(sourceId)` (`damageMultiplier` 2) on a win, the plain prevention shield (`damageMultiplier` 0) on a loss — so one consumption path in `DamagePreventionService.applyChosenSourceNextDamageToAnyTargetShield` covers combat and noncombat damage to any recipient. A non-zero multiplier is a replacement, not prevention, so it still applies while damage can't be prevented
- `FlipCoinDoubleOrPreventNextCombatDamageFromAttackingCreatureEffect()` — `ON_ANY_CREATURE_ATTACKS`: "Whenever a creature attacks, flip a coin. If you win the flip, the next time that creature would deal combat damage this turn, it deals double that damage instead. If you lose the flip, the next time that creature would deal combat damage this turn, prevent that damage" (Impulsive Maneuvers). The trigger's attacking creature is the non-targeting `targetId`; the handler installs `SourceNextDamageToAnyTargetShield.combatDoubling(sourceId)` or `.combatPrevention(sourceId)`, which skips noncombat damage and is consumed by the next combat damage event
- `EyeForAnEyeEffect()` — SPELL: "The next time a source of your choice would deal damage to you this turn, instead that source deals that much damage to you and Eye for an Eye deals that much damage to that source's controller" (Eye for an Eye). Reflection, not prevention — you still take the damage; an equal reflected event is dealt at the chosen source's controller. Source chosen on resolution (any battlefield permanent). Shield in `GameData.eyeForAnEyeShields`, applied via `DamagePreventionService.applyEyeForAnEyeReflection` (hooked in `DamageSupport.dealDamageToPlayer` + `CombatDamageService.accumulatePlayerDamage`), reflected damage queued in `pendingEyeForAnEyeReflections`
- `PreventDamageFromChosenSourceEffect.nextDamageToEnchantedCreature()` — activated ability on an Aura (no target): "The next time a source of your choice would deal damage to enchanted creature this turn, prevent that damage" (Kithkin Armor, `SacrificeSelfCost`). Resolves "enchanted creature" from `StackEntry.sourcePermanentSnapshot.getAttachedTo()` because the Aura is sacrificed as a cost. Installs `SourceNextDamageToAnyTargetShield.forRecipient(sourceId, enchantedId)` — the same one-shot shield as Sanctum Guardian but limited to that single recipient
- `RedirectNextDamageFromChosenSourceToSelfEffect()` — activated ability (no target): "The next time a source of your choice would deal damage this turn, that damage is dealt to this creature instead" (Opal-Eye, Konda's Yojimbo). Redirection (replacement), not prevention — the intended recipient (player, planeswalker or creature) takes nothing and the ability's own source permanent takes the full amount, combat or noncombat. Source chosen on resolution (any battlefield permanent). Shield in `GameData.sourceNextDamageRedirectToPermanentShields`, applied via `DamagePreventionService.applySourceNextDamageRedirectToPermanent` (hooked in `DamageSupport.dealCreatureDamage`/`dealDamageToPlayer` + `CombatDamageService.accumulatePlayerDamage`/`applyCombatCreatureDamage`), redirected damage queued in `pendingSourceRedirectDamage`. Kept in place while the destination is no longer a creature on the battlefield; damage to the destination itself is left alone
- `ReflectNextDamageFromChosenSourceToItsControllerEffect()` — SPELL: "The next time a source of your choice would deal damage this turn, that damage is dealt to that source's controller instead" (Reflect Damage). Redirection (replacement), not prevention — the original recipient (player, planeswalker or creature) takes nothing and the source's controller takes the full amount, combat or noncombat. Source chosen on resolution (any battlefield permanent). Shield in `GameData.reflectDamageToSourceControllerShields`, applied via `DamagePreventionService.applyReflectDamageToSourceControllerShield` (hooked in `DamageSupport.dealCreatureDamage`/`dealDamageToPlayer` + `CombatDamageService.accumulatePlayerDamage`/`applyCombatCreatureDamage`), redirected damage queued in `pendingEyeForAnEyeReflections`
- `RedirectNextCombatDamageToOpponentEffect()` — activated ability (target creature): "The next time this creature would deal combat damage to an opponent this turn, it deals that damage to target creature instead" (Soltari Guerrillas). One-shot, source-keyed redirection; only combat damage dealt to an opponent is diverted. Shield in `GameData.sourceNextCombatDamageToOpponentRedirectShields`, applied via `DamagePreventionService.applySourceNextCombatDamageToOpponentRedirect` in `CombatDamageService.accumulatePlayerDamageInternal`
- `PreventFixedDamagePerSourceToControllerEffect.fromAnySource(int amount)` / `.fromCreatures(int amount)` / `.fromArtifacts(int amount)` — STATIC: "If a source would deal damage to you, prevent N of that damage" (Urza's Armor); `fromCreatures` narrows to creature sources only (Orbs of Warding), and `fromArtifacts` narrows to artifact sources only (Sphere of Purity). Prevents a fixed `amount` per matching source, combat and noncombat, to the controller; multiple copies stack. The three-argument `fromCreatures(amount, combatOnly, requiresUntappedSource)` form supports Thunderstaff's untapped, combat-only creature prevention. Via `DamagePreventionService.applyControllerFixedPerSourceDamagePrevention`, hooked in `DamageSupport.dealDamageToPlayer` (noncombat) and `CombatDamageService.accumulatePlayerDamage` (combat, per attacker)
- `PreventFixedDamagePerSourceToControllerEffect.fromAnySource(int amount)` / `.fromCreatures(int amount)` / `.fromColors(Set<CardColor> colors, int amount)` — STATIC: "If a source would deal damage to you, prevent N of that damage" (Urza's Armor); `fromCreatures` narrows to creature sources (Orbs of Warding), and `fromColors` narrows to sources whose damage colors include one of the given colors (Sphere of Duty uses green and 2). Prevents a fixed `amount` per matching source, combat and noncombat, to the controller; multiple copies stack. Source colors are evaluated for damage purposes. Via `DamagePreventionService.applyControllerFixedPerSourceDamagePrevention`, hooked in `DamageSupport.dealDamageToPlayer` and `CombatDamageService.accumulatePlayerDamage`
- `PreventFixedDamageToPlaneswalkersYouControlEffect(int amount)` — STATIC: "If a source would deal damage to a planeswalker you control, prevent N of that damage" (Djeru, With Eyes Open). Planeswalker sibling of Urza's Armor: prevents a fixed `amount` per source from each damage event to a planeswalker the source's controller controls, combat and noncombat; multiple copies stack. Implements the `PlaneswalkerDamagePreventionEffect` capability interface (read as a FACT, not by concrete type). Via `DamagePreventionService.applyPlaneswalkerFixedPerSourceDamagePrevention`, hooked in the planeswalker branches of `DamageSupport.dealCreatureDamage` / `DamageSupport.resolveAnyTargetDamage` (noncombat) and `CombatDamageService.accumulatePlayerDamage` (combat, per attacker)
- `PreventDamageUpToAmountToSelfEffect(int amount)` — STATIC: prevent a whole damage event to the source permanent when that event is at most `amount` (Callous Giant). Applies to combat and noncombat damage; larger events are unaffected. Implements `SelfDamagePreventionEffect` and is consumed by `DamagePreventionService.applyCreaturePreventionShield`
- `PreventCombatDamageToAttackingCreaturesYouControlEffect()` — STATIC: prevent all combat damage dealt to attacking creatures the source's controller controls (Dolmen Gate). Checked in `DamagePreventionService.applyCreaturePreventionShield` via `permanent.isAttacking()` + `isCombatDamage`
- `PreventAllCombatDamageToSelfEffect()` — STATIC: prevent all combat damage that would be dealt to the source permanent itself (Seraph of the Sword). Read off the permanent's own STATIC effects in `DamagePreventionService.applyCreaturePreventionShield`. The creature still deals its own combat damage; noncombat damage unaffected
- `PreventAllCombatDamageToSelfFromBlockersEffect()` — STATIC: prevent all combat damage dealt to the source permanent by creatures blocking it (Armored Transport). Per-source check in `DamagePreventionService.isCombatDamageFromBlockerPrevented`; damage the creature takes while blocking, and noncombat damage, is unaffected
- `PreventAllCombatDamageToAttachedCreatureEffect()` — STATIC on an Aura/Equipment: prevent all combat damage dealt to the attached creature (General's Kabuto). To-side only; the creature still deals its own combat damage and noncombat damage is unaffected. Checked in `DamagePreventionService.applyCreaturePreventionShield` via `hasAuraWithEffect`
- `PreventAllCombatDamageToAndBySelfEffect()` — STATIC: prevent all combat damage that would be dealt to and dealt by the source permanent itself (Fog Bank). Always-on marker read off the permanent's own STATIC effects: the to-side in `DamagePreventionService.applyCreaturePreventionShield`, the by-side in `GameQueryService.isPreventedFromDealingDamage(.., isCombatDamage)`. Noncombat damage unaffected. Use `PreventAllCombatDamageToAndByEnchantedCreatureEffect` for the Aura wording
- `CantBeDestroyedByLethalDamageUnlessSingleSourceEffect()` — STATIC marker (Ogre Enforcer): can't be destroyed by lethal damage unless lethal damage from a single source is marked on it. Read in `StateBasedActionService` against `Permanent.markedDamageBySource`; 0 toughness and deathtouch still destroy. No layer handler.
- `PreventDamageToCreaturesEffect(boolean noncombatOnly, boolean allCreatures, boolean excludeSource)` — STATIC: prevent all damage dealt to creatures. Use `.youControl(true)` = noncombat damage only to your creatures, combat unaffected (Mark of Asylum); `.youControl(false)` = all damage including combat to your creatures (Inner Sanctum); `.otherCreaturesYouControl(true)` = noncombat damage only to other creatures you control, leaving the source itself unprotected (Crystal Barricade); `.all()` = all damage to every creature on the battlefield regardless of controller (Bubble Matrix). Damage to players/planeswalkers is unaffected. Checked in `DamagePreventionService.applyCreaturePreventionShield`
- `PreventAllDamageToCreaturesYouControlEffect(PermanentPredicate filter)` — STATIC: prevent ALL damage (combat + noncombat) dealt to creatures the source's controller controls that match `filter` (`null` = every creature you control). Emmara Tandris uses `PermanentAllOfPredicate(PermanentIsCreaturePredicate, PermanentIsTokenPredicate)` for "creature tokens you control". Checked in `DamagePreventionService.applyCreaturePreventionShield` via `GameQueryService.isAllDamageToControlledCreaturePrevented`
- `PreventSpellDamageToOpponentAndCreateTokensEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` — STATIC: if a spell you control would deal damage to an opponent, prevent it and create one `token` per 1 damage prevented (Hostility). Hooked in `DamageSupport.dealDamageToPlayer`
- `PreventAllDamageToControllerAndExileFromGraveyardEffect()` — STATIC: prevent ALL damage (combat + noncombat) to the controller and exile a card from their graveyard for each 1 damage prevented (Immortal Coil). Via `DamageSupport.applyImmortalCoilPrevention`, hooked in both `DamageSupport.dealDamageToPlayer` (noncombat) and `CombatDamageService.applyPlayerDamage` (combat). Pair with the empty-graveyard `StateTriggerEffect` + `ControllerLosesGameEffect`
- `NefariousLichDamageReplacementEffect()` — STATIC replacement: replace damage to the controller with an exact exile of that many non-token graveyard cards; if the full exile cannot happen, the damage is still replaced and the controller loses. Via `DamageSupport.applyNefariousLichReplacement`, hooked in noncombat and combat player-damage paths
- `NefariousLichLifeGainReplacementEffect()` — STATIC replacement: replace life gain by drawing that many cards. Via `LifeSupport.applyGainLife` and `LifeSupport.applySetLifeTotal` through `DrawService`
- `DamageLifeFloorEffect(int floor, LifeFloorCondition condition)` — STATIC: while the condition holds, damage that would reduce the controller's life total to less than `floor` reduces it to `floor` instead. `CONTROLS_A_CREATURE` = Worship `(1, ...)`; `LIFE_AT_LEAST_FLOOR` = Elderscale Wurm `(7, ...)`. Damage-only (not life loss/payment/poison); full damage still dealt. `GameQueryService.damageLifeFloor` picks the highest active floor
- `PreventAllDamageToControllerEffect()` — STATIC: "Prevent all damage that would be dealt to you" (Glacial Chasm). Prevents ALL damage (combat + noncombat, including infect damage before poison conversion) to the controller of the permanent carrying it; damage to their permanents is unaffected. Via `DamageSupport.applyControllerAllDamagePrevention`, hooked in `DamageSupport.dealDamageToPlayer` (noncombat) and `CombatDamageService.applyPlayerDamage` (combat). The rider-free counterpart of `PreventAllDamageToControllerAndExileFromGraveyardEffect`. `PreventAllDamageToControllerEffect(true)` restricts it to the controller's own turn ("During your turn, …", Personal Sanctuary)
- `PreventDamageEffect.allToTargetCreatures()` — prevent all damage to target creature this turn (Wellgabber Apothecary). Adds target to `GameData.creaturesWithAllDamagePrevented`, checked in `DamagePreventionService.applyCreaturePreventionShield`, cleared at turn cleanup
- `PreventDamageEffect.allToSelf()` — activated ability (no target): prevent all damage that would be dealt to the source permanent this turn (Gideon of the Trials 0). Adds `entry.getSourcePermanentId()` to `GameData.creaturesWithAllDamagePrevented`; also guarded on the planeswalker-loyalty damage path in `DamageSupport.resolveAnyTargetDamage` so it stops loyalty loss. Cleared at turn cleanup
- `PreventDamageEffect.allCombatToSelf()` — no-target ability/trigger: prevent all **combat** damage that would be dealt to the source permanent this turn (Oketra's Avenger exert trigger). Combat-only variant of `allToSelf()`; adds `entry.getSourcePermanentId()` to `GameData.creaturesWithCombatDamagePrevented` (noncombat damage still goes through). Cleared at turn cleanup
- `PreventDamageEffect.allByTargetPermanentUntilNextTurn()` — activated ability, benign PERMANENT target: "Until your next turn, prevent all damage target permanent would deal" (Gideon of the Trials +1). Stores target → controller in `GameData.permanentsPreventedFromDealingDamageUntilNextTurn`; unlike `allByTargetCreatures()` it survives the cleanup step and expires at the controller's next turn (`TurnProgressionService`). All read sites go through `GameData.isPreventedFromDealingDamage(id)`
- `PreventDamageEffect.allToAndByTargetPermanentUntilNextTurn()` — activated ability, benign PERMANENT target: "Until your next turn, prevent all damage that would be dealt to and dealt by target permanent" (Kiora, the Crashing Wave +1). Combines the preceding effect with incoming damage prevention, storing target → controller in `GameData.permanentsProtectedFromDamageUntilNextTurn`; both entries expire at the controller's next turn
- `PreventDamageEffect.allToMatchingPermanents(PermanentPredicate)` — non-targeted: prevent all damage (combat + noncombat) this turn to every permanent matching the predicate, any controller (Ethersworn Shieldmage — "prevent all damage that would be dealt to artifact creatures this turn"). Adds the predicate to `GameData.allDamagePreventionPredicates`; re-evaluated per damage event via `GameQueryService.isAllDamagePreventedByPredicate` in `DamagePreventionService.applyCreaturePreventionShield` (ruling: set not locked at resolution), cleared at turn cleanup
- `DamageSourcesOfColorsAreColorlessEffect(Set<CardColor>)` — STATIC: sources of those colours are colourless sources of *damage* (Ghostly Flame). Damage-only; protection from the colour still stops blocking/targeting/enchanting. Read by `GameQueryService.getDamageSourceColor`/`hasProtectionFromDamageSource`
- `PreventColorDamageToEnchantedCreatureEffect()` — STATIC marker on an Aura: prevent all damage to enchanted creature from sources of the Aura's chosen colour (Prismatic Ward). Pair with `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD ChooseColorOnEnterEffect()`; damage-only, NOT protection. Queried in `GameQueryService.isColorDamageToEnchantedCreaturePrevented` from `DamageSupport.dealCreatureDamage` + `CombatDamageService.applyCombatCreatureDamage`
- `PreventDamageEffect.allCombatBySelf()` — no-target ability/trigger: prevent all **combat** damage the source permanent would deal this turn (Goblin Snowman's block trigger). By-side counterpart of `allCombatToSelf()`; adds `entry.getSourcePermanentId()` to `GameData.creaturesPreventedFromDealingCombatDamage`. Pair the two for "prevent all combat damage that would be dealt to and dealt by it this turn". Cleared at turn cleanup
- `PreventDamageEffect.allByTargetCreatures()` / `allCombatByTargetCreatures()` — prevent all damage target creature(s) would deal this turn (Soul Parry, Inquisitor's Snare). The combat-only factory prevents only combat damage via `GameData.creaturesPreventedFromDealingCombatDamage`, gating attacker participation in `CombatDamageService` (Resistance Fighter). Cleared at turn cleanup
- `PreventDamageEffect.allToController()` — SPELL: "Prevent all damage that would be dealt to you this turn" (Riot Control). Adds the controller to `GameData.playersWithAllPlayerDamagePrevented`, checked in `DamagePreventionService.applyPlayerPreventionShield` (so combat + noncombat, every damage path). Unlike `allToControllerAndCreatures()` the controller's creatures are NOT shielded. Cleared at turn cleanup
- `PreventDamageEffect.allToControllerFromAttackers()` — SPELL: prevent all damage attacking creatures would deal to the controller this turn (Deep Wood). Adds controller to `GameData.playersWithDamageFromAttackersPrevented`; combat damage prevented in `CombatDamageService.applyPlayerDamage`, noncombat only when the source permanent is attacking. Cleared at turn cleanup. Pair with `setSpellCastTimingRestriction(DECLARE_ATTACKERS_IF_ATTACKED)`
- `PreventDamageToOtherCreaturesAndAddPlusCountersEffect()` — STATIC: prevent all damage (combat or noncombat, any source) to *another* creature you control and put a +1/+1 counter on it per 1 damage prevented (Vigor). Checked in `DamagePreventionService.applyCreaturePreventionShield`; the effect is on a different permanent than the one being damaged
- `ReduceMinusOneMinusOneCountersEffect()` — STATIC replacement marker (Vizier of Remedies): "if one or more -1/-1 counters would be put on a creature you control, that many minus one are put on it instead." Every -1/-1 placement site routes its count through `GameQueryService.reduceMinusOneMinusOneCounters`, which subtracts one per marker the affected creature's controller controls (floored at zero). Unlike `CantHaveMinusOneMinusOneCountersEffect` (granted per-creature) it sits on the source and is COUNTED, so multiple copies stack. Covers spell/ability/each-creature counters, wither & infect damage, proliferate, enters-with, persist, and put-counter costs (Devoted Druid)
- `AddOnePlusOneCountersEffect()` — STATIC replacement marker (Hardened Scales): "if one or more +1/+1 counters would be put on a creature you control, that many plus one are put on it instead." Uses the shared +1/+1 counter replacement pipeline, adding one per marker the affected creature's controller controls. Covers spell/ability/each-creature counters, proliferate, enters-with, undying, explore, unleash, devour, tokens-with-counters, Vigor prevention, and put-counter costs. Multiple copies stack
- `DoubleCountersOnControlledPermanentsEffect()` — STATIC replacement marker (Doubling Season): doubles any counter type put on a permanent its controller controls. Uses the shared typed counter replacement pipeline, so it covers +1/+1, -1/-1, loyalty, charge, proliferate, enters-with, and other counter placement paths. Multiple copies stack multiplicatively
- `CantHavePlusOnePlusOneCountersEffect()` — STATIC per-creature marker (Blightbeetle): blocks +1/+1 counter placement on the creature carrying it, including counters from spells, abilities, proliferate, as-enters replacements, and damage-prevention effects. Usually granted with `GrantEffectEffect(..., GrantScope.OPPONENT_CREATURES)`; it does not block other counter types
- `PreventDamageToSelfFromCreaturesEffect(sourcePredicate)` — STATIC: "Prevent all damage that would be dealt to this creature by creatures" (Uncle Istvan), optionally restricted by a source predicate (Argothian Pixies uses artifact creatures). Prevents all damage (combat + noncombat) to the permanent carrying it whenever the source is a matching creature; noncreature sources are unaffected. Combat damage is prevented in `CombatDamageService`/`DamagePreventionService`; noncombat via `GameQueryService.isCreatureSourceDamageToSelfPrevented` hooked in `DamageSupport.dealCreatureDamage`
- `PreventAllDamageToSelfFromCreaturesItBlocksEffect()` — STATIC: prevents all damage (combat + noncombat) to the source creature from creatures it is blocking (Wall of Vapor). The current blocking assignment is checked in both combat and noncombat creature-damage paths
- `WallOnlyTargetingRestrictionEffect()` — STATIC marker: the permanent cannot be targeted by a spell or ability whose legal targets are restricted to Walls. Checked by the targeting services, including modal spells' full mode union
- `PreventDamageToSelfAndSourceControllerDrawsEffect()` — STATIC: "If a source would deal damage to this creature, prevent that damage. The source's controller draws cards equal to the damage prevented this way" (Swans of Bryn Argoll). Prevents all damage (combat + noncombat, any source) to the permanent carrying it; the source's controller draws one card per point prevented. Via `DamagePreventionService.applySwansSourceControllerDraw`, hooked in `DamageSupport.dealCreatureDamage` (noncombat, covers burn/mass/bite) and `CombatDamageService.applyCombatCreatureDamage` (combat, per source)
- `PreventCombatDamageToSelfAndExileFromLibraryEffect()` — STATIC: "If combat damage would be dealt to this creature, prevent that damage and exile that many cards from the top of your library" (Gloom Surgeon). Combat damage only (noncombat burn/bite/mass damage still applies); its controller exiles that many cards off their library top, stopping early on an empty library. Via `DamagePreventionService.applyPreventCombatDamageToSelfAndExile`, hooked only in `CombatDamageService.applyCombatCreatureDamage`
- `PreventNextDamageFromSelfToYouEffect()` — activated ability: "The next time this creature would deal damage to you this turn, prevent that damage" (Mercenaries). No source choice — shields the activating player from the ability's source permanent via `playerSourceNextDamageShields`. Pair with `.withActivatableByAnyPlayer()`
- `PreventDamageFromChosenSourceEffect` (factories `nextDamageToYou()` / `nextDamageToYouAndGainLife()` / `nextDamageToYouAndDamageSourceController()` / `nextDamageToYouAndExileFromLibrary()` / `nextDamageToYou(filter, label)` / `nextDamageToYouOfChosenColor()` / `nextDamageToAnyTarget()` / `nextDamageToAnyTargetAndDamageRedSourceController()` / `nextDamageToYouAndYourCreatures()` / `allDamageToYou()` / `allDamage(filter, label)` / `allDamageFromSourceSharingColorWithImprintedCard()`) — "a source of your choice would deal damage" prevention; source chosen on resolution, optionally restricted by a `PermanentPredicate` (Circle of Protection cycle) or by sharing a color with the imprinted card (Mourner's Shield). One-shot next-damage shields protect you (`playerSourceNextDamageShields`, Reverse Damage's variant also gains that much life; Deflecting Palm's variant also deals the prevented amount to the chosen source's controller) or any target (`sourceNextDamageToAnyTargetShields`, Sanctum Guardian; Honorable Passage adds a red-source rider that deals the prevented amount to the source's controller); the all-damage scope shields for the whole turn, controller-only (Auriok Replica) or globally (Burrenton Forge-Tender and Mourner's Shield)
- `RedirectTargetCreatureDamageFromChosenSourceToSelfEffect()` — activated ability targeting a creature: all damage a chosen source (picked on resolution) would deal to that creature this turn is dealt to the source permanent instead (Oracle's Attendants). Shield in `GameData.creatureDamageRedirectShields`, checked in both combat and noncombat creature-damage paths via `DamagePreventionService.applyCreatureRedirectShields`; reuses `pendingSourceRedirectDamage`
- `RedirectTargetCreatureDamageFromChosenSourceToTargetEffect()` — SPELL with two creature targets: all damage a chosen source (picked on resolution) would deal to the first target creature this turn is dealt to the distinct second target creature instead (Kor Chant). Reuses `CreatureDamageRedirectShield` with an unlimited amount and the existing source-choice interaction.
- `RedirectTargetCreatureNextDamageFromChosenSourceToControllerEffect()` — activated ability targeting a creature: the next single damage event a chosen source (picked on resolution) would deal to that creature this turn is dealt to the ability's controller (you) instead, then consumed (Jade Monolith). Same `creatureDamageRedirectShields` machinery as Oracle's Attendants, but redirects to a player and only for the next event (`CreatureDamageRedirectShield.NEXT_EVENT`)
- `RedirectNextDamageEffect(RedirectRole protectedRole, RedirectRole destinationRole, DynamicAmount|int amount, TargetPredicate declaredTarget[, PermanentPredicate targetPredicate])` — "the next N damage that would be dealt to X this turn is dealt to Y instead", any source, consumed once N damage has been redirected. `RedirectRole` (`SOURCE_PERMANENT` / `TARGET` / `CONTROLLER` / `ENCHANTED_PERMANENT`, the last being the Aura source's attached permanent) fills both ends. A permanent-protecting instance installs an amount-limited `creatureDamageRedirectShield`; a player-protecting one installs a `PlayerNextDamageRedirectShield` (the player only, not their permanents). `targetSpec()` is harmful iff `destinationRole == TARGET` — that object takes the damage, so CR 702.16b protection applies; `null` means no target. Zhalfirin Crusader `(SOURCE_PERMANENT, TARGET, 1, ANY_TARGET)`, Zealous Inquisitor `(…, CREATURE)`, Personal Incarnation `(SOURCE_PERMANENT, CONTROLLER, 1, NONE)`, Martyrdom `(TARGET, SOURCE_PERMANENT, 1, ANY_TARGET)`, Hazduhr the Abbot / Daughter of Autumn `(TARGET, SOURCE_PERMANENT, XValue()/Fixed(1), CREATURE, whiteCreature)`, Vassal's Duty `(TARGET, CONTROLLER, Fixed(1), CREATURE)`, Ward of Piety `(ENCHANTED_PERMANENT, TARGET, 1, ANY_TARGET)`
- `RedirectNextInstantOrSorceryDamageToControllerEffect()` — activated ability: "the next time an instant or sorcery spell would deal damage to you this turn, that spell deals that damage to its controller instead" (Aegis of Honor). Installs one player shield per activation; only direct damage from an instant/sorcery spell matches, so combat and activated/triggered ability damage are unaffected. The shield is consumed on the next matching damage event and cleared at turn cleanup.
- `RedirectYourDamageToEnchantedCreatureThisTurnEffect()` — aura ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD (Saving Grace): "all damage this turn to you and permanents you control is dealt to enchanted creature instead." Installs a turn-scoped `TurnDamageRedirectToCreatureShield(controller, enchantedCreatureId)`; applied at all four damage paths via `DamagePreventionService.applyTurnDamageRedirectToCreature` (any source, unlimited, reuses `pendingSourceRedirectDamage`). Destination locked in at resolution; skipped while it's not a creature on the battlefield or when the damaged permanent IS the destination. Pair with `StaticBoostEffect(0, 3, ENCHANTED_CREATURE)`
- `DoubleCombatDamageToCreaturesThisTurnEffect()` — one-shot SPELL: for the rest of the turn, a creature dealing combat damage to a creature deals double instead (damage to players untouched). Increments `GameData.combatDamageToCreaturesDoublingsThisTurn`, applied in `GameQueryService.applyCombatDamageMultiplier` when the recipient is a creature; cleared at turn cleanup. Blind Fury = this + `RemoveKeywordEffect(TRAMPLE, ALL_CREATURES)`
- `DoubleControllerDamageThisTurnEffect()` — one-shot SPELL: for the rest of the turn, sources the controller controls deal double damage (combat + noncombat). Increments `GameData.controllerDamageDoublingsThisTurn` for the caster, applied in `GameQueryService.getControllerDamageMultiplier`; cleared at turn cleanup. Insult = this + `DamageCantBePreventedThisTurnEffect()`
- `DoubleDamageFromTargetPermanentThisTurnEffect()` — one-shot targeted SPELL: for the rest of the turn, the targeted permanent deals double damage (combat + noncombat). Keyed by permanent identity in `GameData.permanentDamageDoublingsThisTurn`, so multiple Overblazes stack and damage from other permanents is unaffected
- `RegisterOpponentGraveyardLifeLossThisTurnEffect()` — one-shot activated-ability effect: for the rest of the turn, whenever a card is put into an opponent's graveyard **from anywhere**, that player loses 1 life (Duskmantle Guildmage). Adds an `OpponentGraveyardLifeLossWatcher(controllerId, sourceCard)` to `GameData.opponentGraveyardLifeLossWatchers`; `GraveyardService.addCardToGraveyard` pushes one `LoseLifeEffect(1, TARGET_PLAYER)` trigger per watcher whose controller is an opponent of the graveyard owner. Each activation adds its own watcher (they stack) and the watcher outlives the source permanent; cleared at turn cleanup
- `RegisterLifeGainOpponentLifeLossThisTurnEffect()` — one-shot activated-ability effect: for the rest of the turn, whenever you gain life, each opponent loses that much life (Vizkopa Guildmage). Adds a `LifeGainOpponentLifeLossWatcher(controllerId, sourceCard)` to `GameData.lifeGainOpponentLifeLossWatchers`; `TriggerCollectionService.checkLifeGainTriggers` pushes one `LoseLifeEffect(EventValue(), EACH_OPPONENT)` trigger per watcher whose controller gained the life. Each activation adds its own watcher (they stack) and the watcher outlives the source permanent; cleared at turn cleanup
- `RegisterGlobalTriggeredAbilityUntilEndOfTurnEffect(EffectSlot, CardEffect)` — registers a turn-scoped global trigger whose source is a resolving spell rather than a permanent. The trigger collector currently supports `ON_ANY_CREATURE_DEALT_DAMAGE` and `ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES`, stamps the affected creature or its controller as a non-targeting target, and stamps the event value. Stored in `GameData.temporaryGlobalTriggeredAbilities`, copied for simulation, and cleared at turn cleanup. Shriveling Rot
- `DoubleDamageEffect()` — double all damage (static)
- `DoubleDamageToEnchantedPlayerEffect()` — double damage dealt to enchanted player (static Curse)
- `DoubleControllerDamageEffect(StackEntryPredicate, boolean)` — double controller's damage
- `DoubleDamageFromChosenSubtypeEffect()` — STATIC: double damage from sources carrying the subtype
  chosen for this permanent as it entered the battlefield; stacks multiplicatively with other damage
  multipliers and applies to combat and noncombat damage
- `AdditionalDamageToPlayersFromColorSourcesEffect(Set<CardColor>, int)` — STATIC: a source of any of those colours deals that much damage plus N to a **player** instead (Tok-Tok, Volcano Born = RED, 1). Every player, any controller, combat and noncombat alike; damage to permanents unaffected; stacks additively; only when damage > 0. Queried by `GameQueryService.getDamageToPlayerColorSourceBonus` from `DamageSupport.dealDamageToPlayer` and `CombatDamageService`
- `AdditionalControllerDamageEffect(int, StackEntryPredicate)` — STATIC: matching controller sources deal that much damage plus N instead (Pyromancer's Gauntlet). Filter e.g. AnyOf[AllOf(TypeIn(INSTANT,SORCERY), ColorIn(RED)), AllOf(CardTypeIn(PLANESWALKER), ColorIn(RED))]; combat unaffected; stacks additively; only when damage > 0. Queried by `GameQueryService.getControllerDamageBonus` from `applyDamageMultiplier(GameData, int, StackEntry)`
- `AdditionalColorSourceDamageEffect(int, CardColor)` — STATIC: any *other* source of that color you control deals that much damage plus N instead (Embermaw Hellion).
