- `ChooseCardNameRevealTopCardEffect()` — controller names a card, then reveals the top card of their library; if it matches, the source sacrifice and `DrawCardEffect(3)` are spliced into the paused resolution, leaving the revealed card on top until those draws resolve (Diviner's Lockbox)
- `ChooseCreatureNameRevealTopCardsToHandRestToGraveyardEffect(int count)` — controller names a **creature** card (only creature card names in the game are offered), reveals the top `count` cards of their library; every revealed card with that name goes to hand, the rest to the graveyard (Wood Sage = 4, `{T}` activated ability)
- `ChooseNameExileTopRevealUntilNamedToHandEffect(int topExileCount[, int lifeLossPerExiled])` — controller names a card, exiles top N of their library, then reveals until the named card (to hand; other revealed cards exiled). If never found, remaining library is exiled (Demonic Consultation = 6; Spoils of the Vault = 0, 1 life per exiled card)
- `ChooseNameRevealRandomCardFromHandDealDamageEffect(damage)` — controller names a card, then reveals a card at random from **their own** hand; on a name match the source deals `damage` to any target (Cursed Scroll with `2`; empty hand = no reveal, no damage)
- `ChooseNameRevealRandomCardsFromTargetHandDiscardMatchingEffect(DynamicAmount)` — controller chooses a card name, target player reveals up to X distinct cards at random from their hand, and that player discards the revealed cards with the chosen name (Nebuchadnezzar)
- `TargetPlayerNameCardRevealTopEffect(damageOnMiss)` — target player names a card, then reveals their top library card; match → their hand, mismatch → their graveyard + source deals `damageOnMiss` damage to them (`0` = no damage) (Vexing Arcanix with `2`; targets a player)
- `DestroyTargetWallDealManaValueDamageToControllerEffect()` — destroy target Wall (can't be regenerated), then deal its mana value as damage to its controller (snapshotted pre-destruction). `targetSpec()` narrows to a WALL permanent (Word of Blasting)
- `DestroyTargetArtifactDealManaValueDamageToSourceEffect()` — destroy target artifact (regenerable), then that artifact deals damage equal to its mana value to the ability's source permanent. The artifact is the damage source (CR 608.2h last known information); damage happens even if destruction failed. `targetSpec()` narrows to an ARTIFACT permanent (Goblin Tinkerer)

## Exile

- Delayed TARGET `FlickerEffect` also supports a multi-target group (`target(filter, 0, 99)`): all legal targets are exiled together and return at the requested step, grouped by owner.
- `ExileTargetPermanentEffect()` or `(boolean returnEndStep)` — exile target
- `ExileTargetAndAttachedMatchingEffect(PermanentPredicate attachedFilter)` — exile target creature(s) and matching attached permanents, exiling all matching attachments first. Soul Nova uses `PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(EQUIPMENT)`; Silence the Believers uses `PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(AURA)`
- `ExileTargetPermanentThenEffect(CardEffect thenEffect, ThenEffectRecipient recipient[, PermanentPredicate thenCondition])` — exile sibling of `DestroyTargetPermanentThenEffect`: exile target, then resolve `thenEffect` via its own handler against the chosen recipient (the exiled permanent's controller is snapshotted pre-exile for `TARGET_CONTROLLER`). Optional `thenCondition` gates the then-effect on a predicate evaluated against the target before exile. Path to Exile = `(SearchLibraryEffect(basicLand, BATTLEFIELD_TAPPED), TARGET_CONTROLLER)`; Gideon's Defeat = `(GainLifeEffect(5), CONTROLLER, PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(GIDEON))`
- `ReturnTargetPermanentToHandThenEffect(CardEffect thenEffect, ThenEffectRecipient recipient[, Condition thenCondition])` — bounce sibling of the then-family: bounce target, then resolve `thenEffect`. Optional `thenCondition` is checked **after** the bounce against the original caster (Compelling Deterrence = `(DiscardEffect(1, CONTROLLER), TARGET_OWNER, ControlsPermanent(Zombie))`)
- `FlickerEffect.exileTargetReturnAtEndStep([boolean tapped])` — exile target + return at end step (SELF: `exileSelfReturnAtEndStep()`; mass: `exilePlayersPermanentsReturnAtStep(PermanentPredicate, TurnStep)`; your own, no target, hasted return: `exileControllersPermanentsReturnAtStepWithHaste(PermanentPredicate, TurnStep)` [Legion's Initiative]; immediate: `flickerTarget()` / `flickerTargetUnderYourControl()` [returns under effect controller, Restoration Angel] / `flickerSelfUnderYourControl()` [SELF immediate under controller, Deadeye Navigator] / `flickerTargetWithCounters(int)` / `flickerTargetWithBonus(CardSubtype, CardEffect)` / `flickerTargetWithBonusCounters(CardSubtype, int)` [subtype-gated +1/+1 counters, Essence Flux]). An immediate TARGET flicker also works bound to a multi-target group (`target(filter, 2, 2)`, Ghostly Flicker) — all chosen permanents are exiled before any returns
- `ExileGraveyardCardsEffect(GraveyardExileScope.TARGET_PLAYER_ENTIRE)` — exile target player's whole graveyard (also: `DYING_CREATURE_CONTROLLER` for a delayed death trigger such as Burn Away, `OWN`, `OWN_ALL_MATCHING`, `TARGET_CARDS_ANY_GRAVEYARD` [+`CardTypePredicate`], `TARGET_CARDS_OPPONENT_GRAVEYARD`, `TARGET_PLAYER_ALL_MATCHING` [+filter, records the exiled count on `eventValue`], `ALL_PLAYERS`, `ALL_OPPONENTS`, `EACH_OPPONENT_KEEP` [count = cards kept, Watchers of the Dead]). `exactTargetedFromAnyGraveyard(count, filter, trackWithSource)` is the exact, source-tracked variant used by Spellweaver Helix
- `ExileBottomCardOfTargetPlayerGraveyardEffect()` — exile the bottom (oldest) card of a target player's graveyard; an empty graveyard is a no-op (Phyrexian Furnace)
- `ExileGraveyardCardCreateTokenIfCreatureEffect([CardPredicate filter])` — exile target card from any graveyard; if it was a creature card, create a 2/2 black Zombie (Deluge of the Dead). A non-null `filter` narrows the graveyard target, as on Boneclad Necromancer's optional "target creature card" ETB.
- `BattleDefeatedExileAndCastTransformedEffect()` — Siege defeat intrinsic: exile this battle, then cast it transformed without paying mana (queued by `BattleDefeatSupport` when last defense counter is removed)
- `ExileAllCreaturesEffect()` — exile all creatures
- `ExileAllPermanentsEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — exile matching permanents (use `PermanentTruePredicate` for a literal "exile all permanents", Worldfire)
- `ExileOtherSpellsAndCounterAbilitiesEffect()` — exile all other stack spells + counter all abilities (Summary Dismissal; see Counter spells)
- `ExileAllPermanentsUntilSourceLeavesEffect(PermanentPredicate, boolean returnTapped)` — O-ring style mass exile: exile all matching permanents until the source leaves, then return each under its owner's control (tapped iff `returnTapped`). Realm Razer = `(new PermanentIsLandPredicate(), true)`
- `ExileDamageSourcePermanentUntilSourceLeavesEffect(PermanentPredicate filter, boolean combatOnly[, Condition intervening])` — "Whenever a creature deals combat damage to you, exile that creature until this leaves the battlefield" (Hixus, Prison Warden). `ON_ANY_PERMANENT_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_YOU` slot; queues a non-targeting trigger that resolves `ExileTargetPermanentUntilSourceLeavesEffect` on the damage source
- `ExileTriggeringCreatureUntilSourceLeavesEffect(int minimumOtherCreatures)` — non-targeting global creature-entry trigger: binds the entering permanent by identity, checks the other-creature count when the trigger is created and resolves, exiles it, and returns it under its owner's control when the source leaves. Portcullis = `(2)`
- `ExileTargetPermanentAndAllWithSameNameUntilSourceLeavesEffect()` - O-ring exile of target nonland permanent + every other same-name permanent; returns when source leaves. Detention Sphere. Card filter adds "not named Detention Sphere"; wrap in `MayEffect` for "you may"
- `ExileTargetCreaturesUntilSourceLeavesEffect(int maxTargets, boolean returnToHand)` — "exile up to N other target creatures from the battlefield and/or creature cards from graveyards"; returns them when the source leaves (to hand iff `returnToHand`). One mixed-zone selection across both pools. Angel of Serenity = `(3, true)`
- `PutAllPermanentsOnBottomOfLibraryEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — put all matching permanents on the bottom of their owners' libraries (Hallowed Burial, `new PermanentIsCreaturePredicate()`)
- `PermanentAuctionEffect()` — SPELL: exile all nontoken permanents, then players take turns (controller first) claiming one exiled card each onto the battlefield tapped until the pool empties (Thieves' Auction)
- `IllicitAuctionEffect()` — SPELL (targets a creature): each player may bid life for control of target creature; controller opens at 0, players top the high bid in turn order, high bidder loses that much life (a life loss — can exceed their life total) and gains control indefinitely (Illicit Auction)
- `ExileTargetPermanentAndTrackWithSourceEffect()` — exile + track exiled card with source permanent (cards "exiled with" it)
- `ExileTopCardsToSourceEffect(int[, boolean faceDown[, boolean toGraveyardOnControlLoss]][, LibraryScope scope])` — exile top N of one or more libraries, tracked with the source permanent; fizzles if the source has left the battlefield. `scope` = `CONTROLLER` (default) / `TARGET_PLAYER` (targeted player, Mindreaver) / `TARGET_OPPONENT` (Grimoire Thief, Nightveil Specter — two-player resolves against the single opponent, no target choice) / `EACH_PLAYER` (Knowledge Pool). Only `TARGET_OPPONENT` returns a `CombatDamageTriggerContextEffect` context (`DAMAGED_PLAYER`). `toGraveyardOnControlLoss` on the first arms the SBA control-loss watch that dumps the whole pile into its owner's graveyard when the source changes controller or leaves the battlefield (Duplicity)
- `SharedFateDrawReplacementEffect()` — STATIC draw replacement: the drawer exiles the top card of an opponent's library face down, tracked with the source permanent and the exiling player; the exiling player may look at and play/cast those cards while the source remains on the battlefield (Shared Fate)
- `ExileTopCardsOfTargetOpponentCreateTokenPerChosenColorEffect(DynamicAmount count, CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` — player-targeting spec; on resolution the controller chooses a colour (`ChoiceContext.ExileTopCardsChosenColorTokensChoice`), the target opponent exiles the top `count` cards of their library, and the controller creates one `tokenTemplate` token per exiled card of that colour (lands excluded, per printed colours). Use `new XValue()` for an `{X}...` cost (Oona, Queen of the Fae)
- `CreateTokenPerPermanentOfChosenColorEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` — on resolution the controller chooses a colour (`ChoiceContext.CreateTokensPerPermanentOfChosenColorChoice`), then creates one `tokenTemplate` token per permanent of that colour on the battlefield (any controller, lands excluded per effective colours). No target; template `amount` ignored. Wrap in `MayPayManaEffect` for a "you may pay" gate (Rith, the Awakener)
- `SearchLibraryForCardsToExileWithSourceEffect(CardPredicate filter)` — ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD: search library for any number of matching cards, exile each tracked with the source, then shuffle (Endless Horizons, PLAINS). Pair with `PutCardExiledWithSourceIntoHandEffect`
- `SearchLibraryForCardsToExileFaceDownPileEffect(int count)` — ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD: search library for N cards, exile them face down as a pile tracked with the source, shuffle the pile, then shuffle the library (Mangara's Tome, 5)
- `RegisterNextDrawFromExiledPileReplacementEffect()` — activated ability: the next draw this turn is instead the top card of the source's exiled pile, put into its owner's hand (Mangara's Tome)
- `PutCardExiledWithSourceIntoHandEffect()` — put one card the controller owns exiled with the source into hand (chooses if several); wrap in `MayEffect`. Endless Horizons upkeep, Gustha's Scepter `{T}` ability
- `PutAllCardsExiledWithSourceIntoOwnersHandsEffect()` — put every card exiled with the source permanent into its owner's hand. Bag of Holding's sacrifice ability.
- `ExileCardFromHandFaceDownWithSourceEffect(boolean toGraveyardOnControlLoss)` — controller exiles a chosen hand card face down tracked with the source (only its controller may look at it). The flag arms the SBA control-loss watch that dumps every card exiled with the source into its owner's graveyard on control change or when it leaves the battlefield (Gustha's Scepter)
- `ExileHandFaceDownThenReturnCardsExiledWithSourceEffect()` — exile the controller's **whole** hand face down tracked with the source, then put every *other* card they own exiled with that source into their hand (the two piles swap). An empty hand still counts as "if you do", so the return half always happens. Wrap in `MayEffect` for "you may" (Duplicity's upkeep trigger)
- `ExileEachPlayerHandFaceDownAndReturnAtNextEndStepEffect()` — exile every player's hand face down without source tracking, remember each exiled card, and queue a delayed triggered ability for the next end step. The delayed `DiscardEachPlayerHandAndReturnExiledCardsEffect` makes each player discard their current hand, then returns only that player's remembered cards. Pair with `EachPlayerDrawsCardEffect(7)` for Memory Jar.
- `ReturnCardExiledWithSourceToBattlefieldEffect()` — battlefield sibling of the above: return one card exiled with the source to the battlefield under the **ability controller's** control (CR 110.2a), choosing if several. Purgatory upkeep, wrapped in `MayPayManaEffect("{4}", 2, …)`
- `ReturnExiledCardToBattlefieldUnderOwnerControlEffect(UUID exiledCardId)` — returns one specific exiled card to the battlefield under its **owner's** control. Authored with `null` on a token blueprint's `ON_DEATH` slot; `CreateTokenEffectHandler` bakes in the id of the card that created the token (Tatsumasa, the Dragon's Fang — exiled to pay its own activation cost, returned when the Dragon Spirit token dies). Does nothing if that card already left exile
- `ReturnAllCardsExiledWithSourceEffect([boolean underControllerControl][, CardPredicate filter][, boolean turnFaceUp])` — return matching cards exiled with the source to the battlefield; `false`/no-arg = owners' control (Helvault ON_DEATH, Endless Sands sac), `true` = ability controller's control (Cold Storage sac), and `turnFaceUp` reveals the whole source-tracked pile before returning matches (Pyxis of Pandemonium)
- `ReturnEnchantedCreatureToOwnerHandOnDeathEffect()` — aura `ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD` trigger: when the enchanted creature dies, return it to its owner's hand (Demonic Vigor)
- `ReturnEnchantedCreatureToBattlefieldOnDeathEffect(boolean underAuraControllersControl)` — aura `ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD` trigger: when the enchanted creature dies, return it to the battlefield under its owner's control (Abduction, no-arg form) or under the Aura controller's control (Unhallowed Pact, False Demise, `true`), which tracks the returned permanent as a stolen creature so the control change persists
- `ReturnEnchantedCreatureAndReattachAuraOnDeathEffect()` — aura death trigger: return the enchanted creature under its owner's control, then at the next end step return the source Aura under its owner's control attached to that same returned permanent (Gift of Immortality). The delayed return is skipped if the creature or Aura card has left its expected zone.
- `ReturnTargetCardFromExileToHandEffect(CardPredicate, boolean ownedOnly)` — exile to hand
- `ExilePermanentsInsteadOfGraveyardEffect()` — static replacement: permanents are exiled instead of entering a graveyard. Applies only to permanents leaving the battlefield, not to permanent cards in other zones. Samurai of the Pale Curtain

## Tokens

- `EachOpponentCreatesTokenEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` - each opponent of the ability's controller creates the wrapped token under their own control; the token's dynamic amount is re-evaluated per creating opponent (Akroan Horse)

- `CreateTokenOfChosenColorAndSubtypeEffect()` — create one 2/2 creature token using the source permanent's chosen color and chosen creature subtype; the token has no keyword abilities. Used by Volrath's Laboratory after `ChooseColorOnEnterEffect()` and `ChooseSubtypeOnEnterEffect()`

- `CreateTokenEffect(...)` — create tokens (many constructors, see EFFECTS_INDEX.md). The count is a `DynamicAmount` (`int` ctors are `Fixed` sugar): any "create a token for each …" or "create X tokens" = this effect + an amount (`XValue`, `PermanentCount`, `CardsInGraveyard`, `CountersOnSource`, `AttachmentsOnSource`, `OpponentPoisonCounters`, `CreatureDeathsThisTurn`, `ColorManaSymbolsAmongControlledPermanents`, `Divided`, …) — never a new effect class
- `CreateTokenBlockingCombatOpponentEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` — create the wrapped token(s) under the ability controller's control and record them as blocking the attacking creature carried by a combat trigger; use for "create a token that's blocking that creature" (Brimaz, King of Oreskos)
- `CreateTokenWithSourceCounterPTEffect(CounterType, CreateTokenEffect)` — creates the supplied token blueprint and gives it a characteristic-defining ability making its power and toughness equal to the given counter type on the specific permanent that created it; records the token under that source for later source-leaves cleanup (Saproling Burst)
- `CreateTokenEffect.whiteSpirit(int)` — 1/1 white Spirit creature token with flying
- `CreateTokenEffect.blueSpirit(int)` — 1/1 blue Spirit creature token with flying
- `CreateTokenEffect.blackZombie(int)` — 2/2 black Zombie creature token
- `CreateTokenEffect.blackZombieWithDecayed(int)` — 2/2 black Zombie with decayed (can't block; sacrifice at end of combat when it attacks)
- `CreateTokenEffect.whiteSoldier(int)` / `.whiteSoldier(DynamicAmount)` — 1/1 white Soldier creature token(s); `DynamicAmount` overload for "create X … Soldier tokens" (`new XValue()`, Martial Coup)
- `CreateTokenEffect.ofTreasureToken(int)` — treasure tokens
- `CreateTokenEffect.ofClueToken(int)` — clue tokens (investigate)
- `CreateTokenEffect.ofBloodToken(int)` — blood tokens (`{1}, {T}, Discard a card, Sacrifice this token: Draw a card.`)
- `PayXManaCreateXTokensEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` — resolution-time "you may pay {X}. If you do, create X [tokens]": prompts X (≤ available mana), pays it, creates X copies of `token` (X=0 = decline). `token`'s own amount is ignored. Rise of the Hobgoblins. Use this, NOT `MayPayManaEffect("{X}", …)`, which can't pay/plumb `{X}` at resolution
- `PayXManaSearchLibraryEffect(CardPredicate filter)` — resolution-time optional pay-X tutor to the battlefield: the controller may decline, or chooses X, pays generic mana, then searches for one matching card with mana value X or less. Choosing X=0 still searches; Taj-Nar Swordsmith uses an Equipment subtype filter.
- `PayXManaReanimateCreatureWithManaValueXEffect(CounterType enterWithCounter, boolean exileIfLeavesBattlefield)` — resolution-time "you may pay {X}. When you do, return target creature card with mana value X from your graveyard to the battlefield" (Isareth the Awakener). Prompts/pays X, then puts a reflexive `TRIGGERED_ABILITY` entry on the stack (targetZone `GRAVEYARD`) carrying a pre-targeted `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect`; a single legal card is targeted with no prompt, several open a `GraveyardChoice`. `enterWithCounter` (Isareth's corpse counter) and `exileIfLeavesBattlefield` ride along on the normal graveyard-return path
- `PayXManaDealXDamageToAnyTargetEffect(String manaCost)` — "you may pay `manaCost` (contains {X}). If you do, it deals X damage to any target". Target chosen when the ability goes on the stack (harmful ANY_TARGET → creature/planeswalker/battle/player only); on resolution prompts X capped by potential mana (`ManaCost.calculateMaxX` on virtual pool — tap lands during the prompt), pays `manaCost`, deals X damage (X=0 = decline). Flameblast Dragon (`"{X}{R}"`, ON_ATTACK). Use this, NOT `MayPayManaEffect("{X}…", …)`
- `EnchantedControllerMayPayToPreventDamageEffect(int amount)` — `ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` (Power Leak): the enchanted permanent's controller (stack `targetId`) may pay any amount of mana to prevent that much of `amount` damage dealt to them at their upkeep. Prompts that player for X (`XValueChoice`, capped at `amount`), pays generic mana, deals `amount dz??dz? X` through the damage system. No mana ⇒ full `amount`, no prompt. `DamageDealingEffect` (players only)
- `CreateTokenWithDyingSourceCountersEffect(CreateTokenEffect template)` — `ON_DEATH`: if the dying creature had dz?A1 +1/+1 counter, create `template` with that many +1/+1 counters (e.g. Ambitious Augmenter's Fractal)
- `CreateTokensForEachDyingSourceCounterEffect(CreateTokenEffect template)` — `ON_DEATH`: "create one `template` token for each counter on it." The death collector snapshots the dying creature's total counter count (every concrete counter type) and creates that many copies of `template` (e.g. Kinsbaile Borderguard's 1/1 white Kithkin Soldier). Use `(CounterType, template)` when the wording names a specific counter type (e.g. Chasm Skulker's +1/+1 counters); the one-argument form keeps the all-counter behavior
- `MoveDyingSourceCountersToTargetCreatureEffect()` — `ON_DEATH`: if the dying creature had dz?A1 counter (any type), move all of its counters onto up to one target creature (e.g. Scolding Administrator). Intervening-if snapshots the counters at death; targets any creature
- `DrawCardForEachDyingSourceCounterEffect(CounterType counterType)` — `ON_DEATH`: "draw a card for each `counterType` counter on it." Snapshots the dying creature's count of that type at death and draws that many (e.g. Dusk Urchins, MINUS_ONE_MINUS_ONE)
- `PutCounterOnTargetForEachDyingSourceCounterEffect(CounterType counterType[, boolean optional])` — `ON_DEATH`: "put a `counterType` counter on target creature for each `counterType` counter on it." Snapshots the dying creature's count at death and puts that many on a targeted creature (any creature). `optional=true` is the "you may" form (Soulstinger) — the collector wraps the snapshotted effect in a `MayEffect` (target still chosen as the trigger stacks, may decline at resolution); the `(CounterType, boolean, PermanentPredicate)` overload narrows the target structurally (e.g. artifact creature). Default mandatory (Grief Tyrant). MINUS_ONE_MINUS_ONE
- `DistributeCountersAmongCreaturesOnDeathEffect.fromDyingSourceCountersAmongControlledCreatures(CounterType)` — `ON_DEATH`: "you may distribute a number of `counterType` counters equal to the number on this creature among any number of creatures you control" (Vastwood Hydra). Does not target; collector snapshots the count and wraps in `MayEffect`; division rides on `pendingETBDamageAssignments` at resolution (only controlled creatures)
- `DistributeCountersAmongCreaturesOnDeathEffect.fixedAmongAnyCreatures(CounterType, int total)` — `ON_DEATH`: "you may distribute N `counterType` counters among any number of target creatures" (Jugan, the Rising Star — five +1/+1). Same resolution-time division as the Vastwood Hydra form (`pendingETBDamageAssignments`, wrapped in `MayEffect` by the death collector), but the total is printed rather than snapshotted and ANY creature on the battlefield may receive counters, including an opponent's
- `MoveCounterFromTargetCreatureToTargetCreatureEffect(boolean moveAll)` / `(CounterType)` — move counters from the first target creature onto the second (reads flat multi-target positions 0/1). `moveAll=false` (also `()`) moves one counter of the first kind present; `moveAll=true` moves every counter of every kind; the `(CounterType)` constructor is the "any number of [type] counters" variant that prompts the controller for how many (0..the count present) via `ChoiceContext.MoveCountersAmountChoice`. No-op if the first creature has no counters or either target is gone. As an activated ability pair with the multi-target `ActivatedAbility` constructor + two creature filters (Leech Bonder `{U}, {Q}`); as a spell use two `target(creatureFilter)` groups (Fate Transfer, `moveAll=true`; Bioshift, `(PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE)` + `setMultiTargetConstraint(CONTROLLED_BY_FIRST_TARGET)`)
- `MoveCounterFromSourceToTargetCreatureEffect(CounterType)` — move one counter of that type from the SOURCE permanent onto target creature (Afiya Grove's upkeep trigger). No-op if the source left the battlefield or has no such counter; placement goes through `placeCounterOnPermanent` so put-counter triggers fire
- `MoveCounterFromTargetCreatureToSourceEffect(CounterType)` — move one counter of that type from target creature onto the SOURCE permanent (Arcbound Fiend's upkeep trigger). No-op if the source or target left the battlefield or the target has no such counter; placement goes through `placeCounterOnPermanent` so put-counter triggers fire
- For "create a token that gains [keyword] until end of turn", set `CreateTokenEffect`'s `grantedKeywordsUntilEndOfTurn` (e.g. `new CreateTokenEffect(amount, name, p, t, color, colors, subtypes, innateKeywords, Set.of(Keyword.HASTE))` — Artistic Process Elemental gains haste). Distinct from the token's innate `keywords`.
- `CreateXTokenWithXCountersEffect(String tokenName, int power, int toughness, CardColor color, Set<CardColor> colors, List<CardSubtype> subtypes, CounterType counterType)` — create one token with X counters of `counterType` from ability/spell X value (e.g. Berta's Fractal with `PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`)
- `ExileTargetCardFromGraveyardAndCreateTokenCopyEffect(CardPredicate, ownGraveyardOnly, additionalSubtypes, grantHaste, exileAtEndStep[, colorOverride, powerOverride, toughnessOverride])` — exile graveyard target, create token copy with optional extra subtypes/haste/end-step exile; optional Eternalize-style color/P/T overrides (The Scarab God 4/4 black Zombie); full form adds `Set<Keyword> additionalKeywords` (printed on the copy) + `createZombieTokenWithExiledCardStats` (a second, black Zombie token with the exiled card's P/T — Soul Separator), `targetPutIntoGraveyardFromAnywhereThisTurn`, and `exileOtherControlledTokensOfSubtype` (Abyssal Harvester)
- `ExileOwnCreatureFromGraveyardCreateZombieTokenCopyEffect()` — non-targeting resolution-time "you may exile a creature from your graveyard → 4/4 black Zombie token copy with haste until EOT" (God-Pharaoh's Gift; Zombie **replaces** other types)
- `ExileOwnGraveyardCardThenEachOpponentLosesLifeEffect(CardPredicate, int)` — non-targeting resolution-time optional exile of one matching card from your graveyard; if exiled, each opponent loses the fixed life amount (Trystan, Penitent Culler)
- `CreateTokenCopyOfTargetPermanentEffect()` or `(grantHaste, exileAtEndStep)` or `(grantHaste, exileAtEndStep, sacrificeAtEndStep)` or `(additionalSubtypes, additionalTypes, powerOverride, toughnessOverride, Map<CounterType, Integer> initialCounters)` — create token copy of targeted permanent; optional type/subtype/P/T overrides, post-ETB counters, granted haste, and exile (Heat Shimmer) **or** sacrifice (Minion Reflector — dies-triggers fire) at next end step
- `CreateTokenCopyOfTargetCreatureForTargetPlayerEffect()` — target player creates a token copy of target creature you control (two targets: player + creature); Echocasting Symposium
- `CreateTokenCopyOfEachControlledCreatureTokenEffect()` or `(creaturesOnly)` — "For each [creature] token you control, create a token that's a copy of that permanent" (populate-all). Snapshots matching tokens first (new copies aren't copied), respects the token multiplier. No target. Default/`true` = creature tokens only (Rhys the Redeemed); `false` = all tokens (Second Harvest)
- `CreateTokenCopyAndLinkToSourceEffect()` — ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD, targets a nontoken creature: create a token copy of it AND forge a two-way `chosenPermanentId` bond between the source permanent and the token, attaching an `ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD` `RemoveLinkedPermanentEffect(SACRIFICE)` to the token. Dance of Many
- `RemoveLinkedPermanentEffect(Mode)` (`EXILE` / `SACRIFICE`) — ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD and/or ON_SELF_BECOMES_UNTAPPED: removes the permanent linked to the source via `chosenPermanentId`. On the leaves slot a dedicated trigger collector bakes the linked id into the effect (not the entry's `targetId`, which would fizzle against a spell target filter); on the untap slot the id stays null and the handler reads it off the still-present source. Both clear the link (second untap is a no-op) and the handler clears the partner's back-reference before removal to avoid the reciprocal removal bouncing back. Dance of Many (enchantment leaves → `EXILE` token; token leaves → `SACRIFICE` enchantment); Coffin Queen (`EXILE` on both slots, fed by `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect.linkToSource`)
- `DestroyLinkedPermanentEffect(boolean cannotBeRegenerated)` — non-targeting: destroys the permanent linked to the source via `chosenPermanentId`. Put on `ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD` **and/or** `ON_SELF_BECOMES_UNTAPPED`; pair with `GainControlOfTargetEffect.linkingToSource(…)` so the stolen creature is remembered. Resolution clears the link, so a later untap destroys nothing. Merieke Ri Berit

## Life

- `GainLifeEffect(DynamicAmount[, GainLifeRecipient])` or `(int)` — gain life; dynamic derivations via `DynamicAmount` (PermanentCount, CardsInHand, CardsInGraveyard, CountersOnSource, GreatestPowerAmongControlled, XValue, Scaled, Sum, `ColorManaSymbolsInHand`, …). Chroma-from-hand "gain N life for each [color] mana symbol in cards in your hand" = `GainLifeEffect(new Scaled(new ColorManaSymbolsInHand(ManaColor.GREEN), N))` (Phosphorescent Feast, N=2; "reveal any number" modelled as the whole hand). `GainLifeEffect.sacrificeOnly(N)` is the `ON_DEATH` form of "when you sacrifice this, gain N life". `recipient=TARGET_CONTROLLER` gives the life to the target permanent's controller: "its controller gains life = its toughness" = `GainLifeEffect(new TargetToughness(), GainLifeRecipient.TARGET_CONTROLLER)` (Condemn). `TargetPower()` is the power analogue: "you gain life = target's power, then destroy it" = `GainLifeEffect(new TargetPower())` + `DestroyTargetPermanentEffect(false)` (Chastise)
- `TotalToughnessOfControlledCreatures()` — the sum of effective toughness among creatures the
  controller controls; evaluated from the effect's controller at resolution
- `TotalPowerOfControlledCreatures()` — the sum of effective power among creatures the controller
  controls; negative power contributes negatively to the total
- `GainLifeEffect(DynamicAmount[, GainLifeRecipient])` or `(int)` — gain life; dynamic derivations via `DynamicAmount` (PermanentCount, CardsInHand, CardsInGraveyard, CountersOnSource, GreatestPowerAmongControlled, XValue, Scaled, Sum, `ColorManaSymbolsInHand`, …). Chroma-from-hand "gain N life for each [color] mana symbol in cards in your hand" = `GainLifeEffect(new Scaled(new ColorManaSymbolsInHand(ManaColor.GREEN), N))` (Phosphorescent Feast, N=2; "reveal any number" modelled as the whole hand). `recipient=TARGET_CONTROLLER` gives the life to the target permanent's controller: "its controller gains life = its toughness" = `GainLifeEffect(new TargetToughness(), GainLifeRecipient.TARGET_CONTROLLER)` (Condemn). `TargetPower()` is the power analogue: "you gain life = target's power, then destroy it" = `GainLifeEffect(new TargetPower())` + `DestroyTargetPermanentEffect(false)` (Chastise)
- `TargetPlayerGainsLifeEffect(DynamicAmount|int)` — target gains life (`XValue` for "target player gains X life", Stream of Life)
- `DoubleTargetPlayerLifeEffect()` — double target life
- `DoubleEnchantedCreatureControllerLifeEffect()` — Aura `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`: double the enchanted creature controller's life total; the combat trigger context bakes that player as the non-targeting stack-entry context (Celestial Mantle)
- `SetLifeTotalEffect(DynamicAmount amount, SetLifeTotalRecipient recipient)` — the whole "life total becomes" family, clamped at 0. `recipient` = CONTROLLER / TARGET_PLAYER / EACH_PLAYER; the spec targets a player iff `TARGET_PLAYER` (a null target is a no-op — Torgaar's "up to one"). Sugar: `(int, recipient)`, `(DynamicAmount)` and `(int)` (the last two default to CONTROLLER). Non-targeting "your life total becomes N/that number" = `SetLifeTotalEffect(5)` (Form of the Dragon) or `SetLifeTotalEffect(new CardsInLibrary(CountScope.CONTROLLER))` (Invincible Hymn). "target player's life total becomes N" = `SetLifeTotalEffect(10, SetLifeTotalRecipient.TARGET_PLAYER)` (Magister Sphinx, Sorin Markov); "half their starting life total, rounded down" = `GameData.STARTING_LIFE_TOTAL / 2` (Torgaar). **EACH_PLAYER evaluates the amount once per player, with that player as the amount's controller**, and fixes every new total before applying any — so `CountScope.CONTROLLER` reads "they" (Biorhythm `new PermanentCount(new PermanentIsCreaturePredicate(), CountScope.CONTROLLER)`) and a cross-player amount is snapshotted (Arbiter of Knollridge `new HighestLifeTotalAmongPlayers()`). Worldfire is `SetLifeTotalEffect(1, SetLifeTotalRecipient.EACH_PLAYER)`. CR 119.5: applied as gaining/losing the difference, so can't-change-life / can't-gain-life and gain/loss triggers all apply
- `ExchangeTargetPlayersLifeTotalsEffect()` — two target players exchange life totals (Soul Conduit, Axis of Mortality)
- `ExchangeLifeTotalWithCreatureStatEffect(Stat[, Recipient])` — exchange a player's life with source creature's POWER (Evra) or TOUGHNESS (Tree of Redemption / Tree of Perdition). Default `Recipient.CONTROLLER`; `TARGET_PLAYER` + `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT)` for Tree of Perdition. Layer 7b base-stat override
- `PsychicTransferEffect()` — targets a player; if controller's and target's life totals differ by 5 or less, they exchange life totals (Psychic Transfer)
- `LoseLifeEffect(DynamicAmount amount, LoseLifeRecipient recipient, boolean controllerGainsLifeLost)` — the whole life-loss family. `recipient` = CONTROLLER / TARGET_PLAYER / TARGET_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER / DYING_CREATURE_CONTROLLER / DEFENDING_PLAYER / EACH_PLAYER / EACH_OPPONENT; `controllerGainsLifeLost` drains total life lost back to you. `DEFENDING_PLAYER` (combat triggers — Vedalken Ghoul's `ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED`) reads the attacked player/planeswalker-controller from the trigger's `attackedTargetId`, chooses no target, fires "loses life" triggers. `DYING_CREATURE_CONTROLLER` (only for `ON_DAMAGED_CREATURE_DIES` — Touch of Moonglove's "its controller loses 2 life") reads the dying creature's last-known controller, which `GraveyardService` bakes onto the trigger's `targetId`; chooses no target, fires "loses life" triggers. Sugar: `(int)` = `(Fixed, CONTROLLER, false)` (lose N life), `(int, recipient)`, `(DynamicAmount, recipient)`, `(int, recipient, boolean)`. Amount: `EventValue()` for "equal to the life you gained" (Sanguine Bond `(new EventValue(), TARGET_PLAYER)`); `PermanentCount(filter, CONTROLLER)` for "1 life for each … you control" (Bishop); `new XValue()` for Exsanguinate `(new XValue(), EACH_OPPONENT, true)`. the spec targets a player iff `recipient == TARGET_PLAYER`
- "target player loses N life and you gain M life" (fixed drain) — **compose** `LoseLifeEffect(N, LoseLifeRecipient.TARGET_PLAYER)` + `GainLifeEffect(M)` as two effects on one slot; targeting auto-derives from the loss, controller gains the fixed M on resolution. Omit the `GainLifeEffect` for a pure "target player loses N life". The `TARGET_PLAYER` loss does NOT fire "loses life" triggers (pre-existing quirk). Syphon Life, Soul Feast, Bleak Coven Vampires
- `GainLifeEqualToTargetCreatureStatEffect(DynamicAmount amount)` — controller gains life equal to `amount` evaluated against a single target creature (`TargetPower()`, or `Sum(TargetPower(), TargetToughness())` for Predator's Rapport's "power plus its toughness"). Declares its own `TargetPredicates.creature()` spec (unlike `GainLifeEffect(new TargetPower())`, whose target is supplied by a co-effect like Chastise's destroy), so it stands alone as a targeted trigger. `LifeGainEffect`. Wrap in `MayEffect` and give the card a `target(PermanentPredicateTargetFilter(creature + PermanentControlledBySourceControllerPredicate))` for "you may gain life equal to the power of target creature you control" (Wall of Reverence, `CONTROLLER_END_STEP_TRIGGERED`; the target is chosen as the trigger goes on the stack — CR 603.3d — and the may prompt follows at resolution)
- `SpellCastLifeDrainEffect(int lifeLoss, int lifeGain, CardPredicate spellFilter)` — `ON_OPPONENT_CASTS_SPELL` drain: opponent who casts a matching spell loses `lifeLoss`, you gain `lifeGain` (filter null = any). Yawgmoth's Edict
- `SpellCastDamageToCasterEffect(int damage, CardPredicate spellFilter, Condition intervening)` — `ON_OPPONENT_CASTS_SPELL` damage counterpart of `SpellCastLifeDrainEffect`: the source deals `damage` to the opponent who cast a matching spell (filter null = any), through the normal damage system; optional `intervening` = intervening-"if" (CR 603.4), trigger-time + resolution check. Mindsparker; Scab-Clan Berserker (`SourceIsRenowned`)
- `LoseLifeUnlessPaysEffect(int lifeLoss, int payAmount, CardPredicate spellFilter)` — `ON_OPPONENT_CASTS_SPELL` punisher: casting opponent loses `lifeLoss` life unless they pay `{payAmount}` (filter null = any). Can't-pay auto-loses; else may-ability prompt. Isolation Cell (2, 2, CREATURE)
- `DamageUnlessPaysEffect(int damage, int payAmount, CardPredicate spellFilter)` — `ON_OPPONENT_CASTS_SPELL` punisher, **damage** version of `LoseLifeUnlessPaysEffect` (goes through the damage system: preventable, infect, redirect). `DamageDealingEffect`. Can't-pay auto-deals; else may-ability prompt. Soul Barrier (2, 2, CREATURE)
- `EachPlayerTakesDamageUnlessPaysEffect(int damage, String manaCost)` — `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`: for each player (APNAP), deals `damage` unless that player pays `manaCost` (independent choices; can't-pay auto-deals then continues). `DamageDealingEffect`. Lim-DAdz?l's Hex `(1, "{B/3}")` — monocolored hybrid encodes "pay {B} or {3}". Distinct from `ForcedCostOrElseEffect.anyPlayerMayPay` (one shared fallback)
- `DamageTargetPlayerUnlessPaysPerCounterEffect(int damage, CounterType, String costPerCounter)` — deals `damage` to the stack entry's `targetId` player unless they pay `costPerCounter` × counters on the source. Pair with `OPPONENT_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` ("at the beginning of the chosen player's upkeep"). Zero counters = no prompt. Energy Vortex `(3, VORTEX, "{1}")`. `DamageDealingEffect`
- `DealDamageToEachDestroyedPermanentControllerEffect()` — `DestroyEachTargetPermanentEffect` rider: "deals damage to each player equal to the number of permanents they controlled that were put into a graveyard this way". Per-player amount (not one shared total like `MassDamageEffect(EventValue(), true)`); reads the controller tally off `StackEntry.eventPlayerIds`. `DamageDealingEffect`. Builder's Bane
- `DamageEachDestroyedPermanentControllerUnlessPaysEffect(int damage, String manaCost)` — `DestroyAllPermanentsEffect` rider: "for each permanent destroyed this way, deals `damage` to that permanent's controller unless they pay `manaCost`". One payment **per destroyed permanent** (not per player), grouped APNAP; reads the destroyed permanents' controllers off `StackEntry.eventPlayerIds`. `DamageDealingEffect`. Stench of Evil `(1, "{2}")`
- `EachDestroyedPermanentControllerGainsLifeEqualToManaValueEffect()` — `DestroyAllPermanentsEffect` rider: "The controller of each of those permanents gains life equal to its mana value." One life gain **per destroyed permanent** (a player who lost three artifacts gains the sum), reading `StackEntry.eventPlayerIds` paired positionally with the last-known mana values on `StackEntry.eventManaValues`; indestructible/regenerated permanents grant nothing. Seeds of Innocence
- `DrawCardUnlessPaysEffect(int drawCount, int payAmount, CardPredicate spellFilter)` — `ON_OPPONENT_CASTS_SPELL` Rhystic draw (Mystic Remora `(1, 4, noncreature)`, Rhystic Study `(1, 1, null)`). `CardDrawingEffect`. Opponent chooses pay first; if they don't/can't, source controller may draw. Convenience ctor `(drawCount, payAmount)` |
- `RegisterLoseLifeAtNextDrawStepUnlessPaysEffect(int lifeLoss, int payAmount)` — `ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER` trigger (Nafs Asp, `(1, 1)`): schedules a `LoseLifeAtNextDrawStepUnlessPays` delayed action on the damaged player (baked in as `targetId` via `DAMAGED_PLAYER`); at the beginning of that player's next draw step `StepTriggerService` drains it into a `ForcedCostOrElseEffect` pay-or-lose-life may-prompt (pay `{payAmount}` → no loss, else lose `lifeLoss`). Life loss, not damage — won't re-trigger
- `RegisterPoisonAtNextUpkeepUnlessPaysEffect(int amount, String manaCost)` — `ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER` trigger (Sabertooth Cobra, `(1, "{2}")`): poison sibling of `RegisterLoseLifeAtNextDrawStepUnlessPaysEffect`. Schedules a `PoisonAtNextUpkeepUnlessPays` delayed action on the damaged player (baked in as `targetId` via `DAMAGED_PLAYER`); at the beginning of that player's next **upkeep** `StepTriggerService` drains it into a `ForcedCostOrElseEffect` pay-or-poison may-prompt (pay `manaCost` → no counter, else `GivePoisonCountersEffect(amount, CONTROLLER)`). Pair with `GivePoisonCountersEffect(1, TARGET_PLAYER)` in the same slot for the immediate counter
- `TormentOfHailfireEffect(int lifeLoss)` / `.once(int lifeLoss)` / `(int lifeLoss, Integer fixedIterations)` — three-way punisher: "Repeat N times. Each opponent loses lifeLoss life unless they sacrifice a nonland permanent or discard a card." Compact ctor: N = xValue (Torment of Hailfire). `.once(N)` / non-null `fixedIterations`: fixed pass count (Nicol Bolas, the Deceiver +3 = `.once(3)`). Per opponent per iteration (APNAP), a pruned `ColorChoice` list-pick (lose life inline / sacrifice a nonland permanent / discard a card); life is always offered. One-opponent-at-a-time loop over `GameData.torment`, re-running via `rerunCurrentEffectAfterInteraction`. No target
- `ForbiddenRitualEffect(int lifeLoss)` — SPELL, target opponent: sacrifice a nontoken permanent; if you do, that opponent loses N life unless they sacrifice any permanent or discard; you may repeat (same target). Progress on `GameData.forbiddenRitual`; repeat via `PendingInteraction.ForbiddenRitualRepeatChoice`; opponent pick is `ChoiceContext.ForbiddenRitualPenaltyChoice` (lands OK). Forbidden Ritual (`2`)
- `KillingWaveEffect()` — SPELL: "For each creature, its controller sacrifices it unless they pay X life." X = xValue. APNAP multi-select of creatures to keep (pay X each, capped by `life/X`); then simultaneous pay + sacrifice of non-kept. X=0 keeps all with no prompt. `MultiPermanentChoiceContext.KillingWaveKeep`. Killing Wave
- `WintersChillEffect()` — SPELL, X-scaled attacking-creature targets: for each, its controller may pay {1} or {2}. Pay nothing → `DESTROY_AT_END_OF_COMBAT`; pay only {1} → combat-damage prevention to and by; pay {2} → no further effect. Pair with `COMBAT_BEFORE_BLOCKERS` + `setXValueCap(PermanentCount(AllOf(IsLand, HasSupertype(SNOW)), CONTROLLER))` + `targetX(...)`. Driven over `GameData.wintersChill` / `ChoiceContext.WintersChillPaymentChoice`
- `PlayersCantGainLifeEffect()` — can't gain life (static)
- `OpponentsCantGainLifeEffect()` — opponents of this permanent's controller can't gain life (static, global query checks each effect relative to the affected player; Erebos, God of the Dead)
- `PlayersCantGainLifeThisTurnEffect()` — one-shot SPELL: no player can gain life for the rest of the turn (Skullcrack). Sets `GameData.playersCantGainLifeThisTurn`, read by `GameQueryService.canPlayerGainLife`, cleared at turn cleanup
- `PlayersCantPayLifeOrSacrificeCreaturesEffect()` — can't pay life or sacrifice creatures to cast spells or activate abilities (static)
- `OpponentEffectsCantCauseSacrificeEffect()` — opponents' spells and abilities can't cause you to sacrifice permanents (static, Sigarda, Host of Herons)
- `CountersCantBePlacedEffect()` — STATIC global (Solemnity): players can't get counters and counters can't be put on artifacts/creatures/enchantments/lands (planeswalkers keep loyalty). Queried at `GameQueryService.cantHaveCounters` + `canPlayerGetPoisonCounters`
- `CantBecomeUntappedEffect()` — STATIC: the permanent can't become untapped. Checked by the untap-step gate, untap effects, tap-or-untap effects, and untap-cost validation; grant to an Aura's host with `GrantEffectEffect(..., GrantScope.ENCHANTED_CREATURE)`
- `RemoveAllCountersAndLockPermanentEffect()` — Suncleanser mode 1: strip every counter kind from target creature and lock it out of counters while the source stays on the battlefield (`GameData.countersLockedPermanentsWhileSourceOnBattlefield` → `GameQueryService.cantHaveCounters`)
- `RemoveAllCountersAndLockPlayerEffect()` — Suncleanser mode 2: clear the target player's poison and lock them out of counters while the source stays (`GameData.countersLockedPlayersWhileSourceOnBattlefield` → `GameQueryService.canPlayerGetPoisonCounters`); pair with a `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT)` for "target opponent"
- `TargetPlayerCantGainLifeRestOfGameEffect()` — the stack entry's target player can't gain life for the rest of the game (persistent, per-player). Non-targeting on `ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`; Stigma Lasher
- `AllDamageDealtWithWitherEffect()` — STATIC global: all damage is dealt as though its source had wither (creature damage becomes -1/-1 counters; player damage normal). Everlasting Torment
- `NoncombatDamageToOpponentCreaturesAsMinusCountersEffect()` — STATIC: the controller's sources deal noncombat damage to opponents' creatures as -1/-1 counters instead (combat damage unaffected). Like wither, still counts as damage dealt. Checked in `GameQueryService.noncombatDamageToOpponentCreatureAsCounters` (noncombat path only). Soul-Scar Mage
- `DoubleLifeGainEffect()` — STATIC: controller's life gain is doubled (Boon Reflection). Applied in `LifeSupport.applyGainLife`; multiple copies stack multiplicatively (2^count)
- `AdditionalLifeGainEffect(int amount)` — STATIC: the controller gains `amount` additional life whenever they would gain life (Angel of Vitality). Multiple copies add together and modify each positive life-gain event once
- `OpponentLifeGainBecomesLifeLossEffect()` — STATIC: if an opponent of the controller would gain life, that player loses that much life instead (Tainted Remedy). Applied in `LifeSupport.applyGainLife`/`applySetLifeTotal` via `GameQueryService.lifeGainBecomesLifeLoss`
- `RedistributePlayerLifeTotalsEffect()` — the resolving controller redistributes the players' current life totals among them (each total is reassigned to a player of their choice). Reverse the Sands

## Poison counters

- `GivePoisonCountersEffect(int, PoisonRecipient)` — give poison; recipient routes CONTROLLER (self) / TARGET_PLAYER / EACH_PLAYER / ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER. On an `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER` trigger, `TARGET_PLAYER` binds to the damaged player (Pit Scorpion: "whenever this deals damage to a player, that player gets a poison counter")
- `GivePoisonCountersEffect(int, TARGET_PLAYER, CardPredicate spellFilter)` — `ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL` trigger descriptor (Hand of the Praetors)
- `RemoveAllPoisonCountersAndDamageTargetPlayerEffect()` — target player loses all poison counters, source deals that much damage to them (Leeches). One effect because the damage equals the counters removed

## Creature pump / boost

- `BoostTargetCreatureEffect(DynamicAmount power, DynamicAmount toughness)` or `(int, int)` — target +X/+Y. Any "for each …", "+X/+X" (X paid), or "where X is …" target-pump = this effect + a `model/amount/DynamicAmount` — never a new per-variant class. The amount evaluates against the SOURCE, so counting refers to the effect's controller, not the pumped target. E.g. `(new XValue(), new XValue())` (Untamed Might), `(new PermanentCount(new PermanentIsCreaturePredicate(), CountScope.CONTROLLER), same)` (Elder of Laurels), `(new Sum(new Fixed(1), new CardsInGraveyard(filter, CountScope.CONTROLLER)), new Fixed(0))` (Ancestral Anger). Add a trailing `GrantDuration.UNTIL_YOUR_NEXT_TURN` — `(int, int, GrantDuration)` — for "gets -2/-0 until your next turn" (Jace, Telepath Unbound +1); the pump then rides a floating continuous effect and survives end-of-turn cleanup
- `BuffTargetCreatureIndefinitelyEffect(int power, int toughness, Set<Keyword> keywords)` or `(int, int)` — target creature gets +power/+toughness and gains `keywords` **indefinitely** (no duration, CR 611.2b). Use for "this effect lasts indefinitely" pumps (Riding the Dilu Horse); NOT for until-EOT pumps (use `BoostTargetCreatureEffect` + `GrantKeywordEffect`). Recorded as a `PERMANENT` floating continuous effect on the target — +P/+T in sublayer 7c, keywords in layer 6 (read off the float by `GameQueryService.assembleStaticBonus`); copies stack additively
- `BoostTargetCreatureWhileSourceTappedEffect(int power, int toughness)` — target creature gets +power/+toughness **for as long as the source permanent remains tapped** (CR 611.2b; Tawnos's Weaponry). Records a `WHILE_SOURCE_TAPPED` floating `BuffTargetCreatureIndefinitelyEffect` keyed to the source, read in sublayer 7c; expired when the source untaps (`CreatureControlService.onSourceUntapped` → `GameData.expireTappedSourceFloatingEffects`) or leaves. Pair with `MayNotUntapDuringUntapStepEffect` static + a `{T}` cost so the controller can keep the source tapped
- `BoostAllCreaturesWhileSourceTappedEffect(int power, int toughness)` — all creatures get +power/+toughness for as long as the source permanent remains tapped (Thran Weaponry). The handler snapshots creatures at resolution and records one source-linked `WHILE_SOURCE_TAPPED` `BuffTargetCreatureIndefinitelyEffect` per creature, so later creatures are not affected; the existing untap/leave cleanup ends all boosts
- `CardNamedPredicate(String cardName)` — card filter for exact name match (use with graveyard-count boosts above)
- `BoostSelfEffect(DynamicAmount, DynamicAmount)` or `(int, int)` — self +X/+Y; one-shot in trigger/ability slots, continuous in STATIC. Any "for each …" self-boost = this effect + a `model/amount/DynamicAmount` (`PermanentCount`, `CardsInGraveyard`, `AttachmentsOnSource`, `CreaturesBlockingSource`, `OpponentPoisonCounters`, `ImprintedCreaturePower/Toughness`, `ImprintedCardManaValue`, `LandsMatchingImprintedName`, `ChosenPermanentPower`, `XValue`, `Scaled`, `Fixed`) — never a new per-variant effect class. `ChosenPermanentPower` = effective power (at resolution) of the permanent chosen during activation, e.g. the creature tapped by `TapCreatureCost(…, trackTappedCreaturePower=true)` — Impelled Giant's "+X/+0 where X is the power of the creature tapped this way"
- `BoostSelfOrEnchantedCreatureUntilEndOfTurnEffect(DynamicAmount, DynamicAmount)` or `(int, int)` — boosts the source permanent until end of turn, or its attached creature while the source is attached. Used for a bestow permanent whose ability says "this creature gets …; if this permanent is an Aura, enchanted creature gets … instead" (Erebos's Emissary)
- `AttachedBoostEffect(DynamicAmount, DynamicAmount, GrantScope[, boolean amountsFromAttachedCreature])` — STATIC +X/+Y on the enchanted/equipped creature (`ENCHANTED_CREATURE`/`EQUIPPED_CREATURE`). Attached-scope sibling of `BoostSelfEffect`; any "for each …" aura/equipment boost = this effect + a `DynamicAmount`. `CountScope.CONTROLLER` = the aura/equipment's controller (CR 109.5); `CountScope.ATTACHED_CONTROLLER` = the enchanted/equipped creature's controller ("its controller", not CR 109.5 "you"; nothing when unattached; Righteous Authority). Negative per-count = wrap in `Scaled(…, -1)`. Blanchwood Armor, Blackblade Reforged, Bonehoard, Runechanter's Pike, Quag Sickness, Strata Scythe, Righteous Authority — never a new `BoostCreaturePer*` class. Optional 4th arg `amountsFromAttachedCreature=true` evaluates the amounts from the **attached creature's** perspective instead, so `IfSourceAttacking` reads its combat state (Tahngarth's Rage: `IfSourceAttacking(Fixed(3), Fixed(-2))` / `IfSourceAttacking(Fixed(0), Fixed(-1))`)
- `ShrinkEnchantedCreatureEffect(DynamicAmount amount)` — STATIC `-X/-Y` on the enchanted creature where `Y = min(X, toughness - 1)`, so it can never kill the creature by itself. Unlike `AttachedBoostEffect`, the amount is evaluated from the **enchanted creature's** perspective: `IfSourceAttacking` reads its combat state, `DEFENDING_PLAYER` the player it attacks, `CONTROLLER` "its controller". Snowblind
- `BoostSelfWhenCombatOpponentMatchesEffect(PermanentPredicate opponentFilter, int power, int toughness)` — "Whenever this creature blocks or becomes blocked by one or more [filter] creatures, it gets +X/+Y until end of turn." Place in ON_BLOCK and/or ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED (plain, non-PER_BLOCKER). Non-targeting: the trigger always goes on the stack; the handler applies the boost only if a combat opponent (a creature this blocks, or a creature blocking it) matches the filter, and fires once regardless of how many match. Dwarven Soldier (`PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(ORC)`, +0/+2). The opponent-filtered, self-boost analog of Deathgazer's `DestroyCombatOpponentAtEndOfCombatEffect` Optional 4th arg `Set<Keyword> grantedKeywords` also grants those keywords until end of turn — Crimson Roc (`PermanentNotPredicate(PermanentHasKeywordPredicate(FLYING))`, +1/+0, `Set.of(FIRST_STRIKE)`, ON_BLOCK only, for "whenever this creature blocks a creature without flying").
- `DoubleSelfPowerToughnessEffect()` — double self P/T
- `DoubleAllOwnCreaturesPowerToughnessEffect()` — double P/T of each creature you control (per-creature current effective P/T; Unnatural Growth). Pair with `EACH_BEGINNING_OF_COMBAT_TRIGGERED` for "at the beginning of each combat"
- `BoostAllOwnCreaturesEffect(DynamicAmount, DynamicAmount[, PermanentPredicate, GrantDuration])` — all own +X/+Y; `(int, int[, PermanentPredicate])` convenience wraps in `Fixed`. Defaults to end of turn; `GrantDuration.UNTIL_YOUR_NEXT_TURN` stores a floating layer effect for the controller's next turn. Any "where X is …" / power- or graveyard-derived mass own-pump = this effect + a `DynamicAmount` (evaluated once at resolution) — e.g. `new GreatestPowerAmongControlled()` (Overwhelming Stampede), `new CardsInGraveyard(new CardTypePredicate(CREATURE), CONTROLLER)` (Garruk, the Veil-Cursed). Never a new per-variant class
- `BoostAllCreaturesEffect(DynamicAmount, DynamicAmount)` or `(…, PermanentPredicate)` or `(…, PermanentPredicate, EachPermanentScope)` — creatures +X/+Y; `(int, int[, PermanentPredicate])` / `(int, int, EachPermanentScope)` convenience wraps in `Fixed`. Scope `ALL_PLAYERS` (default, both sides) or `TARGET_PLAYER` ("creatures target player controls", player-targeting spec, Shields of Velis Vel). "X paid" mass pump = `new Scaled(new XValue(), mult)` / `new XValue()` (Ichor Explosion, Flowstone Slide)
- `StaticBoostEffect(int, int, Set<Keyword>, GrantScope, PermanentPredicate)` — static +X/+Y + keywords
- `BoostOwnCreaturesByManaSymbolEffect(ManaColor, int powerPerSymbol, int toughnessPerSymbol)` — chroma anthem: each creature you control gets +P/+T per mana symbol of that color in its own cost (hybrid/Phyrexian symbols of that color count). Light from Within
- `BoostOtherMulticoloredCreaturesByColorCountEffect(int powerPerColor, int toughnessPerColor)` — anthem: each *other* multicolored creature you control gets +P/+T per color it has. Monocolored/colorless creatures and the source itself are unaffected (layer-5-aware color count). Knight of New Alara `(1, 1)`
- `SetBasePowerToughnessEffect(int, int)` — set target creature's base P/T until end of turn; `(int, int, GrantScope)` for continuous static (e.g. `ENCHANTED_CREATURE`, Deep Freeze); `SetBasePowerToughnessEffect.toughnessOnly(int)` — "has base toughness X" only, base power untouched (Chariot of the Sun); `SetBasePowerToughnessEffect.powerOnly(int)` — "has base power X" only, base toughness untouched (Vhati il-Dal)
- `SetSelfPowerToughnessFromTargetCreatureEffect(int powerFromTargetToughnessOffset, int toughnessFromTargetPowerOffset, PermanentPredicate targetPredicate)` — sets the SOURCE's P/T until end of turn from the target creature's current toughness/power plus the given offsets; values locked in at resolution (no CDA). `targetSpec()` is a benign CREATURE narrowed by `targetPredicate`. Sworn Defender = `(-1, 1, PermanentAllOfPredicate(IsCreature, InCombatWithSource))` — power becomes target's toughness − 1, toughness becomes target's power + 1
- `SetTargetBasePowerToughnessUntilNextUpkeepThenAddCounterEffect(int power, int toughness)` — Cycle of Life: target creature has base P/T until the controller's NEXT UPKEEP (floating 7b with `EffectDuration.UNTIL_CONTROLLERS_NEXT_UPKEEP`, so it survives end-of-turn cleanup), then a `+1/+1` counter arrives at that upkeep via a `PutCounterOnPermanentAtNextUpkeep` delayed action. `(power, toughness, CounterType, amount)` for other counters
- `SetAllOwnCreaturesBasePowerToughnessEffect(DynamicAmount, DynamicAmount)` or `(int, int)` — set base P/T of all creatures you control to X/X until end of turn (layer 7b, modifiers apply on top). X-cost ability = `new XValue()` (Mirror Entity)
- `SetAllUnblockedCreaturesBasePowerToughnessEffect(int, int)` — set base P/T of every unblocked creature (any player's) until end of turn (layer 7b). "Unblocked" = attacking + no blocker, locked in at resolution (Inkfathom Witch)
- `GrantBaseStatsToCounterBearersEffect(CounterType, int power, int toughness, Set<Keyword>)` — resolves to a game-wide continuous rule: "every creature with a [type] counter has base P/T power/toughness and has [keywords] for as long as it has such a counter" (layer 7b set + layer 6 keywords). Installs source-independent PERMANENT floating effects scoped by `PermanentHasCountersPredicate`, so the rule survives the source leaving and lapses when the counter is removed (scope re-evaluated each pass); idempotent. Put in `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD` and pair with an upkeep `PutCounterOnTargetPermanentEffect(theCounter)`. Aven Mimeomancer (feather counter → base 3/1 + flying)
- `BecomeCreatureTypeWithBasePowerToughnessEffect(int power, int toughness, CardSubtype addedSubtype[, CardSubtype requiredSubtype])` — one-shot non-targeting SELF effect: permanently adds `addedSubtype` (into `grantedSubtypes`) and sets base P/T **indefinitely** (permanent base override, layer 7b via fresh timestamp — not until end of turn). Optional `requiredSubtype` = intervening "if" checked at resolution (source must already have that subtype, granted counts). Figure of Destiny's level-up chain. Pair permanent keyword grants (flying/first strike) as STATIC `ConditionalEffect(new SourceHasSubtype(subtype), new GrantKeywordEffect(kw, SELF))`
- `BecomeCreatureEffect(int power, int toughness, List<CardSubtype> subtypes[, Set<Keyword> keywords])` — one-shot non-targeting SELF effect: permanently changes the source into only a creature with the supplied base P/T and creature subtypes. Optional keywords are added to the runtime card copy. Uses a runtime card copy so the source's noncreature card types are removed. Lurking Jackals, Opal Archangel
- `BecomeEnchantmentEffect()` — one-shot non-targeting SELF effect: restores the source permanent's original card form, including its enchantment type and abilities. Opal Acrolith
- `GrantProtectionFromTriggeringSpellColorsEffect()` — one-shot non-targeting SELF effect: adds permanent protection from every color of the spell that caused the trigger to the source's runtime card. Opal Titan
- `SwitchPowerToughnessEffect()` — switch P/T
- `SwitchAllCreaturesPowerToughnessEffect()` — switch the power and toughness of each creature on the battlefield until end of turn; the affected creatures are fixed when the effect resolves
- `BoostByBushidoEffect()` — continuous Bushido lord: each OTHER Samurai its controller controls gets +1/+1 for each point of Bushido that creature has. Takeno, Samurai General

`DynamicStaticBoostEffect(DynamicAmount, DynamicAmount, GrantScope[, PermanentPredicate])` — dynamic continuous +X/+Y for a creature scope; amounts are evaluated from the static source and its controller. Use for board-count anthems such as Hold the Gates; pair a separate `GrantKeywordEffect` when the static text also grants a keyword. `CreaturesEnteredBattlefieldThisTurn(CountScope.CONTROLLER)` counts creature cards that entered under the source controller this turn, including tokens.

- `BoostLegendaryCreaturesByOtherLegendaryCreaturesEffect(int powerPerCreature, int toughnessPerCreature)` â€” each legendary creature the source controller controls gets +P/+T for each other legendary creature that player controls; a legendary source that is also a creature receives the bonus. Heroes' Podium `(1, 1)`

## P/T setting / counters

- `BecomeCreatureTypeWithBasePowerToughnessEffect.replacingSubtype(...)` — permanent subtype replacement with an optional base-P/T change; use the overload without P/T to retain the current base stats.

- `SetCreatureTypesToImprintedCreatureEffect(List<CardSubtype> retainedSubtypes)` — static layer-4 effect that replaces the source's creature subtypes with the imprinted creature card's creature subtypes while retaining the supplied types. Used with `SetPowerToughnessToAmountEffect(ImprintedCreaturePower, ImprintedCreatureToughness)` for Duplicant.

- `SetPowerToughnessToAmountEffect(DynamicAmount power, DynamicAmount toughness)` — CDA that sets P/T on a 0/0 base (pass the same amount for both). Replaced the `PowerToughnessEqualTo*` family + `BoostSelfBySlimeCountersOnLinkedPermanentEffect`. Amounts: `PermanentCount(IsLand/IsCreature/IsArtifact/HasSubtype…, CONTROLLER)` (lands/creatures/artifacts/Swamps you control), `CardsInGraveyard(filter, CONTROLLER|ANY_PLAYER)`, `CardsInExile(filter, CONTROLLER)` (exile by owner — Seize the Storm flashback cards), `CardsInHand(CONTROLLER)` (hand size), `ControllerLifeTotal()` (life total), `CountersOnLinkedPermanent(type, id)` (linked-permanent counters), `ColorManaSymbolsInGraveyard(color, CONTROLLER)` (chroma of graveyard cards — Umbra Stalker = black), `ChosenNumberOnSource()` (the number last chosen for this permanent — Shapeshifter: power = `ChosenNumberOnSource()`, toughness = `Sum(Fixed(7), Scaled(ChosenNumberOnSource, -1))`). `TotalPowerOfCardsExiledWithSource()`/`TotalToughnessOfCardsExiledWithSource()` (summed P/T of the cards exiled tracked with this permanent — Sutured Ghoul). Compose offsets/negation with `Sum`/`Scaled`/`Fixed`. Pair "choose a number" with `ChooseNumberOnEnterEffect`/`ChooseNumberEffect`
- `PutCountersOnSourceEffect(int power, int toughness, int amount)` — counters on self; it can also be granted through `GrantTriggeredAbilityEffect` for a creature's `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_CREATURE` trigger
- `DiscardCardsAndPutCountersOnSourceEffect(CardPredicate cardFilter, int countersPerCard, String cardDescription)` — controller chooses any number of matching hand cards to discard; the source gets `countersPerCard` +1/+1 counters per card discarded. Uses an X-value count choice followed by filtered sequential discard choices, then applies the counters synchronously when the discard completes. Mind Maggots (`CardTypePredicate(CREATURE)`, 2)
- `RenownEffect(int amount)` — renown N (CR 702.112a) on `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`; counters + `Permanent.renowned`, gated by the `SourceIsRenowned` condition; the flip fires `ON_SELF_BECOMES_RENOWNED` (Relic Seeker) and `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_BECOMES_RENOWNED` (Valeron Wardens) triggers
- `MonstrosityEffect(int amount)` — resolves a monstrosity ability once, putting +1/+1 counters on the source and marking it `Permanent.monstrous`; use `SourceIsMonstrous` for the source's permanent bonuses and activation restriction
- `PutCountersOnSourceEqualToEnteringPowerEffect(int power, int toughness, boolean optional)` — ON_ANY_OTHER_CREATURE_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD: put counters on self = entering creature's power; `optional` = "you may" (Hamletback Goliath)
- `PutCountersOnSourceEqualToDyingPowerEffect(int power, int toughness, boolean optional)` — ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES: put counters on self = the dying creature's last-known effective power; `optional` = "you may" (Kresh the Bloodbraided). Death sibling of the entering-power effect
- `EachPlayerLosesLifeAndPutCountersOnSourceEqualToLifeLostEffect(int lifeLossPerPlayer)` — UPKEEP_TRIGGERED: each player loses N life, then the source gets a +1/+1 counter for each 1 life actually lost this way (Blood Tyrant)
- `PutCountersOnEnchantedCreatureEffect(CounterType, int amount)` — activated-ability effect on an Aura: put `amount` counters on the creature it is attached to (Krasis Incubation `{1}{G}{U}, Return this Aura to its owner's hand: Put two +1/+1 counters on enchanted creature`). Implements `AttachedPermanentSelfTargetingEffect` + a self-targeting `TargetSpec`, so `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService` captures `attachedTo` onto the stack entry **before** costs are paid — a bounce/sacrifice cost that detaches the Aura still leaves the counters landing on the creature it had enchanted (CR 608.2h)
- `PutCountersOnSelfEffect(CounterType)` — one counter of a type on self (charge, +1/+1, study, invitation, etc.)
- `PutCounterOnSelfThenDrawPerCounterEffect(CounterType)` — put one counter of that type on the source, **then** draw a card for each counter of that type on it (Mind Unbound, `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`, `LORE`). One effect, not counter+draw as two slot effects, because separate upkeep effects become separate LIFO stack entries and the draw would resolve first
- `PutCountersOnSelfEffect(CounterType, int count)` — N counters of a type on self (e.g. Withengar Unbound: 13 +1/+1); on `ON_EQUIPPED_CREATURE_DEALS_COMBAT_DAMAGE`, the source is the Equipment when its equipped creature deals combat damage (Umezawa's Jitte)
- `PutCountersOnSelfEffect(CounterType, DynamicAmount)` — dynamic count on self, e.g. `(CounterType.TOWER, new XValue())` for "{X}: Put X tower counters" (Helix Pinnacle)
- `PutCountersOnGrantingEquipmentEffect(CounterType)` — counters on the Equipment that granted the ability rather than on the activating permanent (Hankyu's aim counters); bound to the granting Equipment at activation time
- `RemoveAllCountersAsCostEffect(CounterType[, boolean fromGrantingEquipment])` — activation cost: remove all counters of that type and snapshot the count into `xValue` (read back with `XValue`). `fromGrantingEquipment = true` pays from the granting Equipment (Hankyu); default pays from the activating permanent (Jar of Eyeballs)
- `PutCounterOnTargetPermanentEffect(CounterType, int)` — counters on target permanent (`PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`/`MINUS_ONE_MINUS_ONE`/…); `(…, new XValue())` for "X counters"; `(…, count, boolean regenerateIfSurvives)` (Gore Vassal); `withTargetRestriction(…, targetPredicate)` to restrict legal targets; `(…, count, PermanentPredicate)` for a non-targeting own-permanent choice
- `DistributeCountersAmongTargetsEffect(CounterType, DynamicAmount total, DivisionMode, boolean removeAtNextCleanup)` — the counter analogue of `DealDividedDamageEffect`. `evenlyAmongTargets(CounterType, int total)` = "distribute two counters among one or two target creatures": even split of `total` across the chosen target group (1 target → 2, 2 targets → 1 each), the forced CR 601.2d distribution for two counters among ≤2 targets; bind to `target(creatureFilter, 1, 2).addEffect(...)`. Splendid Agony (`MINUS_ONE_MINUS_ONE`, harmful spec), Common Bond (`PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`, benign), Contagion (`MINUS_TWO_MINUS_ONE`, harmful). `chosenAmongTargetCreatures(CounterType, DynamicAmount total)` = a genuine "distribute X counters among any number of target creatures" — no `target()` call; targets and per-target counts come from the cast-time damageAssignments map (harness `castInstant(player, idx, Map)`), total evaluated at cast time (Spoils of War, `CardsInGraveyard(artifact-or-creature, OPPONENTS)`). `chosenUntilNextCleanup(CounterType, int total)` = the same chosen division with a fixed total plus the Bounty of the Hunt rider — every placed counter is scheduled onto `Permanent.getCountersToRemoveAtNextCleanup()` and shed by `TurnCleanupService` at the beginning of the next cleanup step (clamped to what the creature still has, so counters it already had survive)
- `PutPlusOnePlusOneCounterOnEachCreatureTargetPlayerControlsEffect()` — +1/+1 on each creature the target player controls (bind to the player target group via `target(...).addEffect(...)`)
- `PutCounterOnEachControlledPermanentEffect(CounterType, int, PermanentPredicate)` — counters on each own permanent matching predicate (use `PermanentIsCreaturePredicate` for "each creature you control")
- `RemoveCounterFromEachControlledPermanentEffect(CounterType, int, PermanentPredicate)` — remove up to N counters from each own permanent matching predicate, clamped at zero (Heartmender's "remove a -1/-1 counter from each creature you control")
- `RemoveCounterFromEachMatchingPermanentEffect(CounterType, int, PermanentPredicate, EachPermanentScope)` — remove up to N counters from each matching permanent across `ALL_PLAYERS` or the `TARGET_PLAYER` battlefield; the target-player form reads the stack entry's target (Temporal Distortion's "remove all hourglass counters from permanents that player controls")
- `PutCounterOnEachMatchingPermanentEffect(CounterType, int|DynamicAmount, PermanentPredicate, EachPermanentScope)` — counters on each matching permanent across `ALL_PLAYERS`/`TARGET_PLAYER` (each attacking / other / all creatures; each creature target player controls)
- `PutCounterOnReferencedPermanentEffect(CounterType[, int][, PermanentPredicate condition])` — counter(s) on the **attached** permanent, i.e. the source Aura *or* Equipment's `attachedTo` host (Aura vs Equipment is not a semantic axis). Works in UPKEEP_TRIGGERED (Primal Cocoon, the M13 Ring cycle), ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_TAPPED (Spirit Shackle) and ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES (Sadistic Glee — the counter lands on the host, not the creature that died). The optional `condition` gates the placement at resolution ("if it's blue"), it is not a targeting restriction
- `PutCounterOnReferencedPermanentEffect(PermanentReference.TRIGGERING, CounterType)` — same effect, but on the permanent whose event produced the trigger ("put a wind counter on **it**"). Reads `StackEntry.triggeringPermanentId`, set by the becomes-tapped trigger collectors; never targets/fizzles. Freyalise's Winds
- `AddCounterToEnchantedCreatureThenDestroyAtThresholdEffect(CounterType, int threshold)` — one Aura upkeep trigger (Consuming Ferocity): put a counter of that type on enchanted creature; if it then has `threshold`+ such counters, **the creature** (CR 608.2h) deals damage equal to its power to its controller, then it is destroyed and can't be regenerated. Single effect because it is a single triggered ability
- `RemoveCounterFromSourceThenDestroyEnchantedAtZeroEffect(CounterType, int damageToController)` — Aura trigger (Orcish Mine): remove one counter of that type from the Aura; if it was the last, destroy the enchanted permanent and the Aura deals that much damage to its controller. Use on `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` + `ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_TAPPED`
- `TurnFaceUpReplacementEffect` / `PutCountersOnTurnFaceUpEffect(int)` — replacement behavior for "as this creature is turned face up, put N +1/+1 counters on it". Register it in `ON_TURNED_FACE_UP`; the turn-face-up action applies the counters immediately, so it is not queued as a triggered ability (Hooded Hydra)
- `EnterWithCountersEffect(CounterType, DynamicAmount)` — "enters the battlefield with … counters" (as-enters replacement effect): fixed = `Fixed(n)`, X paid = `XValue()`, "for each …" = a counting amount (`CreatureDeathsThisTurn`, `LifeGainedThisTurn(CONTROLLER)`, `DistinctManaCostsAmongCardsInGraveyard()`, `Sum(PermanentCount(...), CardsInGraveyard(...))`, …). "If kicked" / "Raid —" variants wrap it in `ConditionalEffect(new Kicked()/new Raid(), …)`
- `DevourEffect(int multiplier)` — Devour N as-enters replacement (`ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`): controller may sacrifice any number of their *other* creatures; enters with `multiplier ×` that many +1/+1 counters and records the devoured creatures. Read the count with the `CreaturesDevoured` amount, or a per-subtype count with `DevouredCreaturesOfSubtype(CardSubtype)`. Tar Fiend = `new DevourEffect(2)` + `DiscardEffect(new CreaturesDevoured(), DiscardRecipient.TARGET_PLAYER)`; Voracious Dragon = `new DevourEffect(1)` + `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect(new Scaled(new DevouredCreaturesOfSubtype(CardSubtype.GOBLIN), 2))` ("twice the number of Goblins it devoured"; a devoured Changeling counts as any type)
- `SacrificePermanentsAsEntersForCountersEffect(PermanentPredicate filter, int countersPerPermanent)` — as-enters replacement (`ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`, CR 614.1c): "As this permanent enters, sacrifice any number of permanents matching `filter`. It enters with that many +1/+1 counters on it." Non-keyword generalization of `DevourEffect` (any permanent type, nothing recorded on the permanent). The entering permanent itself is never offered; nothing to sacrifice ⇒ no prompt. Shimatsu the Bloodcloaked = `(new PermanentTruePredicate(), 1)`
- `SacrificeAllPermanentsAsEntersEffect(PermanentPredicate filter)` — as-enters replacement (`ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`): sacrifice every permanent you control matching `filter` before the entering permanent is placed on the battlefield. The entering permanent is not included. Overlaid Terrain uses `PermanentIsLandPredicate`.
- `ExileAnyNumberOfCreatureCardsFromGraveyardOnEnterEffect()` — as-enters replacement (CR 614.1c, `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`): controller exiles any number of creature cards from their own graveyard, tracked with the entering permanent. Pair with `SetPowerToughnessToAmountEffect(TotalPowerOfCardsExiledWithSource, TotalToughnessOfCardsExiledWithSource)` in STATIC for the Sutured Ghoul CDA. Empty graveyard ⇒ no prompt
- `PayAnyAmountOfLifeOnEnterEffect()` — as-enters replacement (`ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`): controller picks 0..their current life total and loses that much life before ETB triggers; the amount is stored via `Permanent.setChosenNumber`. Pair with `SetPowerToughnessToAmountEffect(ChosenNumberOnSource, ChosenNumberOnSource)` in STATIC for the Minion of the Wastes CDA (paying 0 ⇒ 0/0, dies to SBA)
- `SacrificeAnyNumberOfCreaturesSetPowerToughnessOnEnterEffect()` — as-enters replacement (`ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`, CR 614.1c): controller sacrifices any number of their *other* creatures (zero legal) before ETB triggers; the sacrificed creatures' total effective power/toughness is stamped on the entering permanent as a durable layer-7b base-P/T override (`permanentBasePowerOverride`/`permanentBaseToughnessOverride`), so counters and boosts apply on top. No other creatures or an empty pick ⇒ 0/0, dies to SBA. Dracoplasm
- `ExploitEffect()` — Exploit (CR 702.110): wrap in `MayEffect` on `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`; may sacrifice any controlled creature (incl. self). Success fires `ON_EXPLOIT` if source was on battlefield at resolution start. Pair `ON_EXPLOIT` + `CounterSpellEffect` + `StackEntryHasTargetPredicate` for Overcharged Amalgam
- `RemoveCountersToCreateTetraviteTokensEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` — Tetravus upkeep trigger 1: "you may remove any number of +1/+1 counters from this creature. If you do, create that many tokens from `tokenTemplate`." Prompts a 0..N number choice (0 = decline), removes that many +1/+1 counters and creates that many tokens, each recorded in `GameData.sourceCreatedTokens` as "created with" the source. Paired with the exile effect below
- `ExileTetraviteTokensToPutCountersOnSelfEffect()` — Tetravus upkeep trigger 2: "you may exile any number of tokens created with this creature. If you do, put that many +1/+1 counters on this creature." Multi-permanent choice over the source's tracked, still-alive tokens (any controller); exiles the chosen ones and puts that many +1/+1 counters on the source
- `GraveyardEnterWithAdditionalCountersEffect(CardSubtype, int)` — graveyard static: while in your graveyard, creatures of that subtype you control enter with N extra +1/+1 counters (Dearly Departed / HUMAN)
- `ControlledCreaturesEnterWithAdditionalCountersEffect(CardSubtype, DynamicAmount)` — battlefield static: while on the battlefield, each other creature of that subtype you control enters with the evaluated number of extra +1/+1 counters (fixed int constructor for Sage of Fables / WIZARD; `PermanentCount` for Giada, Font of Hope / ANGEL)
- `ControlledCreaturesEnterWithAdditionalCountersThisTurnEffect(int)` — one-shot: for the rest of this turn, each creature you control enters with N extra +1/+1 counters (Zameck Guildmage); turn-long, no subtype filter, survives the source leaving
- `ControlledCreaturesEnterWithSourcePowerCountersEffect(CardSubtype)` — battlefield static: each other creature you control enters with additional +1/+1 counters equal to the source's power, and with that subtype in addition to its other types (Master Biomancer / MUTANT)
- Increment keyword — keyword-driven (`Keyword.INCREMENT`, auto-loaded from Scryfall): +1/+1 counter on self when mana spent on a cast spell exceeds self's current power or toughness. Add nothing to the card; behavior lives in `TriggerCollectionService.collectIncrementTriggers` (resolution effect: `IncrementTriggerEffect`). E.g. Ambitious Augmenter
- `ProliferateEffect()` — proliferate
- `AdjustEachCounterKindOnTargetEffect()` — targets any permanent (benign `PERMANENT`); "for each kind of counter on target permanent, put another of that kind on it or remove one" (Quarry Hauler). Snapshots the counter kinds present and prompts ADD/REMOVE once per kind via the generic list-choice; no-op if none. Distinct from `ProliferateEffect` (adds only, all counter-bearing permanents) and `DoubleCountersOnTargetPermanentEffect` (doubles, no choice)
- `DoublePlusOneCountersOnControlledCreaturesEffect()` — non-targeting; doubles the number of +1/+1 counters on each creature the controller controls (Kalonian Hydra, `ON_ATTACK`). Skips creatures with no +1/+1 counters and those that can't have counters; fires the counters-put-on-self triggers afterwards
- `DoublePlusOneCountersOnTargetCreatureEffect()` — benign creature-targeting effect; doubles the number of +1/+1 counters on each chosen creature, using the shared counter-placement replacement and trigger pipeline (Solidarity of Heroes)
- `KickerEffect(String cost)` — mana kicker declaration; `KickerEffect.tap(PermanentPredicate, String)` declares a non-mana tap kicker
- `BuybackEffect(String cost)` / `BuybackEffect(int discardCount[, boolean randomDiscard])` / `BuybackEffect(PayLifeCost, int discardCount, boolean randomDiscard)` — STATIC declaration of a spell's optional buyback cost (CR 702.27). Paid when the caster announces buyback (wire `PlayCardRequest.buyback`; chosen discard costs use `discardHandCardIndices`, while random discard costs are selected automatically); the stack entry is stamped `StackEntry.buyback`, read at resolution by the `BuybackPaid` condition. Pair with `ConditionalEffect(new BuybackPaid(), ReturnToHandEffect.selfSpell())` in the SPELL slot so the spell returns to its owner's hand as it resolves; countered or fizzled spells (which never resolve) still go to the graveyard. Harness `castInstantWithBuyback` / `castSorceryWithBuyback`
- `SpliceEffect(CardSubtype onto, String cost)` — STATIC: splice onto [subtype] for [cost] (CR 702.47). Cast via `harness.castWithSplice` / `GameService.playCardWithSplice`; host spell must have the subtype (e.g. Arcane). Through the Breach
