- `SpliceEffect(CardSubtype onto, String cost, PermanentPredicate tapCost)` — same, for a non-mana splice cost "Tap an untapped white creature you control" (Hundred-Talon Strike: `(ARCANE, "", creature + white)`). The permanent to tap is passed as the extra `spliceCostPermanentIds` argument of `harness.castWithSplice` / `GameService.playCardWithSplice`, one id per spliced card that has a tap cost, in splice order; tapping as a cost ignores summoning sickness
- `SpliceEffect.returning(CardSubtype onto, PermanentPredicate returnCost)` — same, for a bounce splice cost "Return a blue creature you control to its owner's hand" (Veil of Secrecy). The permanent to return rides on the same `spliceCostPermanentIds` argument as a tap cost, one id per spliced card with a permanent cost, in splice order
- `SpliceEffect.exilingGraveyard(CardSubtype onto, int count)` — same, for the graveyard splice cost "Exile four cards from your graveyard" (Horobi's Whisper). No extra cast argument: splice has no client-side choice UI, so the engine exiles the oldest `count` cards of the caster's graveyard; too few cards means the cast is rejected
- `SpliceEffect(CardSubtype onto, String cost)` — STATIC: splice onto [subtype] for a mana cost; use `SpliceEffect(CardSubtype onto, List<CastingCost>)` for composed costs such as sacrificing permanents, or the three-argument constructor to add splice-only effects alongside the card's normal SPELL effects (CR 702.47). Cast via `harness.castWithSplice` / `GameService.playCardWithSplice`; host spell must have the subtype (e.g. Arcane). Through the Breach, Torrent of Stone, Roar of Jukai
- `RemoveXCountersFromSourceCost(CounterType)` — `CostEffect`; removes X counters of that type from the source permanent, X chosen at activation and bounded by the counters actually present (prompted like a variable loyalty cost, not an `{X}` mana cost). The chosen X rides on the activation's `xValue`, readable back with an `XValue` amount; the engine prompts with an `XValueChoice` when the client does not announce X. Night Dealings (`CounterType.THEFT`), Cruel Sadist (`CounterType.PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`), Talon of Pain (`CounterType.CHARGE`)

## Keywords / abilities

- `GainKeywordsOfCreatureCardsExiledWithSourceEffect()` — STATIC self-only effect: the source gains the watched keyword set found on every creature card exiled with it, and carries over fixed protection effects represented by `ProtectionGrantingEffect`. Used by Death-Mask Duplicant.
- `GrantEffectToTargetEffect.toSourcePermanent(EffectSlot, CardEffect)` — indefinite, non-targeting grant of a triggered ability to the source permanent.

- `GrantHexproofFromOwnColorsEffect()` — STATIC: each other creature you control has hexproof from each of its current colors; the colors are recomputed from the affected creature's layered characteristics (Tam, Mindful First-Year)
- `GrantKeywordEffect(Keyword, GrantScope)` or `(Keyword, GrantScope, PermanentPredicate)` or `(Set<Keyword>, GrantScope)` — grant keywords. Add a trailing `GrantDuration` (`(Keyword, GrantScope, GrantDuration)` / `(Set<Keyword>, GrantScope, GrantDuration)`) for one-shot duration: `END_OF_TURN` (default), `UNTIL_YOUR_NEXT_TURN`, `UNTIL_YOUR_NEXT_UPKEEP`, or `WHILE_SOURCE_ON_BATTLEFIELD` ("for as long as you control [source]" — Aegis Angel; floating layer-6 effect keyed to the source, survives cleanup, ends when the source leaves the battlefield or changes controller), or `INDEFINITE` (no stated duration, so it lasts until the end of the game per CR 611.2a — Nature's Blessing; `PERMANENT` floating layer-6 effect mirrored into `Permanent.persistentGrantedKeywords`, neither cleared by turn cleanup). In `STATIC` slot the grant is continuous and the duration is ignored. Static land scopes `OWN_LANDS` / `ALL_LANDS` grant to lands you control / every land on the battlefield matching `filter` (Shimmer: `(PHASING, ALL_LANDS, new PermanentHasSourceChosenSubtypePredicate())`). `GrantKeywordEffect.toTargetIf(Keyword, PermanentPredicate grantCondition)` — grant to target ONLY if it matches `grantCondition` (checked at resolution; target stays legal, only the grant is conditional — Vampire's Zeal, Blessing of Belzenlok). `grantCondition` is distinct from `filter`/the spec predicate.
- `CantHaveOrGainKeywordEffect(Keyword)` — marker granted with `GrantEffectEffect` for "can't have or gain [keyword]". It removes the keyword from the affected permanent, blocks later layer-6 grants while active, and one-shot keyword grants are skipped while the restriction applies (Archetype of Courage).
- `GrantEffectToOwnCreaturesUntilEndOfTurnEffect(EffectSlot, CardEffect)` — snapshot grant of a temporary triggered ability to creatures you control until EOT (later ETBs/control changes excluded). Driven/Despair (`ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER` + draw / discard). Own-creatures analog of `GrantEffectToTargetUntilEndOfTurnEffect`
- `GrantEffectEffect(CardEffect, GrantScope)` / `(…, PermanentPredicate)` — continuous grant of a CardEffect to matching permanents. Granting `CumulativeUpkeepEffect` is collected at upkeep via `GrantedUpkeepEffectSupport` (Breath of Dreams). `GrantScope.SELF_AND_PAIRED` grants to the source + its soulbond partner — use with `ConditionalEffect(SourceIsPaired, …)` for non-keyword paired grants such as protection (Diregraf Escort)
- `GrantEffectToTargetEffect(EffectSlot, CardEffect)` / `(EffectSlot, CardEffect, EffectDuration)` — grant a triggered ability to the target permanent. Default duration `PERMANENT` (Balduvian Shaman → `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` + `CumulativeUpkeepEffect("{1}")`; Musician → `DestroyUnlessPaysPerCounterEffect(MUSIC, "{1}")`); `UNTIL_END_OF_TURN` uses temporary storage. Skips if the target already has an equal effect in that slot (Musician "if it doesn't have"). Collected by step/combat trigger services alongside native card effects. `GrantEffectToTargetEffect.toEnchantedPermanent(slot, effect, duration)` grants to the Aura's enchanted permanent instead (no target, no skip-if-present) — Genju of the Fields
- `GrantEffectToTargetUntilEndOfTurnEffect(EffectSlot, CardEffect)` — until EOT grant of a temporary triggered ability to the target creature(s). Collected for `ON_DEATH`, `ON_ATTACK`, `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`, `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` and `ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED` (Barreling Attack: trample + "when that creature becomes blocked this turn, it gets +1/+1 for each creature blocking it")
- `RemoveKeywordEffect(Keyword, GrantScope)` or `(Keyword, GrantScope, PermanentPredicate filter)` — one-shot keyword removal until end of turn (floating layer-6 removal). Scopes handled by the normal handler: `SELF`, `TARGET`, `OPPONENT_CREATURES` (mass removal from opponents' creatures — Invert the Skies), and `ALL_CREATURES` (every creature — Hour of Devastation strips indestructible). In `STATIC` slot it's a continuous removal via the static handler's creature-scope matcher. Pass an `EffectDuration` — `(Keyword, GrantScope, EffectDuration)` or `(Keyword, GrantScope, filter, EffectDuration)` — for a non-end-of-turn loss; `EffectDuration.PERMANENT` = indefinite loss that lasts as long as the permanent stays on the battlefield (Elder Land Wurm's "When this creature blocks, it loses defender", ON_BLOCK). A later grant of the same keyword still re-adds it (timestamp ordering).
- `GrantChosenKeywordEffect(List<Keyword> options, GrantScope scope)` — prompt to choose one keyword from options, grant it until end of turn. `GrantScope.TARGET` grants to the target permanent (bind to its target group in multi-target spells — Golem Artisan, Practiced Offense); `GrantScope.SELF` is the untargeted form granting to the source permanent (Urza's Avenger, Illusionary Presence). No other scope is accepted
- `GrantChosenLandwalkToSelfEffect()` — prompt for any implemented land type, then the source gains that type of landwalk until end of turn. Supports nonbasic land types through a defender-land predicate. Illusionary Presence
- `GrantChosenSnowLandwalkToTargetEffect()` — prompt for a land type, then target creature gains **snow** landwalk of it until end of turn (can't be blocked while the defender controls a land that is both snow and that type, CR 702.14c). Never use a landwalk `Keyword` for this — those check the subtype only and would give plain landwalk. Stores a predicate in `Permanent.unblockableIfDefenderControlsUntilEndOfTurn`. Barbarian Guides (paired with `ReturnTargetPermanentToHandAtEndStepEffect`)
- `GrantLandwalkOfSacrificedLandToTargetEffect()` — target creature gains plain landwalk of each land type of the land sacrificed to pay the ability's `SacrificePermanentCost`, until end of turn. Reads the sacrificed land's card off `Permanent.chosenCard` (recorded at payment) and delegates the grant to `GrantKeywordEffectHandler`. benign CREATURE spec. Excavator
- `GrantFlashToCardTypeEffect(CardPredicate)` / `(CardPredicate, boolean appliesToAllPlayers)` — flash to card types (static); the 2-arg form grants it to every player, not just the controller (Aluren)
- `GrantFlashToSpellsThisTurnEffect()` — one-shot: controller may cast spells this turn as though they had flash (Alchemist's Refuge)
- `GrantFlashToCardTypeThisTurnEffect(CardType)` — one-shot: controller may cast spells of the given type this turn as though they had flash (Winding Canyons)
- `MayCastTopInstantOrSorceryFromGraveyardUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — until EOT, cast the top GY card if it is an instant/sorcery (normal cost; exile instead of GY). Bösium Strip; tracked in `GameData.mayCastTopInstantOrSorceryFromGraveyardUntilEndOfTurn`
- `GrantFlashToNextSpellOfTypeThisTurnEffect(CardType)` — one-shot: the next spell of that type the controller casts this turn can be cast as though it had flash (Quicken)
- `EmpowerNextCreatureSpellThisTurnEffect(boolean uncounterable, int additionalPlusOneCounters)` — one-shot: the next creature spell the controller casts this turn can't be countered / its creature enters with extra +1/+1 counters (Savage Summoning)
- `SetSelfKeywordIndefinitelyEffect(Keyword keyword, boolean gained)` — "this creature gains/loses [keyword]" indefinitely (CR 611.2b), NOT until end of turn. Records a PERMANENT floating continuous effect on the source; latest activation per keyword replaces the previous one. Mist Dragon
- `FlashCastWithCleanupSacrificeEffect()` — STATIC on the card: the Mirage flash clause ("cast as though it had flash"; sacrificed at the next cleanup step if cast when a sorcery couldn't have been cast). No handler — casting timing, entry stamping and the cleanup sweep are engine-side (Ward of Lights, Necromancy)
- `BecomeAuraReanimateFromGraveyardEffect()` — ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD: intervening-if still on battlefield, become an Aura, put target creature card from any graveyard onto the battlefield under your control, and attach (Necromancy). `graveyardCard(ALL_GRAVEYARDS)` targetSpec; ETB graveyard-target flow chooses as the trigger goes on the stack
- `LicidBecomeAuraEffect(endCost)` — activated: source loses the ability, becomes an Aura with enchant creature and attaches to target creature; benign `CREATURE` targetSpec (Quickening Licid)
- `LicidEndEffect()` — the `endCost` payment installed on the Aura face by `LicidBecomeAuraEffect`; detaches and reverts to the printed creature. Never used on a card class
- `ProtectionFromChosenColorEffect(GrantScope.ENCHANTED_CREATURE)` — STATIC on an Aura: enchanted creature has protection from the Aura's chosen colour; pair with `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD ChooseColorOnEnterEffect()`. The Aura is never detached by the protection it grants (Ward of Lights). No-arg = self-scoped, `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD` (Voice of All)
- `GrantProtectionChoiceUntilEndOfTurnEffect(GrantScope.OWN_CREATURES, PermanentPredicate filter)` — untargeted mass grant: choose one colour, every creature you control matching `filter` gains protection from it until end of turn (Brave the Elements, `new PermanentColorInPredicate(Set.of(CardColor.WHITE))`). `null` filter = each creature you control; membership fixed on resolution; no prompt if nothing qualifies. The same record's `TARGET` / `SELF` scopes are the single- and self-target forms; `filter` is rejected on those
- `GrantProtectionFromColorsUntilYourNextTurnEffect(Set<CardColor>)` — target creature you control gains protection from the listed colors until your next turn. Uses a floating layer-6 effect, so the grant survives cleanup and expires with the ability controller’s next turn. Sygg, Wanderbrine Shield.
- `GrantProtectionFromOpponentCreaturesUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — target creature you control gains protection from creatures controlled by its opponents until end of turn. The source/controller comparison is evaluated live, so the protection follows a later control change; cleared by `resetModifiers()`. Crypsis.
- `GrantMadnessEqualToManaCostEffect(CardPredicate)` — owned off-BF matching cards gain madness; cost = mana cost (static; Falkenrath Gorger)
- `GrantSpellCastingAbilityToSpellsEffect(Keyword, CardPredicate)` — spells you cast matching the predicate have the granted ability (static). Only `CONSPIRE` (Wort, the Raidmother) and `CONVOKE` (Chief Engineer) are accepted — the cost gates query no others
- `CanAttackAsThoughHasteUnlessEnteredThisTurnEffect()` — STATIC self-scoped: this creature can attack as though it had haste unless it entered the battlefield this turn (Chaos Lord); attack-only bypass read in `AttackLegalityService.canAttack`. Gate the parity half of the card with `ConditionalEffect(new TotalPermanentCountEven(), …)`
- `ActivateCreatureAbilitiesAsThoughHasteEffect()` — controller may activate abilities of creatures they control as though they had haste (static; lifts summoning-sickness on ability activation only, does NOT grant haste — Thousand-Year Elixir)
- `SpendWhiteManaAsRedEffect()` — controller may spend white mana as though it were red mana (static; Sunglasses of Urza). Rides the per-player `ManaPool.isWhiteSpendableAsRed` flag honored by `ManaCost.canPay`/`pay`; set via `GameQueryService.canSpendWhiteManaAsRed`
- `SpendWhiteManaAsAnyColorEffect()` — controller may spend white mana as mana of any color, and other mana only as colorless (static; Celestial Dawn). Rides the per-player `ManaPool.isWhiteSpendableAsAnyColor` flag; `ManaCost` rewrites the pool (non-white colored → colorless, white → the pips the cost needs) before the ordinary payment path
- `GrantActivatedAbilityEffect(ActivatedAbility, GrantScope)` or `(ActivatedAbility, GrantScope, PermanentPredicate)` — grant ability. `GrantScope.SELF_AND_PAIRED` = source + its soulbond partner (Deadeye Navigator). Static land scopes `OWN_LANDS` / `ALL_LANDS` grant to lands you control / every land (Blanket of Night = `{T}: Add {B}` on `ALL_LANDS`); `ALL_LANDS_INCLUDING_SELF` also covers the source land itself (subtype grants only — Urborg, Tomb of Yawgmoth)
- `GrantKeywordEffect(Keyword, GrantScope.SELF_AND_PAIRED)` — grant keyword to source + soulbond partner while paired (Lightning Mauler haste); wrap in `ConditionalEffect(new SourceIsPaired(), …)` on STATIC
- `GrantTriggeredAbilityEffect(EffectSlot, CardEffect, GrantScope)` — continuously grant a *triggered* ability (STATIC slot); Tandem Lookout grants `(ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER, DrawCardEffect(1), SELF_AND_PAIRED)` inside `ConditionalEffect(new SourceIsPaired(), …)`. Unlike `GrantEffectToTargetUntilEndOfTurnEffect` it is layer-recomputed, so it turns off when the condition stops holding. `ON_ATTACK` grants are collected off each attacker in `CombatAttackService` (Thorncaster Sliver: `(ON_ATTACK, DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect(1), ALL_OWN_CREATURES, PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(SLIVER))`)
- `SoulbondChoosePartnerEffect()` — Soulbond self-ETB: choose another unpaired creature you control to pair with (wrap in `MayEffect` on `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`)
- `SoulbondPairWithEnteringEffect()` — Soulbond "another creature enters": may pair this unpaired creature with the entering unpaired creature (`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD`; collector wraps as may)
- `ConditionalEffect(new SourceIsPaired(), wrapped)` — "as long as ~ is paired with another creature"
- `BoostPairedCreatureOfTargetEffect(power, toughness)` — one-shot soulbond rider: "if it's paired with a creature, that creature also gets +X/+Y until end of turn". Boosts the target's `pairedWithId` partner only; pair it with `BoostTargetCreatureEffect` in the same target group (Joint Assault)
- `GrantAdditionalBlockEffect(int)` or `(int, PermanentPredicate controlledFilter)` — block N additional (filter → each controlled permanent matching predicate, e.g. Cenn's Tactician)
- `GrantAdditionalBlockToTargetUntilEndOfTurnEffect(int)` — target creature can block N additional creatures **this turn** (one-shot, until-EOT; SPELL slot). Temporary sibling of `GrantAdditionalBlockEffect`. Act of Heroism. `(int, GrantScope.SELF)` grants it to the source instead (untargeted activated ability — Mounted Archers)
- `RegenerateEffect()` or `(boolean targetsPermanent)` or `(boolean targetsPermanent, boolean opponentMayDrawOnRegenerate)` or `withMinusOneCounterOnRegenerate()` or `enchantedCreature()` or `withGainControlOnRegenerate()` — regenerate. Rider flags tag the shield so secondary effects fire only when it is actually spent: Soldevi Sentry's `opponentMayDrawOnRegenerate` (`Permanent.opponentDrawRegenerationShield`), Matopi Golem's `putMinusOneCounterOnRegenerate` (`Permanent.minusOneCounterRegenerationShield`) and Debt of Loyalty's `gainControlOnRegenerate` (`Permanent.gainControlRegenerationShields` — remembers the spell's controller, who gains permanent control when the shield is spent). Do NOT pair a plain `RegenerateEffect` with a sibling rider effect — that would pay out on activation even if the creature never regenerates. Plain shields are consumed first, so a tagged shield is only spent once it is all that is left. A plain `RegenerateEffect()` on an Aura/Equipment ability is auto-redirected to the attached creature; use `enchantedCreature()` when the source is not always an Aura (Nurturing Licid), so the ability no-ops instead of regenerating itself
- `RegeneratesIfWouldBeDestroyedEffect()` — STATIC self-replacement: "if this creature would be destroyed, regenerate it" — always-on intrinsic regeneration (regenerates every time, no shield consumed); honored by `GraveyardService.tryRegenerate`. Mossbridge Troll
- `PreventTargetCreatureRegenerationThisTurnEffect()` — "target creature can't be regenerated this turn": sets `cantRegenerateThisTurn` on the target so `GraveyardService.tryRegenerate` refuses it for the rest of the turn (beats shields AND intrinsic regen); cleared at turn cleanup. `targetSpec()` narrows to a CREATURE (harmful). Also a `CombatOpponentReferencingEffect` for ON_BLOCK / ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED PER_BLOCKER (Lim-DAdz?l's Cohort). Hurr Jackal
- `PhaseOutSelfAndCombatOpponentEffect()` — "whenever this creature blocks or becomes blocked by a creature, this creature and that creature phase out": a `CombatOpponentReferencingEffect` for ON_BLOCK / ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED PER_BLOCKER. Handler phases out the stack entry's source permanent and its non-targeting target via `PhasingService.phaseOut(...)` — both leave the battlefield for `GameData.phasedOutPermanents` (CR 702.26b), are removed from combat (CR 506.4), and phase in during their own controller's next untap step (CR 702.26a). Dream Fighter
- `PhaseOutEffect(PhaseOutSubject.SOURCE)` — "this creature phases out" with no combat opponent (activated ability). Single-permanent sibling of the above; source plus attachments move to `GameData.phasedOutPermanents` and phase in on its controller’s next untap step. Mist Dragon, Crystal Golem. Also usable as a `ForcedCostOrElseEffect` fallback ("phases out unless you pay {cost}" — Vaporous Djinn)
- `PhaseOutEffect(PhaseOutSubject.ATTACHED)` — "{cost}: Enchanted creature phases out" on an Aura (Vanishing). Non-targeting; finds host via the source's `attachedTo`; the Aura phases out indirectly with the host (CR 702.26g)
- `PhaseOutAttackingCreatureAtEndOfCombatEffect()` — "Whenever a creature you control attacks, it phases out at end of combat" (Teferi's Veil). Put on `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_ATTACKS`; non-targeting — the triggering attacker is the entry's `targetId`. Resolution queues a `PhaseOutAtEndOfCombat` delayed action drained in `CombatService.processEndOfCombatPhaseOuts()`, so the creature still deals its combat damage before phasing out via `PhasingService.phaseOut(...)`
- `PreventPhaseOutEffect(PhaseOutSubject)` — "until your next upkeep, [permanent] can't phase out". `TARGET` = chosen permanent (Spatial Binding, the only subject with a `benign(PERMANENT)` spec); `SOURCE` = the ability's own permanent (Ertai's Familiar, non-targeting). Marks `Permanent.cantPhaseOutUntilUpkeepOf`; `PhasingService` skips marked permanents, `StepTriggerService.handleUpkeepTriggers` clears the mark at the marking player's next upkeep. `ATTACHED` is not supported.
- `PhaseOutEffect(PhaseOutSubject.TARGET)` — "target permanent phases out". The only subject with a `targetSpec()` (generic `harmful(PERMANENT)`); pair with a `PermanentPredicateTargetFilter` to restrict the type (Reality Ripple).
- `PhaseOutPermanentsEffect(PermanentPredicate filter, boolean controllerOnly)` — "all lands you control phase out": mass, non-targeting phase-out of every matching permanent (own battlefield only when `controllerOnly`). Taniwha (`UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`, `PermanentIsLandPredicate`).
- `PhaseOutChosenTypeNontokenPermanentsEffect()` — each upkeep the active player chooses artifact/creature/land/non-Aura enchantment; all nontoken permanents of that type (any controller) phase out. Teferi's Realm (`EACH_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`). Choice via `ChoiceContext.TeferisRealmTypeChoice`.
- `TimeAndTideEffect()` — "simultaneously, all phased-out creatures phase in and all creatures with phasing phase out". Non-targeting; `PhasingService.applyTimeAndTide` collects both halves then moves. Attachments follow (CR 702.26g). Time and Tide
- `EquipoiseEffect()` — upkeep: for each land/artifact/creature target player controls in excess of yours, choose that many of theirs to phase out (lands → artifacts → creatures). `harmful(PLAYER)`; pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(ANY)`. Equipoise
- `PreventRegenerationOfCreaturesDamagedBySourceThisTurnEffect()` — until end of turn, the source (or targeted creature) gains "Creatures dealt damage by this creature this turn can't be regenerated this turn": sets `damagedCreaturesCantRegenerateThisTurn` on that permanent, and `GraveyardService.tryRegenerate` refuses any creature that source damaged this turn (`GameData.creatureCardsDamagedThisTurnBySourcePermanent`, so pre-activation damage counts). Cleared at turn cleanup. Bone Shaman / Runesword
- `ProtectionFromColorsEffect(Set<CardColor>)` — protection from colors (static)
- `ProtectionFromSubtypesEffect(Set<CardSubtype>[, boolean creatureSourcesOnly])` — protection from subtypes (static); `creatureSourcesOnly=true` = "protection from [subtype] creatures" (source must also be a creature)
- `GrantProtectionFromChosenTypeToOwnCreaturesEffect(CardSubtype recipientSubtype)` — STATIC: creatures you control with `recipientSubtype` have protection from creatures of the source's chosen type; pair with `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD ChooseSubtypeOnEnterEffect()` (Riders of Gavony)
- `ProtectionFromManaValueEffect(int minManaValue)` — protection from sources with mana value ≥ N (static, Mistmeadow Skulk)
- `ProtectionFromEverythingEffect()` — protection from everything: every source, any colour/type/subtype/mana value (static, Progenitus)
- `GrantSubtypeToTargetCreatureEffect(CardSubtype)` — target creature "becomes a [subtype] in addition to its other types" (permanent, added to `grantedSubtypes`)
- `GrantSubtypeUntilEndOfTurnEffect(CardSubtype, GrantScope)` — additively grants a creature subtype until end of turn; `TARGET_PLAYERS_CREATURES` snapshots each creature controlled by the targeted player and records temporary layered grants. Curious Colossus.
- `TargetCreatureBecomesSubtypeUntilEndOfTurnEffect(CardSubtype)` — target creature **becomes** the given creature type until end of turn, **replacing** all its other creature types (Boldwyr Intimidator: "target creature becomes a Coward"). Sets `Permanent.transientCreatureTypeOverride`, read by the layered pass; contrast the additive/permanent `GrantSubtypeToTargetCreatureEffect`. The `(CardSubtype, GrantScope)` overload widens it to a group: `TARGET_PLAYERS_CREATURES` (Polymorphist's Jest) or `OWN_CREATURES` (every creature you control, no target — Dragonshift overloaded)
- `SourceBecomesSubtypeUntilEndOfTurnEffect(CardSubtype)` — self analog (no target): the **source** permanent becomes the given creature type until end of turn, replacing all its other creature types (Paragon of the Amesha: "this creature becomes an Angel"). Pair with `BoostSelfEffect` + `GrantKeywordEffect(..., GrantScope.SELF)` for "becomes a [type], gets +X/+X, and gains [keywords]"
- `TargetCreatureBecomesSubtypeUntilEndOfTurnEffect(CardSubtype)` — target creature **becomes** the given creature type until end of turn, **replacing** all its other creature types (Boldwyr Intimidator: "target creature becomes a Coward"). Sets `Permanent.transientCreatureTypeOverride`, read by the layered pass; contrast the additive/permanent `GrantSubtypeToTargetCreatureEffect`
- `SourceBecomesSubtypeUntilEndOfTurnEffect(CardSubtype)` or `(List<CardSubtype>)` — self analog (no target): the **source** permanent becomes the given creature type(s) until end of turn, replacing all its other creature types (Paragon of the Amesha: "this creature becomes an Angel"). Pair with `BoostSelfEffect` + `GrantKeywordEffect(..., GrantScope.SELF)` for "becomes a [type], gets +X/+X, and gains [keywords]"
- `GrantBasicLandTypeToTargetEffect(EffectDuration[, CardSubtype fixedSubtype][, boolean replacing])` — target land becomes a chosen basic land type. Default adds "in addition to its other types" (Navigator's Compass / Aquitect's Will); `replacing=true` makes the land **become** the type, losing its others per rule 305.7 (Tideshaper Mystic, UNTIL_END_OF_TURN; Orcish Farmer = fixed SWAMP, UNTIL_CONTROLLERS_NEXT_UNTAP_STEP — survives end of turn, cleared at the land controller's next untap step)
- `NonbasicLandsBecomeTypeEffect(CardSubtype)` — STATIC, global: every nonbasic land (any controller) becomes the basic land type, losing its other land types/abilities and producing that type's mana per rule 305.7. Basic lands unaffected. Blood Moon (MOUNTAIN)
- `LoseAllLandTypesEffect(GrantScope, PermanentPredicate)` — STATIC layer-4 effect: matching lands lose all land subtypes while retaining their other card types; the layer-6 `LosesAllAbilitiesEffect` and a `GrantActivatedAbilityEffect` can be composed with it for Alpine Moon.
- `ControlledLandsBecomeTypeEffect(CardSubtype)` — STATIC: every land **you** control (basic or nonbasic) becomes that basic land type, losing its other land types/abilities per rule 305.7. Other players' lands unaffected. Celestial Dawn (PLAINS)
- `LandsOfSubtypeBecomeTypeEffect(CardSubtype from, CardSubtype to)` — STATIC, global: every land (any controller, basic or nonbasic) carrying the `from` land subtype becomes the `to` basic land type, losing its other land types/abilities and producing that type's mana per rule 305.7. Lands lacking `from` are unaffected. Layer-4 (`LayerSystemService`), managed like Blood Moon. Conversion ("All Mountains are Plains" = `(MOUNTAIN, PLAINS)`)
- `BoostCreaturesOfChosenSubtypeEffect(p, t[, CounterType scalingCounter[, boolean allControllers[, boolean excludeSelf]]])` — STATIC: creatures of the source's `chosenSubtype` get +p/+t. `allControllers=true` boosts every controller's (Brass Herald); `excludeSelf=true` / `BoostCreaturesOfChosenSubtypeEffect.otherOwnCreatures(p, t)` = "**other** creatures you control of the chosen type" (Adaptive Automaton). `scalingCounter` multiplies by that counter's count on the source (Door of Destinies)
- `GrantChosenSubtypeToOwnCreaturesEffect([boolean affectsAllZones[, GrantScope scope]])` / `.toSelf()` — STATIC layer 4: creatures you control (Xenograft; `affectsAllZones=true` also covers stack/hand/graveyard/library/exile — Arcane Adaptation) or, with `GrantScope.SELF` via `toSelf()`, only the source itself (Adaptive Automaton) are the source's `chosenSubtype` in addition to their other types
- `GrantChosenBasicLandTypeToOwnLandsEffect()` — STATIC layers 4 and 6: lands you control gain the source's chosen basic land type in addition to their other types and its intrinsic mana ability. Pair with `ChooseBasicLandTypeOnEnterEffect()` (Realmwright)
- `BasicLandsOfChosenTypesBecomeTypeEffect()` — STATIC, global: basic lands of the source's `chosenSubtype` become its `secondChosenSubtype` (rule 305.7). Pair with `ChooseBasicLandTypeOnEnterEffect(2)`. Nonbasics unaffected. Illusionary Terrain
- `PermanentsMatchingLoseSupertypeEffect(filter, supertype)` — STATIC, global and symmetric: matching permanents lose the supertype. Melting = `(PermanentIsLandPredicate(), CardSupertype.SNOW)`. Read via `GameQueryService.hasEffectiveSupertype`, not via the static accumulator
- `SetTargetPermanentSupertypeEffect(supertype, gained)` — target permanent gains/loses a supertype for as long as it stays on the battlefield (Arcum's Weathervane: snow land is no longer snow / nonsnow basic land becomes snow). Stored per-permanent, read via `GameQueryService.hasEffectiveSupertype`; narrow legal targets with the ability's `TargetFilter`
- `OwnLandsBecomeChosenTypeUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — no target: prompts the controller for a basic land type, then each land they control **becomes** that type until end of turn (type-replacing per rule 305.7, reuses `applyBasicLandType(..., replacing=true)`). Applied once at resolution to lands controlled then. Elsewhere Flask
- `LandsOfChosenTypeBecomeChosenBasicTypeUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — no target: prompts for a land type then a basic land type; each land (any controller) of the first type **becomes** the second until end of turn (rule 305.7). Vision Charm
- `TargetLandBecomesBasicLandTypeUntilSourceLeavesEffect(CardSubtype)` (ability, benign LAND) + `TrackedLandsBecomeBasicLandTypeEffect(CardSubtype)` (STATIC) — a target land becomes a fixed basic land type until the source leaves. The ability records the target's id and subtype on the source permanent; the STATIC companion applies each recorded type in layer 4, so the grant ends automatically when the source leaves. Gaea's Liege uses FOREST; Graceful Antelope uses PLAINS
- `LoseAllCreatureTypesEffect(GrantScope)` — creatures lose all creature types until end of turn; `TARGET` = single creature (Amoeboid Changeling), `TARGET_PLAYERS_CREATURES` = all creatures target player controls (Ego Erasure); "gains all creature types" = `GrantKeywordEffect(Keyword.CHANGELING, sameScope)`
- `LoseSubtypesUntilEndOfTurnEffect(Set<CardSubtype>)` — source permanent loses the given subtypes until end of turn (self-targeting). Haunted Plate Mail "that's no longer an Equipment". Writes `Permanent.transientRemovedSubtypes`; equip while not Equipment has no effect; CR 704.5p unattaches if no longer Aura/Equipment
- `RemoveCardTypeFromTargetPermanentEffect(CardType)` — target permanent loses the given card type until end of turn. Registers a timestamped layer-4 floating effect, so later type-changing effects can add the type again.
- **Paradigm** (`Keyword.PARADIGM` on card, not an effect) — engine handled by `ParadigmService`: first resolve exiles spell + registers `GameData.ParadigmDelayedTrigger`; each precombat main fires `ParadigmCastCopyEffect` → copy in exile + `ParadigmMayCastFromExileEffect` may-cast (`ParadigmCastSupport`)
- `BushidoEffect(DynamicAmount amount)` / `(int amount)` — a Bushido ability, used by the combat trigger slots so other cards can distinguish Bushido from unrelated block triggers. `asBoost()` yields the `BoostSelfEffect(amount, amount)` the trigger resolves. Fixed N for every CHK Bushido creature; `PermanentCount(IsAttacking, ANY_PLAYER)` for bushido X (Fumiko the Lowblood)
- `GrantEffectToSourceUntilEndOfTurnEffect(EffectSlot slot, CardEffect grantedEffect)` — grants the SOURCE permanent a temporary triggered ability until end of turn ("this creature gains '…' until end of turn"). Non-targeting — the recipient is the resolving entry's source permanent. Stored via `Permanent.addTemporaryTriggeredEffect`, cleared by `resetModifiers()` at cleanup. Self-scoped sibling of `GrantEffectToTargetEffect`. Cruel Deceiver

## Combat restrictions / evasion

- `CantBeBlockedEffect()` — unblockable (static)
- `CantBeBlockedByFewerThanNCreaturesEffect(int minBlockers)` — generalized menace: can't be blocked except by N+ creatures (static). Menace = 2; Guile = 3
- `CantBeBlockedByCreaturesMatchingPredicateEffect(PermanentPredicate blockerPredicate)` / `(blockerPredicate, onlyIfDefenderControls)` — can't be blocked by blockers matching the predicate (static). Optional second arg gates the restriction on defender controlling a matching permanent (Arctic Foxes = power dz?A2 only if snow land). Taoist Mystic = `PermanentHasKeywordPredicate(HORSEMANSHIP)`
- `CantBeBlockedByCreaturesWithLessPowerEffect()` — STATIC: creatures with power less than this creature's power can't block it (Shrill Howler / Howling Chorus). Compared against effective powers at declaration in `GameQueryService.findBlockDenial`
- `CantBeBlockedByCreaturesWithPowerLessThanIslandCountEffect()` — STATIC: creatures with power less than the number of Islands controlled by this creature's controller can't block it (Kraken of the Straits). Compared against current effective powers and Island count at declaration in `BlockLegalityService`
- `CantBeBlockedIfAttackingAloneEffect()` — can't be blocked while attacking alone (static)
- `AssignCombatDamageAsThoughUnblockedEffect()` — while blocked, may assign combat damage as though unblocked (Rhox/Thorn Elemental) (static)
- `DefensiveFormationEffect()` — the defending player assigns combat damage dealt by creatures attacking them (static)
- `AssignCombatDamageToDefendingCreatureWhenUnblockedEffect()` — while unblocked, may assign all combat damage to one defending creature instead of the player (Cunning Giant) (static). Prompts the attacker via the combat-damage-assignment interaction when the defender has a creature; single recipient only.
- `AssignNoCombatDamageEffect()` — the stack entry's source permanent (`sourcePermanentId`) assigns no combat damage this turn (`creaturesPreventedFromDealingCombatDamage`). Pair inside `MayEffect(SequenceEffect.of(DealDamage…, AssignNoCombatDamageEffect()))` for Gaze of Pain.
- `AssignNoCombatDamageToEnchantedCreatureEffect()` — the source Aura's enchanted creature assigns no combat damage this turn. Pair with `EnchantedCreatureDealsPowerDamageToTargetCreatureEffect()` inside `MayEffect(SequenceEffect.of(...))` for Laccolith Rig.
- `AssignNoCombatDamageAndDefendingPlayerDiscardsEffect()` — the trigger's source (attacking) creature assigns no combat damage this turn (added to `creaturesPreventedFromDealingCombatDamage`) and the defending player (trigger `targetId`) discards a card at random. Wrap in `MayEffect` for "you may". Used by Cloak of Confusion on `ON_ENCHANTED_CREATURE_ATTACKS_UNBLOCKED`.
- `CantBlockEffect()` — can't block (static)
- `CantBlockUnlessEffect(Condition condition, String requirementDescription)` — STATIC: can't block unless `condition` is met (Marauding Boneslasher: `new ControlsOtherPermanentCount(1, new PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(ZOMBIE))`, "you control another Zombie"). Block-only counterpart of `CantAttackUnlessEffect`; the block half of `CantAttackOrBlockUnlessEffect` WITHOUT restricting attacks. Facet `cantBlockUnless()` on `BlockingRestrictionEffect`; evaluated in `GameQueryService.canBlock`.
- `CantBlockCreaturesWithPowerGreaterOrEqualToOwnToughnessEffect()` — STATIC self-referential restriction (Ironclaw Curse): this creature can't block an attacker whose effective power dz?A this creature's own effective toughness. Evaluated in `GameQueryService.findBlockDenial`; recognized on the blocker's own STATIC effects or via an aura attached to it (`hasAuraWithEffect`). Facet on `BlockingRestrictionEffect` (`cantBlockCreaturesWithPowerAtLeastOwnToughness()`). Pair with `StaticBoostEffect(0, -1, ENCHANTED_CREATURE)` for the full Aura.
- `CantBlockCreaturesWithPowerAtLeastEffect(int minAttackerPower)` — STATIC hard fixed-threshold restriction (Ironclaw Orcs, `(2)`): this creature can't block an attacker with effective power dz?A `minAttackerPower`. Facet `cantBlockCreaturesWithPowerAtLeast()` on `BlockingRestrictionEffect`; evaluated in `GameQueryService.findBlockDenial`.
- `MustAttackEffect()` — must attack (static)
- `MustAttackEffect(GrantScope.ALL_OWN_CREATURES)` — STATIC "creatures you control attack each combat if able", source included (Hellraiser Goblin). `OWN_CREATURES` excludes the source. Prefer this over `MatchingCreaturesMustAttackEffect` for controller-relative wordings — that matcher has no source-controller context
- `ConditionalEffect(condition, new MustAttackEffect())` — STATIC self must-attack gated on a condition ("attacks each combat if able unless …"): counted in `AttackLegalityService.getMustAttackRequirementCount` only when the condition is met for the creature's controller. Marauding Maulhorn = `NotCondition(new ControlsPermanent(PermanentAllOfPredicate(PermanentIsCreaturePredicate, PermanentNamedPredicate("Advocate of the Beast"))))`
- `MustAttackIfAnotherCreatureAttacksEffect()` — STATIC conditional requirement (Ekundu Cyclops): must attack if able whenever another creature you control is declared as an attacker. Validated against the declared set in `CombatAttackService`, not via requirement counts
- `MatchingCreaturesMustAttackEffect(PermanentPredicate matcher)` — STATIC global: every creature matching `matcher` (any controller) must attack each combat if able. Global analogue of self-only `MustAttackEffect`. Goblin Assault (`PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(GOBLIN)`). The matcher is evaluated with the source permanent's card id and controller in the `FilterContext`, so it may be source-relative: Goblin Rabblemaster's "other Goblin creatures you control" = `PermanentAllOfPredicate(IsCreature, HasSubtype(GOBLIN), PermanentControlledBySourceControllerPredicate, Not(PermanentIsSourceCardPredicate))`
- `MatchingCreaturesMustAttackThisTurnEffect(PermanentPredicate matcher)` — one-shot, non-targeted: every creature matching `matcher` (any controller, predicate evaluated with source controller) must attack this turn if able (sets transient `mustAttackThisTurn`, cleared at end of turn). One-shot analogue of static `MatchingCreaturesMustAttackEffect`. "creatures your opponents control attack this turn if able" = `PermanentNotPredicate(PermanentControlledBySourceControllerPredicate())` (Suicidal Charge)
- `TargetPlayersCreaturesMustAttackThisTurnEffect()` — targets a player: every creature that player controls must attack this turn if able (may attack any legal target — not "attack you"). Sets transient `mustAttackThisTurn` on each of the target player's creatures at resolution, cleared at end of turn. Player-targeted analogue of the non-targeted `MatchingCreaturesMustAttackThisTurnEffect`; SPELL slot, `benign(PLAYER)` spec. Imaginary Threats
- `SetCombatRequirementThisTurnEffect(CombatRequirement[, GrantScope scope])` — **the one-shot targeted combat requirements**, one constant per transient `Permanent` flag, all cleared at end of turn. `MUST_ATTACK` "attacks this turn if able", any legal defender (Incite, Imp's Taunt, Heckling Fiends, Courtly Provocateur, Chemister's Trick, Norritt) · `MUST_ATTACK_EFFECT_CONTROLLER` "attacks **you** this turn if able", also sets `mustAttackTargetId` to the ability's controller so planeswalkers aren't legal defenders (Alluring Siren) · `MUST_BLOCK` "blocks this turn if able" with no specific attacker — unlike Provoke's `MustBlockSourceEffect`; enforced in `CombatBlockService.validateMustBlockIfAbleRequirements` (Nacatl Hunt-Pride, Mark for Death) · `MUST_BE_BLOCKED` "must be blocked this turn if able", one blocker suffices (Enlarge, Emergent Growth, Deadly Allure) · `MUST_BE_BLOCKED_BY_ALL` one-shot Lure (Alluring Scent, Taunting Challenge, Revenge of the Hunted, Lead). `benign(CREATURE)` spec; `MUST_BLOCK` also carries `PermanentIsCreaturePredicate`. `scope` defaults to `GrantScope.TARGET`; pass `GrantScope.ALL_OWN_CREATURES` for the untargeted sweep "each creature you control … must be blocked this turn if able" (Joraga Invocation), which declares `TargetSpec.NONE`. CR 508.1d / CR 509.1c — no cost payment is required to obey. For the *printed* versions use the STATIC siblings `MustAttackEffect` / `MustBeBlockedIfAbleEffect` / `MustBeBlockedByAllCreaturesEffect` instead
- `SetCombatRequirementThisTurnEffect(CombatRequirement)` — **the one-shot targeted combat requirements**, one constant per transient `Permanent` flag, all cleared at end of turn. `MUST_ATTACK` "attacks this turn if able", any legal defender (Incite, Imp's Taunt, Heckling Fiends, Courtly Provocateur, Chemister's Trick, Norritt) · `MUST_ATTACK_EFFECT_CONTROLLER` "attacks **you** this turn if able", also sets `mustAttackTargetId` to the ability's controller so planeswalkers aren't legal defenders (Alluring Siren) · `MUST_BLOCK` "blocks this turn if able" with no specific attacker — unlike Provoke's `MustBlockSourceEffect`; enforced in `CombatBlockService.validateMustBlockIfAbleRequirements` (Nacatl Hunt-Pride, Mark for Death) · `MUST_ATTACK_OR_BLOCK` "attacks or blocks this combat if able" — collapses at resolution to `MUST_ATTACK` when the target's controller is the active player and to `MUST_BLOCK` otherwise, since no player does both in one combat (Boros Battleshaper) · `MUST_BE_BLOCKED` "must be blocked this turn if able", one blocker suffices (Enlarge, Emergent Growth, Deadly Allure) · `MUST_BE_BLOCKED_BY_ALL` one-shot Lure (Alluring Scent, Taunting Challenge, Revenge of the Hunted, Lead). `benign(CREATURE)` spec; `MUST_BLOCK` also carries `PermanentIsCreaturePredicate`. CR 508.1d / CR 509.1c — no cost payment is required to obey. For the *printed* versions use the STATIC siblings `MustAttackEffect` / `MustBeBlockedIfAbleEffect` / `MustBeBlockedByAllCreaturesEffect` instead
- `MustAttackUnlessControllerPaysManaValueEffect()` — punisher variant of Norritt's second ability (Arcum's Whistle): the active player may pay {X} (X = the target creature's mana value); declining or accepting without enough mana applies `SetCombatRequirementThisTurnEffect(CombatRequirement.MUST_ATTACK)` + `DestroyTargetIfDidNotAttackAtEndStepEffect()`. `benign(CREATURE)` spec; pair with the Norritt target filter + `ActivationTimingRestriction.BEFORE_ATTACKERS_DECLARED`
- `DefendingPlayerChoosesCreatureToBlockEffect()` — non-targeting attack trigger: the defending player chooses one untapped creature they control at resolution, and that creature must block the triggering attacker this turn if able. With no eligible creatures, it resolves without a choice. Crashing Boars
- `MustAttackNextTurnEffect(TauntTarget)` — targets a player: during their next turn every creature they control attacks the stored object if able, via `GameData.tauntedNextTurn`. `EFFECT_CONTROLLER` = "attacks you" (Taunt, SPELL slot). `SOURCE_PERMANENT` = attacks the ability's **source permanent** (Gideon Jura +2), storing `entry.getSourcePermanentId()` and no-opping when there is none — planeswalker permanent ids are already valid attack targets, so the requirement lapses on its own if the source leaves. Player-targeting spec
- `TargetCreatureMustAttackSourcePermanentNextTurnEffect()` — targets one creature: during its controller's next turn it attacks the ability's **source permanent** if able (Gideon, Battle-Forged +2). The single-creature sibling of `MustAttackNextTurnEffect(SOURCE_PERMANENT)`, which taunts a whole player's board. Recorded in `GameData.creatureMustAttackPermanentNextTurn` and promoted at that player's turn start onto `mustAttackThisTurn` / `mustAttackTargetId`; lapses if the source is no longer attackable
- `SirensCallEffect()` — SPELL, non-targeted: every creature the active player controls must attack this turn if able, and at the next end step all non-Wall creatures they control that didn't attack (and weren't summoning sick) are destroyed. Siren's Call. Pair with `setSpellCastTimingRestriction(OPPONENTS_TURN_BEFORE_ATTACKERS)`. Queues a `DestroyNonAttackersAtEndStep` delayed action. Also reusable in an ACTIVATED_ABILITY (Maddening Imp's `{T}` with `ONLY_DURING_OPPONENTS_TURN_BEFORE_COMBAT`) — the "non-Wall" wording is already covered by the end-step Wall exclusion
- `MustBeBlockedIfAbleEffect()` — must be blocked (static)
- `MustBeBlockedByAllCreaturesEffect()` / `(PermanentPredicate blockerFilter)` — Lure (static); null/no-arg = all able creatures; pass e.g. `PermanentHasKeywordPredicate(FLYING)` for "all creatures with flying able to block this do so" (Talruum Piper)
- `CantBeBlockedUnlessAllDefendingCreaturesBlockEffect()` — attacker-side evasion: a block is legal only when every creature the defending player controls is assigned to this attacker; zero blockers remains legal (Tromokratis)
- `MustBlockTargetCreatureEffect()` — two-target spell: blocker group (0) must block blocked group (1) this turn if able (Hunt Down)
- `MustBlockEachCombatEffect()` — STATIC self-only "this creature blocks each combat if able" (Watchdog). Permanent requirement read off the creature's own STATIC effects each combat, unlike the turn-scoped `SetCombatRequirementThisTurnEffect(CombatRequirement.MUST_BLOCK)`; same enforcement path
- `AllCreaturesMustBlockEachCombatEffect()` — STATIC global "all creatures block each combat if able" requirement. The declare-blockers validator checks every legally able defending creature; it is composed with `AttackingPlayerChoosesBlockersEffect()` for Invasion Plans
- `AttackingPlayerChoosesBlockersEffect()` — STATIC global control effect: the active/attacking player declares the defending player's blockers. The defending player still owns the blockers and pays any block costs
- `EachOpponentCreatureBlocksThisTurnIfAbleEffect()` — non-targeted mass version: every creature your opponents control must block this turn if able (Predatory Rampage)
- `CreaturesWithCounterMustBlockTriggeringAttackerEffect(CounterType)` — `ON_ANY_CREATURE_ATTACKS` mass Provoke: every creature with that counter must block the triggering attacker this turn if able (adds it to `mustBlockIds`). Wrap in `TriggeringPermanentConditionalEffect(new PermanentHasCountersPredicate(type), …)` (Magnetic Web)
- `CreaturesWithCounterAttackTogetherEffect(CounterType)` — STATIC: if a creature with that counter attacks, all creatures with that counter attack if able. Validated at declaration time in `CombatAttackService.validateCounterBearersAttackTogether`, waived under an attack tax (Magnetic Web)
- `EnchantedCreatureCantAttackOrBlockEffect(boolean preventsAttacking, boolean preventsBlocking)` — Aura STATIC prohibition no payment lifts. No-arg ctor = `(true, true)`, the Pacifism wording; `(true, false)` is "can't attack" with blocking still allowed (Forced Worship, Weight of Conscience). All-false rejected by the compact constructor
- `EnchantedCreatureCantTransformEffect()` — Bound by Moonsilver: enchanted creature can't transform (static; checked in `isTransformPrevented`)
- `EnchantedCreatureCantAttackOrBlockAloneEffect(boolean restrictsBlocking)` — Aura static: enchanted creature can't attack alone; when `restrictsBlocking` is true it also can't block alone
- `EnchantedCreatureCanOnlyAttackAloneEffect()` — Errantry: enchanted creature can attack only if it's the sole attacker (static)
- `CanOnlyAttackAloneEffect()` — Master of Cruelties: STATIC self-only "this creature can only attack alone"; same declaration-time check in `CombatAttackService.validateCanOnlyAttackAlone`
- `ExileEnchantedCreatureEffect()` — exile the creature the source Aura is attached to (exile variant of `SacrificeEnchantedCreatureEffect`); use in an Aura's activated ability (Weight of Conscience). Pairs with the `TapTwoCreaturesSharingTypeCost` activated-ability cost.
- `SacrificeEnchantedCreatureOnLeaveEffect()` — "When this Aura leaves the battlefield, that creature's controller sacrifices it." Use in `ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD` on a reanimation Aura (Animate Dead, Necromancy); the enchanted creature's permanent id is baked in at trigger time so it resolves after the Aura is gone.
- `EnchantedControllerSacrificesCreatureOnLeaveEffect()` — "When enchanted creature leaves the battlefield, its controller sacrifices a creature of their choice." Use in `ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD` on an Aura (Funeral March); the leaving creature's controller (not the Aura controller) is baked in at trigger time and sacrifices at resolution.
- `NinjutsuEffect(UUID attackTargetId)` — resolution half of ninjutsu (CR 702.49a): moves the source card from its controller's hand onto the battlefield tapped and attacking `attackTargetId`. Never write it directly — use `Card.addNinjutsu("{2}{U}{U}")`, which registers the hand ability and lets `AbilityActivationService.activateNinjutsuAbility` pay the cost (bounce an unblocked attacker you control) and snapshot the real defender onto the effect. Higure, the Still Wind
- `ReturnSourceAuraToChosenCreatureOnLeaveEffect()` / `(UUID leavingPermanentControllerId)` — returns this Aura from its owner's graveyard when the enchanted permanent leaves the battlefield; the leaving permanent's former controller chooses a legal creature to enchant. Use in `ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD`.
- `MakeCreatureUnblockableEffect()` — target unblockable this turn
- `MakeAllCreaturesUnblockableEffect()` / `MakeAllCreaturesUnblockableEffect.ownCreatures()` — mass "can't be blocked this turn": every creature on every battlefield (Venser, the Sojourner), or only the resolving controller's creatures (Glaring Spotlight)
- `IgnoreOpponentCreatureHexproofEffect()` — STATIC: opponents' hexproof creatures can be targeted by your spells and abilities as though they didn't have hexproof (Glaring Spotlight). Shroud/protection untouched
- `IgnoreOpponentHexproofUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — activated ability: until end of turn, your spells and abilities can target opponents and their hexproof creatures as though they didn't have hexproof (Detection Tower). Shroud/protection untouched
- `CanBeBlockedOnlyByFilterEffect(PermanentPredicate blockerPredicate, String allowedBlockersDescription)` — static evasion on the source: it can be blocked only by blockers matching the predicate (Fear-like, e.g. Dread Warlock = black creatures)
- `GrantCanBeBlockedOnlyByFilterToOwnCreaturesEffect(PermanentPredicate creatureFilter, PermanentPredicate blockerPredicate, String allowedBlockersDescription)` — SPELL one-shot: your creatures matching `creatureFilter` (null = all) can be blocked only by blockers matching `blockerPredicate` until end of turn. Dread Charge = both filters `PermanentColorInPredicate(BLACK)`. Affected creatures snapshotted at resolution; restriction stored transiently on each `Permanent`
- `MakeCreatureBlockableOnlyByFilterThisTurnEffect(PermanentPredicate blockerPredicate, String allowedBlockersDescription)` / `(…, true)` — one-shot: creature can be blocked only by blockers matching `blockerPredicate` until end of turn. Default targets a creature (Joven's Tools = `PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(WALL)`, "Walls"); pass `selfTargeting=true` for "this creature" trigger payoffs (Rhonas's Stalwart: `PermanentPowerAtLeastPredicate(3)` for "can't be blocked by power 2 or less"). Sibling of `GrantCanBeBlockedOnlyByFilterToOwnCreaturesEffect`
- `MatchingCreaturesCantBlockMatchingCreaturesEffect(PermanentPredicate blockerPredicate, PermanentPredicate attackerPredicate, String description)` — global STATIC: while the source is on the battlefield, any creature matching `blockerPredicate` can't block any creature matching `attackerPredicate`, board-wide (Boldwyr Intimidator: "Cowards can't block Warriors"). Both predicates are evaluated against the source's own `FilterContext`, so either side may be controller-relative — `PermanentControlledBySourceControllerPredicate` as the attacker predicate gives "… can't block creatures you control" (Bower Passage). Evaluated in `BlockLegalityService.findBlockDenial`
- `MatchingCreaturesCantBlockMatchingCreaturesUnlessPaysLifeEffect(PermanentPredicate blockerPredicate, PermanentPredicate attackerPredicate, int lifePerBlocker, String description)` — global STATIC life tax (Heat Wave): matching blockers can't block matching attackers unless the defender pays `lifePerBlocker` life per unique qualifying blocker. `GlobalBlockLifeCostEffect`; charged in `CombatBlockService` via `GameQueryService.getGlobalBlockLifeTax`
- `MatchingCreaturesCantAttackOrBlockEffect(PermanentPredicate affectedPredicate, String description)` — global STATIC: while the source is on the battlefield, any creature matching `affectedPredicate` can't attack OR block, board-wide. Predicate is evaluated relative to the source's controller (`sourceControllerId`/`sourceCardId` set), so source-relative predicates like `PermanentControlledBySourceControllerPredicate` resolve "you"/"your opponents". Kulrath Knight: "Creatures your opponents control with counters on them can't attack or block" = `PermanentAllOfPredicate(PermanentNotPredicate(PermanentControlledBySourceControllerPredicate()), PermanentHasCountersPredicate(ANY))`. Light of Day: "Black creatures can't attack or block" = `PermanentColorInPredicate(BLACK)`. Attack side in `CombatAttackService.isCantAttackDueToGlobalRestriction`, block side in `GameQueryService` (`canBlock` + `buildBlockerFacts`)
- `MatchingPermanentsCantActivateTapAbilitiesEffect(PermanentPredicate affectedPredicate)` — global STATIC: while the source is on the battlefield, activated abilities with `{T}` in their costs of matching permanents can't be activated (non-tap abilities stay usable). Board-wide sibling of `EnchantedCreatureCantActivateTapAbilitiesEffect`. Katabatic Winds (`PermanentHasKeywordPredicate(FLYING)`). Enforced via `GameQueryService.isLockedFromActivatingTapAbilities`
- `CantAttackThisTurnEffect(TapUntapScope scope[, PermanentPredicate filter])` — creature(s) can't attack this turn (one-shot, sets `Permanent.cantAttackThisTurn`). Scopes: `TARGET` (target creature, multi-target-group), `ALL_CREATURES` (mass, filtered). Attack-side sibling of `CantBlockThisTurnEffect`; pair the two for "target creature can't attack or block this turn" (Alchemist's Vial). NOT the static `CantAttackUnlessEffect` family.
- `CantBlockThisTurnEffect(TapUntapScope scope[, PermanentPredicate filter])` — creature(s) can't block this turn (one-shot). `TARGET` (target creature, multi-target-group), `TARGET_PLAYERS_PERMANENTS` (target player's / targeted planeswalker's controller's creatures), `TARGET_CONTROLLERS_OTHER_CREATURES` (creature target: every OTHER creature its controller controls — Mark for Death), `ALL_CREATURES` (mass, filtered). NOT the static `CantBlockEffect()`.
- `TargetPlayerChoosesCreatureRestCantBlockEffect()` — SPELL, player-targeting spec: the targeted player chooses one creature they control (kept able to block); all their OTHER creatures can't block this turn. Pair with a `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT)`. 0-1 creatures ⇒ no choice, resolves harmlessly (Goblin War Cry).
- `TargetPlayerChoosesCreaturesToAttackNextTurnEffect()` — player-targeting spec: the targeted player chooses any number of creatures they control; during their NEXT turn only those creatures may attack and they attack if able, and each of them that didn't attack is destroyed at that turn's end step. `GameData.chosenAttackersNextTurn` → `chosenAttackersThisTurn`. Empty choice = no creature may attack. Pair with a `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT)` (Oracle en-Vec).
- `ControlledCreaturesCanBlockAsThoughUntappedEffect()` — `TappedBlockPermissionEffect`; STATIC, tapped creatures you control can block as though they were untapped. Controller-relative (`PermanentControlledBySourceControllerPredicate`), so the permission follows control changes. Masako the Humorless

- `CantBlockThisCombatEffect()` — targeted creature can't block any creature for the current combat; the marker is cleared by `Permanent.clearCombatState()` at end of combat. Use for the exact "can't block this combat" wording, not `CantBlockSourceEffect`, which only forbids blocking one source.

## Tap / untap

- `SimultaneouslyFlipAllPermanentsTapStatesEffect(filter)` - SPELL/ability: snapshot every matching permanent on every battlefield, untap the tapped matches, and tap the untapped matches so the two halves do not cascade (Breaking Wave)
- `EnchantedCreatureDealsDamageEqualToDealtDamageToControllerEffect()` / `(boolean auraIsSource)` — deals xValue damage to the enchanted creature's controller (ON_ENCHANTED_CREATURE_DEALT_DAMAGE = damage it was dealt, Spiteful Shadows; ON_ENCHANTED_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_YOU = damage it dealt to the Aura's controller, Backfire). `auraIsSource=true` = the Aura deals the damage rather than the creature (Binding Agony)
- `ReflectDamageToChosenColorCreatureEffect(PermanentPredicate damagedPermanentFilter)` — watcher (ON_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_YOU_OR_YOUR_PERMANENT): whenever a creature of the source's chosen color damages you or a matching permanent you control, the source deals that much damage back to that creature (Mangara's Equity). Pair with `ChooseColorOnEnterEffect`
- `ReflectSourceDamageToItsControllerEffect(CardColor.RED)` — global watcher (ON_ANY_SOURCE_DEALS_DAMAGE): whenever any red creature or spell deals damage, this permanent deals that much to that source's controller (Justice). Damage from one source is summed into one reflection (CR ruling)
- `TapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.TARGET)` — tap target · `(TARGET, filter)` — the filter is the targeting restriction (mirrors `UntapPermanentsEffect`); e.g. Grasp of the Hieromancer's `PermanentAllOf(IsCreature, ControlledByDefendingPlayer)`
- `TapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.TARGET)` — tap target
- `TapTargetThenUntapAnotherTargetEffect()` — "tap target permanent, then untap another target permanent" (Ral Zarek +1); two permanent filters with `minTargets = maxTargets = 2` (duplicate targets already rejected = "another")
- `DestroyTargetCreatureUnlessControllerPaysToughnessLifeEffect()` — destroy target creature unless its controller pays life equal to its toughness; can't be regenerated (Essence Vortex). `RemovalEffect` (DESTROY). Punisher: the **target creature's controller** decides (pay-life vs. destroy; can't-pay auto-destroys). Cost is the effective toughness at resolution
- `DestroyCreaturesThatDamagedSourceUnlessControllerPaysLifeEffect(int lifeCost)` — ON_DEATH punisher (Giant Albatross, 2): for each creature that dealt damage to the source this turn, destroy it unless **its controller** pays N life; can't be regenerated. One independent prompt per creature (APNAP), can't-pay auto-destroys. Wrap in `MayPayManaEffect` for the "you may pay" gate. Reads `GameData.creatureCardsDamagedBySourceThatDiedThisTurn`, NOT the live `creatureCardsDamagedThisTurnBySourcePermanent` (the dying card is purged from that as death triggers are collected)
- `TapTargetCreatureUnlessControllerPaysLifeEffect(int lifeCost)` — tap target creature unless its controller pays N life (Vectis Dominator, 2). Punisher: the **target creature's controller** decides (pay-life vs. tap; can't-pay auto-taps). Ability-level creature `target(...)` supplies the filter
- `ExileSourceFromGraveyardAndTargetCreatureUnlessPaysEffect(String manaCost)` — graveyard-activated punisher: exile the source card from the graveyard and the target creature unless that creature's controller pays `manaCost` (Carrionette, `{2}`). `RemovalEffect` (EXILE). Paying stops both exiles; an illegal target on resolution fizzles and leaves the source in the graveyard
- `TapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.SELF)` — tap self · `.ENCHANTED` — tap aura's enchanted creature
- `TapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.TARGET_PLAYERS_PERMANENTS, filter)` — tap that player's matching permanents
- `TapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.ALL_CREATURES, filter)` — tap all creatures matching filter (`PermanentIsAttackingPredicate` = all attackers)
- `TapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.ALL_PERMANENTS, filter)` — tap every permanent on every battlefield matching filter, any type ("tap all Islands" = `PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(ISLAND)`; Curse of Marit Lage)
- `TapPermanentsForAmountEffect(DynamicAmount amount, PermanentPredicate filter)` — tap as many matching untapped permanents as possible, up to the evaluated amount; when more are available, the affected player must choose exactly the required number. The amount reads the source permanent or its last-known snapshot (Tangle Wire)
- `TapAndLockOtherPermanentsEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — tap every matching permanent except the source and prevent those permanents from untapping while the source remains tapped (Kill Switch)
- `TapPlayersPermanentsAndDamageEqualToCountEffect(filter)` — END_STEP_TRIGGERED: tap the end-step player's untapped permanents matching filter, then deal them damage equal to the number tapped this way (Monsoon)
- `UntapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.TARGET[, PermanentPredicate])` — untap target (predicate restricts targets)
- `UntapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.SELF)` — untap self · `.ALL_TARGETS` — untap all targets
- `UntapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.SOURCE_PERMANENT)` — untap the source permanent read strictly from `entry.getSourcePermanentId()`; use instead of `.SELF` when the SAME entry also has a real target (`.SELF` falls back to the entry's `targetId` and would untap the target again). "You may untap target tapped creature an opponent controls. If you do, untap this creature" = `target(...)` + `MayEffect(SequenceEffect.of(UntapPermanentsEffect(TARGET), UntapPermanentsEffect(SOURCE_PERMANENT)), prompt)` on `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` (Soldevi Golem)
- `UntapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.CONTROLLED, filter[, chosenCount])` — untap all you control matching; a positive `chosenCount` makes it "untap **up to** N permanents you control" and prompts the controller to pick at resolution (declining everything is legal) — Rewind `(CONTROLLED, PermanentIsLandPredicate, 4)`, Unwind `3` · `.OTHER_CONTROLLED_CREATURES` — untap each other creature you control · `.ATTACKED_CREATURES` — untap creatures that attacked this turn · `.ALL_CREATURES[, filter]` — untap every creature on every battlefield matching filter (null = all creatures); Intruder Alarm
- `UntapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.ALL_PERMANENTS, filter)` — untap every permanent on every battlefield matching filter, any type ("untap all Forests" = `PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(FOREST)`; Llanowar Druid)
- `UntapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.ALL_PERMANENTS, filter)` — untap every permanent on every battlefield matching filter, any type ("untap all Forests" = `PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(FOREST)`; Llanowar Druid)
- `UntapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.TARGET_PLAYERS_PERMANENTS, filter)` — untap all of target player's permanents matching filter (Early Harvest: `PermanentAllOf(land, BASIC supertype)` = target player's basic lands)
- `UntapChosenPermanentEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — "untap target [permanent]" as a **resolution-time controller choice** (not a cast-time target) for a triggered ability on a slot with no targeting pipeline, e.g. combat-damage triggers. Pushed non-targeting; at resolution the controller picks one matching permanent across every battlefield to untap via `MultiPermanentChoiceContext.UntapChosenPermanent`. Initiate's Companion (`ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`, `PermanentAnyOfPredicate(creature, land)`)
- `UntapChosenPermanentEffect(PermanentPredicate, boolean targetPlayerChooses)` — with `true` the stack entry's **target player** picks instead of the controller, for `EACH_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` "that player untaps a land they control" (Hokori, Dust Drinker: `PermanentAllOfPredicate(PermanentIsLandPredicate, PermanentControlledByActivePlayerPredicate)`, `true`, paired with STATIC `MatchingPermanentsDoesntUntapEffect(PermanentIsLandPredicate)`). Restrict the candidates to that player's permanents via the predicate
- `TapChosenPermanentEffect(PermanentPredicate, boolean preventUntapWhileSourceTapped)` — tapping counterpart of `UntapChosenPermanentEffect`: "tap target [permanent]" as a **resolution-time controller choice** for a triggered ability on a slot with no targeting pipeline (`ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER` / `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`). Implements `CombatDamageTriggerContextEffect` (`SOURCE_SELF`) so the damage-dealing permanent is bound as the stack entry's source; with `preventUntapWhileSourceTapped=true` the chosen permanent also gets the `WHILE_SOURCE_TAPPED` untap lock (same lock as `DoesntUntapEffect.targetWhileSourceTapped()`). Thalakos Dreamsower (`PermanentIsCreaturePredicate`, `true`)
- `UntapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.ENCHANTED)` — untap the permanent the source Aura/Equipment is attached to (fizzles if unattached). Place on the attachment in a trigger slot (e.g. `ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES`, Thornbite Staff) or wrap it in a `MayPayManaEffect` on `ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` for "that player may pay {X}. If the player does, untap that creature" (Paralyze, Dance of the Dead)
- `PutCounterOnReferencedPermanentEffect(counterType, count[, condition])` — put counters on the source Equipment's attached creature (same `ATTACHED` reference as the Aura form); optional `condition` predicate gates the placement at resolution ("if it's blue"); Ring of Evos Isle. `PermanentReference.SOURCE` is rejected by the constructor — counters on the ability's own permanent belong on `PutCountersOnSourceEffect`
- `SkipNextUntapEffect(TapUntapScope.ENCHANTED)` — locks the permanent the source Aura/Equipment is attached to; put on the attachment's `ON_ATTACK` for "Whenever equipped creature attacks, it doesn't untap during its controller's next untap step" (Stitcher's Graft). Non-targeting; no-op while unattached
- `MatchingPermanentsDoesntUntapEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — global static: every permanent matching the predicate (any controller, incl. the source) doesn't untap during its controller's untap step; Marble Titan (`PermanentPowerAtLeastPredicate(3)`)
- `DoesntUntapWithCounterEffect(CounterType)` — self-scoped static: this permanent doesn't untap during its controller's untap step while it has a counter of that type; Ice Age depletion lands (Land Cap, `CounterType.DEPLETION`)
- `PutCountersOnUntapLockedPermanentsEffect(CounterType, int)` — non-targeting: puts counters on every permanent the source holds a `WHILE_SOURCE_TAPPED` untap lock on; Giant Oyster (`DRAW_TRIGGERED`, `MINUS_ONE_MINUS_ONE`, 1)
- `RemoveCountersWhenUntapLockEndsEffect(CounterType)` — static marker: when the source untaps or leaves the battlefield, release its untap locks and strip all counters of that type from the permanents it locked; Giant Oyster
- `RemoveCountersInsteadOfUntappingEffect(CounterType)` — global STATIC replacement: a permanent with ≥1 counter of that type that would untap during **its controller's** untap step has all such counters removed instead and stays tapped. Doesn't touch "untap target permanent" or Seedborn-Muse untaps during other players' untap steps; Freyalise's Winds (`WIND`)
- `PayManaPerTappedCreatureToUntapEffect(manaPerCreature, PermanentPredicate)` — `EACH_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` (Magnetic Mountain): "that player may choose any number of tapped [matching] creatures they control and pay {manaPerCreature} for each; if they do, untap those." Acting player = stack entry's target (active player); at resolution they get a `MultiPermanentChoice` capped by affordability, then pay `manaPerCreature`Adz?chosen from their pool and untap (`MultiPermanentChoiceContext.PayManaPerCreatureUntap`, handled in `MultiPermanentChoiceHandlerService`). Pair with STATIC `MatchingPermanentsDoesntUntapEffect(sameFilter)`
- `StorageMatrixEffect()` — global static (Storage Matrix): while any permanent carrying it is untapped, each player's untap step pauses (`UntapStepService.storageMatrixRestrictionApplies`) so the active player chooses artifact/creature/land (a `ColorChoice` with `ChoiceContext.StorageMatrixUntapChoice`); only permanents of the chosen type untap that step. No card-side targeting/config — just `addEffect(EffectSlot.STATIC, new StorageMatrixEffect())`.
- `PlayersSkipUntapStepEffect()` — global static (Sands of Time): each player skips their entire untap step while any copy is on the battlefield (works while the source is tapped). `UntapStepService.playersSkipUntapStepApplies` → `untapPermanents(..., skipUntapStep=true)` — no phasing, no Storage Matrix / Static Orb choice; summoning sickness still clears. Distinct from `MatchingPermanentsDoesntUntapEffect` (locks untaps but still runs phasing).
- `PlayersSkipUpkeepStepEffect()` — global static (Eon Hub): each player skips their upkeep step while any copy is on the battlefield. `TurnProgressionService` jumps from untap directly to precombat main, so upkeep triggers and delayed upkeep actions remain pending until a non-skipped upkeep.
- `SimultaneouslyFlipControlledPermanentsTapStatesEffect(filter)` — `EACH_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`: that player (stack `targetId`) simultaneously untaps each tapped matching permanent they control and taps each untapped match, from a pre-resolution snapshot. Sands of Time = `PermanentAnyOfPredicate(artifact, creature, land)`.
- `StaticOrbEffect(maxUntap, filter, requiresUntappedSource)` — global "can't untap more than N [permanents]" lock (Static Orb, Stoic Angel). While an active copy is present (`requiresUntappedSource` → only while its source is untapped), each player's untap step pauses (`UntapStepService.bindingUntapRestriction`, only when more than `maxUntap` of the matching permanents would untap) so the active player picks up to `maxUntap` of the permanents that match `filter` (`null` = all) and would otherwise untap (a `MultiPermanentChoice` with `MultiPermanentChoiceContext.StaticOrbUntap`); only those, plus any permanents the filter excludes, untap that step. Static Orb: `new StaticOrbEffect()` (cap 2, all permanents, while untapped). Stoic Angel and Smoke: `new StaticOrbEffect(1, new PermanentIsCreaturePredicate(), false)` (cap 1, creatures only, always-on). Winter Orb: `new StaticOrbEffect(1, new PermanentIsLandPredicate(), true)` (cap 1, lands only, while untapped).
- `DoesntUntapEffect.self()` — this permanent doesn't untap (static) · `.enchanted()` — attached aura/equipment's host doesn't untap (static) · `.targetWhileSourceOnBattlefield()` — target doesn't untap while source on battlefield (Dungeon Geists / Time of Ice) · `.targetWhileSourceTapped()` — while source stays tapped (Rust Tick); TARGET factories piggyback on a companion `TapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.TARGET)`
- `DiscardRandomCardThenUntapEnchantedCreatureEffect()` — the enchanted permanent's controller (trigger `targetId`) discards a card at random; the Aura's host untaps **only if** a card was actually discarded. Wrap in `MayPayManaEffect(null, …, MayPayPayer.ENCHANTED_CONTROLLER)` on `ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` (Apathy). Not a `DiscardEffect` + `UntapEquippedCreatureEffect` pair, because an empty hand must leave the creature tapped
- `SkipNextUntapEffect(TapUntapScope.TARGET)` — target permanent skips next untap (piggybacks on companion targeting effect) · `.SELF` — source permanent itself skips next untap, non-targeting, for self-referential triggers like `ON_ATTACK` (Lead Golem) · `.CONTROLLED, filter` — the controller's own matching permanents, non-targeting ("Lands you control don't untap during your next untap step" = `PermanentIsLandPredicate`, Oketra's Last Mercy) · `.TARGET_PLAYERS_PERMANENTS, filter` — that player's matching permanents · `.ALL_CREATURES, filter` — all creatures matching filter (`PermanentIsAttackingPredicate` = all attackers)
- `IfWonClashEffect(wrapped)` — clash-only marker on `EffectSlot.ON_CONTROLLER_CLASHES`: the wrapped effect applies only if the controller won the clash ("If you won, ..."). Consumed by `TriggerCollectionService.fireClashTriggers` at trigger time (not a stack effect). See Entangling Trap: tap target opponent creature + `IfWonClashEffect(SkipNextUntapEffect(TARGET))`. Clash is performed via `performClash` (2-player: both reveal top card, strictly-higher mana value wins).
- `IfLostClashEffect(wrapped)` — mirror of `IfWonClashEffect`: applies only when the controller did **not** win. Pair a won- and lost-variant so exactly one branch fires when the base effect happens regardless of outcome but only a detail differs (Rebellion of the Flamekin: two `MayPayManaEffect("{1}", CreateTokenEffect(...))` differing only in granted `HASTE` on the won branch). Non-targeting clash triggers go straight onto the stack.
- `ClashEffect(List<CardEffect> beforeClash, CardEffect onWin, boolean repeatWhileWinning)` — the clash-*source* stack effect; convenience ctor `ClashEffect(onWin)` = `(List.of(), onWin, false)`. Each iteration dispatches `beforeClash` (via each effect's own handler, against the same entry), performs the clash for the controller via `performClash`, dispatches `onWin` on a win, and with `repeatWhileWinning` repeats the whole sequence until a lost clash (deck-out counts as a loss). "Clash with an opponent. If you win, [X]" = `ClashEffect(X)`; `onWin` may be null for a bare "clash with an opponent". Mirrors `FlipCoinWinEffect`. A multi-step win reward is a `SequenceEffect` — expanded **synchronously** (each step via its own handler, against the same entry), so the steps must be synchronous (no async player-input pause between them). E.g. Oaken Brawler = `ClashEffect(new PutCountersOnSourceEffect(1, 1, 1))`. "[body], then clash with an opponent. If you win, repeat this process" = `ClashEffect(body, null, true)`: Hoarder's Greed = `ClashEffect(List.of(new LoseLifeEffect(2), new DrawCardEffect(2)), null, true)`. Wrap in `MayEffect` for "you may clash" (Sentry Oak = `MayEffect(ClashEffect(SequenceEffect.of(new BoostSelfEffect(2, 0), new RemoveKeywordEffect(Keyword.DEFENDER, GrantScope.SELF))), ...)`). Delegates its `targetSpec()` to `onWin`/`beforeClash`, so a **targeted** win reward works on any targeting slot: e.g. Springjack Knight "whenever this attacks, clash; if you win, target creature gains double strike" = `target(...)` + `ClashEffect(new GrantKeywordEffect(Keyword.DOUBLE_STRIKE, GrantScope.TARGET))` on `ON_ATTACK` (target chosen when the trigger goes on the stack; grant only on a win). Do **not** wrap an interactive `MayEffect` as a `ClashEffect` win reward — the may-pause re-runs the `ClashEffect` (re-clash). For an *optional* win reward, use a bare `ClashEffect(null)` (records its result on the entry) followed by `ConditionalEffect(new WonClash(), new MayEffect(reward, prompt))`: Whirlpool Whelm = `ClashEffect(null)` + `ConditionalEffect(new WonClash(), new MayEffect(new PutTargetOnTopOfLibraryEffect(), prompt))` + `ReturnToHandEffect.target()`.

## Control / steal

- `ExchangeControlOfAllMatchingPermanentsWithTargetPlayerEffect(PermanentPredicate[, ControlDuration])` — targeted mass exchange: the controller and target player exchange control of all matching permanents for the requested duration. Collect both sides before moving either side, then apply one per-permanent floating `GainControlOfTargetEffect`; pair with `UntapPermanentsEffect(CONTROLLED, filter)` and `UntapPermanentsEffect(TARGET_PLAYERS_PERMANENTS, filter)` plus matching haste grants for Twist Allegiance

- `ExchangeControlOfAllCreaturesWithTargetPlayerEffect()` — you and target player exchange control of all creatures each controls until end of turn; snapshots both battlefields before applying paired temporary control effects (Reins of Power)
- `GainControlOfTargetEffect(ControlDuration.PERMANENT[, CardSubtype])` — gain control permanently
- `GainControlOfTargetEffect(ControlDuration.END_OF_TURN)` — gain control until EOT
- Threaten bundle gated by one `MayEffect` — no dedicated effect; compose `MayEffect(SequenceEffect.of(GainControlOfTargetEffect(END_OF_TURN), UntapPermanentsEffect(TARGET), GrantKeywordEffect(HASTE, TARGET)), prompt)` (Dominus of Fealty). `SequenceEffect` gates the three primitives as one unit inside the single-effect `MayEffect`
- `GainControlOfTargetEffect(ControlDuration.WHILE_SOURCE_ON_BATTLEFIELD)` — control while source on battlefield AND you still control it (Olivia)
- `GainControlOfTargetEffect(ControlDuration.WHILE_SOURCE_REMAINS)` — control while source remains on battlefield only (Infernal Denizen)
- `GainControlOfTargetEffect(ControlDuration.WHILE_SOURCE_REMAINS_TAPPED)` — control while source remains tapped, regardless of who controls it (Vedalken Shackles)
- `GainControlOfAllPermanentsTargetPlayerControlsEffect(PermanentPredicate filter)` — gain permanent control of every permanent matching `filter` that the target player controls (player-targeting spec, Gilt-Leaf Archdruid = `PermanentIsLandPredicate`). On `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER` the damaged player is baked in (`CombatDamageTriggerContextEffect` → `DAMAGED_PLAYER`), no `target(...)` needed (Hellkite Tyrant = `PermanentIsArtifactPredicate`)
- `GainControlOfAllPermanentsMatchingEffect(PermanentPredicate[, ControlDuration])` — duration defaults to `PERMANENT`; pass `ControlDuration.END_OF_TURN` for Tibalt, the Fiend-Blooded's −6 — non-targeted mass steal: gain permanent control of every permanent (any controller) matching the predicate that you don't already control (Karrthus, Tyrant of Jund's "gain control of all Dragons" = `PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(DRAGON)`). One per-permanent floating `GainControlOfTargetEffect(PERMANENT)`, like `GainControlOfAllPermanentsTargetPlayerControlsEffect` but predicate-selected. Pair a following `UntapPermanentsEffect(ALL_CREATURES, samePredicate)` for the "then untap all …" rider
- `DestroyTargetNonblackCreatureAndGainControlOfAttachedEquipmentEffect()` — targeted removal/control combination for Murderous Spoils; it snapshots all attached Equipment before destroying the creature, then reuses permanent `GainControlOfTargetEffect` control layers for each Equipment.
- `GainControlOfTargetEffect(ControlDuration.WHILE_SOURCE_TAPPED)` — control while source stays on battlefield, its creator still controls it, AND it remains tapped; ends (and does not resume) the moment the source untaps (Seasinger). Untap hook lives in `TapUntapSupport.untapPermanent` / `MayMiscHandlerService.handleMayNotUntapChoice` → `CreatureControlService.onSourceUntapped`
- `GainControlOfAllPermanentsTargetPlayerControlsEffect(PermanentPredicate filter)` — gain permanent control of every permanent matching `filter` that the target player controls (`canTargetPlayer`, Gilt-Leaf Archdruid = lands, Hellkite Tyrant = artifacts on combat damage)
- `GainControlOfPermanentDefendingPlayerControlsAndAssignNoCombatDamageEffect(PermanentPredicate filter, ControlDuration duration, String choiceNoun)` — Orcish Squatters (lands, `WHILE_SOURCE_ON_BATTLEFIELD`), Kukemssa Pirates (artifacts, `PERMANENT`). Wrap in `MayEffect` on `ON_ATTACKS_UNBLOCKED` (`targetId`=defending player, `sourcePermanentId`=attacker). On resolve presents a max-1 choice among the defending player's permanents matching `filter`; taking one gains control of it for `duration` (wraps a plain `GainControlOfTargetEffect`) and — only when one is taken — adds the source to `creaturesPreventedFromDealingCombatDamage` ("assigns no combat damage this turn"). Choice completion lives in `MultiPermanentChoiceHandlerService` (`GainControlOfPermanentAndAssignNoCombatDamage` context)
