- `DestroyPermanentDefendingPlayerControlsAndAssignNoCombatDamageEffect(PermanentPredicate filter, String choiceNoun)` — Goblin Vandal (artifacts). Destroy counterpart of the gain-control effect above; `ON_ATTACKS_UNBLOCKED`, wrapped in `MayPayManaEffect("{R}", …)`. Max-1 choice among the defending player's matching permanents; destroying one also adds the attacker to `creaturesPreventedFromDealingCombatDamage`
- `GainControlOfCombatOpponentAtEndOfCombatEffect(ControlDuration duration)` (no-arg = `WHILE_SOURCE_ON_BATTLEFIELD`) — The Wretched-style "At end of combat, gain control of all creatures blocking this creature for as long as you control this creature". Put on `ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED` with `TriggerMode.PER_BLOCKER` (blockers only). Each trigger queues a `GainControlOfPermanentAtEndOfCombat` delayed action drained in `CombatService.processEndOfCombatControlGains()`; control is applied with `duration` keyed to the source (source-linked durations re-check the source at end of combat). `ControlDuration.PERMANENT` = durationless "gain control of that creature at end of combat" (Tolarian Entrancer), which happens even if the source died in combat
- `PlayerWithMostLifeGainsControlOfSourceCreatureEffect()` — GhazbA?n Ogre: `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`; the player with strictly the most life gains permanent control of this creature. No-op on a tie for most life (intervening-if)
- `PlayerWithMostCreaturesGainsControlOfSourceCreatureEffect()` — Wild Mammoth: `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`; the unique player with strictly the most creatures gains permanent control of this creature. No-op on a tie (intervening-if)
- `GainControlOfEnchantedTargetEffect()` — Control Magic (static)
- `GainControlOfEnchantedPermanentEffect(ControlDuration)` — non-targeting: gain control of the Aura's attached permanent for the duration; reads the Aura's `attachedTo`, so usable on `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD` / `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` slots with no target (Wellspring)
- `ClashForControlOfEnchantedCreatureEffect()` — Captivating Glance: `CONTROLLER_END_STEP_TRIGGERED` Aura effect; clash, then the winner (controller on win, else clash opponent) gains control of the enchanted creature
- `SacrificeEnchantedPermanentAndReattachSourceAuraEffect()` — Nettlevine Blight: `ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER_END_STEP_TRIGGERED` Aura effect; the enchanted permanent's controller sacrifices it and moves this Aura (keeping its controller) onto another creature/land they control
- `AttachSourceAuraToChosenPermanentEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — resolution-time non-targeting choice: the player named by the stack entry's `targetId` chooses a matching permanent from any battlefield, then the source Aura moves onto it if its enchant restriction allows it. The choice pauses and resumes the resolving effect; used by Steam Vines for a land chosen by the tapped land's controller
- `CreateTokenAndAttachTriggeringAuraEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` — Ajani's Chosen: `ON_ALLY_ENCHANTMENT_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD`, no target. Creates the token blueprint, then — if the enchantment that triggered it is an Aura still on the battlefield that could legally enchant the token (CR 701.3a) — offers a yes/no prompt to move it onto the token
- `AttachAurasToSourceEffect()` — Bruna, Light of Alabaster: `ON_ATTACK` + `ON_BLOCK`, no target. One mixed-zone prompt to attach any number of Auras (battlefield permanents + own graveyard/hand Aura cards) to the source; only Auras that could legally enchant it are offered. `oneAuraSearch()` is the Boonweaver Giant variant: choose up to one Aura from your graveyard, hand, or library, with no battlefield candidates.
- `AttachSourceEquipmentToEnteringCreatureEffect(boolean optional)` (no-arg = optional) — enter-trigger marker for attaching the source Equipment to the creature that entered. Optional mode queues `MayEffect(AttachSourceEquipmentToTargetCreatureEffect)`; mandatory mode queues a non-targeting attachment. Used by Cloak and Dagger and Ronin Warclub.
- `AttachSourceAuraToEnteringCreatureEffect()` — Prison Term: `ON_OPPONENT_CREATURE_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD` marker; "you may attach this Aura to that creature" moves the Aura onto the entering opponent creature (enter collector queues `MayEffect(AttachSourceAuraToTargetCreatureEffect)`)
- `ExileUncastEnteringCreaturesEffect(boolean nontokenOnly)` (no-arg = `false`) — Hallowed Moonlight: `SPELL`, no target. Turn-scoped replacement (CR 614.1) — adds the controller to `GameData.playersExilingUncastEnteringCreaturesThisTurn`; any creature that would enter without having been cast (token, reanimation, copied creature spell per CR 707.10) is exiled instead and never enters, so no ETB trigger fires. Tokens are just dropped (CR 111.7). Applied in `BattlefieldEntryService.applyExileUncastEnteringCreature`; `Permanent.isCast()` is set in `StackResolutionService.createEnteringPermanent`. Cleared at turn cleanup. `nontokenOnly=true` (Mistcaller, on a `SacrificeSelfCost` activated ability) uses the parallel `playersExilingUncastEnteringNontokenCreaturesThisTurn` set so entering tokens are unaffected
- `GatherSpecimensEffect()` — Gather Specimens: `SPELL`, no target. Turn-scoped control-changing replacement (CR 614.1) — adds the controller to `GameData.playersGatheringSpecimensThisTurn`; a creature that would enter under any opponent's control instead enters under the gatherer's control. Applied via `BattlefieldEntryService.resolveEnteringController` (universal placement) + `StackResolutionService` creature/artifact spell paths (ETB/log/legend attribution). Cleared at turn cleanup
- `JuxtaposeEffect()` — Juxtapose: `SPELL`, self-targets a single player (player-targeting spec). Controller and target player exchange control of their greatest-mana-value creature, then artifact (creatures first, then artifacts on the updated board; artifact creatures can move twice). Ties prompt the controlling player (`JuxtaposeSupport`, `PermanentChoiceContext.JuxtaposeTieBreak`); missing type on a side skips that exchange. Permanent swap via two `GainControlOfTargetEffect(PERMANENT)` floating effects
- `JuxtaposeEffect()` — Juxtapose: `SPELL`, self-targets a single player (`canTargetPlayer()`). Controller and target player exchange control of their greatest-mana-value creature, then artifact (creatures first, then artifacts on the updated board; artifact creatures can move twice). Ties prompt the controlling player (`JuxtaposeSupport`, `PermanentChoiceContext.JuxtaposeTieBreak`); missing type on a side skips that exchange. Permanent swap via two `GainControlOfTargetEffect(PERMANENT)` floating effects
- `ScrambleverseEffect()` — Scrambleverse: `SPELL`, non-targeting (`ControlStealingEffect`, PERMANENT). For each nonland permanent independently picks a random player, gives them permanent control via a `GainControlOfTargetEffect(PERMANENT)` floating effect, and untaps it (lands untouched; a permanent whose random pick is its current controller stays put but still untaps)
- `ExchangeControlOfSharedTypeTargetsAndDestroyAurasEffect()` — Gauntlets of Chaos: multi-target activated ability (`{5}`, `SacrificeSelfCost`). Reads two `targetIds` — `[0]` an artifact/creature/land you control, `[1]` a permanent an opponent controls sharing one of those types. The "shares one of those types" cross-target restriction is enforced at announcement via `ActivatedAbility.withMultiTargetConstraint(MultiTargetConstraint.SHARE_ARTIFACT_CREATURE_OR_LAND_TYPE)` (per-position filter [1] is just "artifact/creature/land an opponent controls"). At resolution re-checks legality (CR 701.10), swaps controllers permanently (two `GainControlOfTargetEffect(PERMANENT)` floating effects), then destroys every Aura attached to either permanent.
- `SwapExclusiveBlockersBetweenTwoBlockedAttackersEffect()` — General Jarkeld: multi-target `{T}` ability (`ONLY_DURING_DECLARE_BLOCKERS`). Two blocked attackers; if each could be blocked by all creatures blocking the other, exclusive blockers swap. Shared blockers unchanged; does not re-fire block triggers.
- `ExchangeControlOfTargetPermanentsEffect(PermanentPredicate targetPredicate, boolean requireOpponentManaValueNotGreater[, boolean requireFirstTargetControlledByController])` — generic two-target control exchange (with the default `requireFirstTargetControlledByController = true`, `targetIds[0]` is yours and `[1]` an opponent's); the predicate is re-checked on both targets at resolution. Switcheroo (`(PermanentIsCreaturePredicate(), false, false)`): plain "two target creatures", two `target(TargetFilters.creature())` groups, either slot controlled by anyone, exchange only when the two controllers differ (CR 701.12b). Political Trickery: `SPELL` with two `target(...)` groups (land you control / land an opponent controls), `(PermanentIsLandPredicate(), false)`. Puca's Mischief (`(PermanentNotPredicate(PermanentIsLandPredicate()), true)`): `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`, wrap in `MayEffect`. Reads two `targetIds` — `[0]` a nonland permanent you control, `[1]` a nonland permanent an opponent controls with mana value ≤ target [0]. The two interdependent targets are chosen at trigger time via a bespoke two-step permanent choice (`StepTriggerService.processNextPucasMischiefTarget` → `PucasMischiefOwnTarget`/`PucasMischiefOpponentTarget`, mirroring Capricious Efreet); the "you may" resolves like Axis of Mortality. Re-checks legality at resolution and swaps controllers permanently (CR 701.12a)
  - Legerdemain (`(anyOf(artifact, creature), false, false, false, true)`): "exchange control of target artifact or creature and another target permanent that shares one of those types with it" — two same-filter `target(...)` groups plus `setMultiTargetConstraint(MultiTargetConstraint.SHARE_ARTIFACT_OR_CREATURE_TYPE)` at announcement; the fifth flag `requireSharedArtifactOrCreatureType` re-checks the shared type at resolution (`GameQueryService.sharesArtifactOrCreatureType`, land alone does not count).

- `ExchangeControlOfTargetPermanentsEffect.forTriggeringPermanent(PermanentPredicate targetPredicate)` — trigger-time exchange where the entering permanent is the first exchange participant and its controller chooses the other target; use a source-relative predicate such as `PermanentSharesCardTypeWithSourcePermanentPredicate` when the target must share a type with the entering permanent. The entering permanent is not a legal target for its own trigger.

## Mana

- `AddManaWhenLandOfSubtypeTappedForManaEffect(subtype, color, controllerOnly)` — when `controllerOnly` is true, only matching lands tapped by the source's controller add the extra mana (Crypt Ghast); the two-argument form is symmetric (Vernal Bloom)
- `AddManaWhenCreatureTappedForManaEffect(color)` — `ON_CONTROLLER_TAPS_CREATURE_FOR_MANA`; whenever the source's controller taps a creature they control for mana, they add one additional mana of `color` (Leyline of Abundance)

- `AwardManaEffect(ManaColor, DynamicAmount)`, `(ManaColor, int)`, or `(ManaColor)` — add mana; dynamic quantity: `PermanentCount(filter, CONTROLLER)` for "for each X you control", `CountersOnSource(CHARGE)` for "per charge counter", `SourcePower()` for "equal to its power", `LifeLostThisTurn(OPPONENTS)` for "for each 1 life opponents lost this turn" (Neheb, the Eternal on `POSTCOMBAT_MAIN_TRIGGERED`), `FixedIfControlsAllNamed(List<String> names, amount, otherwise)` for the Urza-land ("Tron") boost — `amount` if you control a permanent of every named card, else `otherwise` (e.g. Urza's Mine `AwardManaEffect(COLORLESS, new FixedIfControlsAllNamed(List.of("Urza's Power-Plant", "Urza's Tower"), 2, 1))`)
- `AwardRestrictedManaEffect` also supports `ManaRestriction.SubtypeOrPlaneswalkerSpells(spellSubtype, planeswalkerSubtype)`, which routes mana into a keyed bucket usable only for spells with `spellSubtype` or planeswalker spells with `planeswalkerSubtype` (Chandra's Embercat).
- `AwardAnyColorManaEffect(int)` / `()` / `(DynamicAmount)` / `(int, ManaSpendRestriction)` / `(int, ManaSpendRestriction, CardSubtype)` — add N mana of any color. `ManaSpendRestriction` is the CR 106.6 rider and also fixes how the colour is picked (`FLASHBACK_ONLY` and `SUBTYPE_SPELL_OR_ABILITY` are the "any combination of colors" wordings — one pick per mana; every other constant colours the whole batch with one pick):
  - `NONE` — plain any-color mana (Birds of Paradise, Gilded Lotus `(3)`). `(new XValue())` scales off the ability's xValue (Springjack Pasture, paired with `GainLifeEffect(new XValue())`)
  - `INSTANT_SORCERY_COPY` — plain mana plus a one-shot delayed trigger copying the next instant/sorcery it pays for (Primal Wellspring)
  - `INSTANT_SORCERY_ONLY` — instant/sorcery-only mana (Resonating Lute, granted to lands)
  - `ARTIFACT_SPELLS_OR_ABILITIES` — artifact-spell-or-artifact-ability-only mana (Vedalken Engineer); the chosen color is stored in `ManaPool.artifactOnlyMana`
  - `FLASHBACK_ONLY` — flashback-only mana (Altar of the Lost `(2, FLASHBACK_ONLY)`)
  - `CREATURE_SPELL_ONLY` — any creature spell, no chosen type; cannot pay abilities (Ancient Ziggurat `(1, …)`, Somberwald Sage `(3, …)`)
  - `CHOSEN_SUBTYPE_CREATURE` / `CHOSEN_SUBTYPE_CREATURE_UNCOUNTERABLE` — creature spells of the source's `ChooseSubtypeOnEnterEffect` type, spell-only; the `_UNCOUNTERABLE` form also makes that spell uncounterable (Pillar of Origins / Unclaimed Territory; Cavern of Souls)
  - `CHOSEN_SUBTYPE_SPELL_OR_ABILITY` — spells of the source's chosen `ChooseSubtypeOnEnterEffect` type or activated abilities of permanents of that type (Eclipsed Realms)
  - `CHOSEN_SUBTYPE_CREATURE_SOURCE_SPELL_OR_ABILITY` — creature spells of the source's chosen type or activated abilities of creature sources of that type (Secluded Courtyard)
  - `SUBTYPE_SPELL` — spells of the effect's **printed** `subtype`, spell-only (Sliver Hive `(1, SUBTYPE_SPELL, SLIVER)`). Same bucket as `CHOSEN_SUBTYPE_CREATURE`; the type is on the card rather than chosen as the source enters
  - `SUBTYPE_SPELL_OR_ABILITY` — spells of the effect's `subtype` **or** activated abilities of permanents of it (Smokebraider `(2, SUBTYPE_SPELL_OR_ABILITY, ELEMENTAL)`). These two are the only restrictions that use the `subtype` component
  - `PARTY_SPELL_OR_ABILITY` — Cleric, Rogue, Warrior, or Wizard spells and activated abilities (Base Camp). Uses a shared party-only mana bucket because one mana can pay for any of the four subtypes
- `AwardChosenColorManaEffect()` — mana ability: add one mana of the source permanent's chosen color (`Permanent.getChosenColor()`). Pair with `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD ChooseColorOnEnterEffect`; rewritten into a concrete `AwardManaEffect` at activation. Produces nothing if no color was chosen (Quirion Elves)
- `AwardManaOfChosenColorEqualToDevotionEffect()` — mana ability: choose one of the five colors, then add mana of that color equal to its devotion. Uses a dedicated mana-ability color-choice context so the devotion amount is evaluated after the choice (Nykthos, Shrine to Nyx)
- `AwardManaToActivePlayerEffect(ManaColor, int)` — adds mana to the active player's pool for an `EACH_PRECOMBAT_MAIN_TRIGGERED` trigger ("that player adds {G}{G}"); recipient comes from the stack entry's `targetId`. Not a `ManaProducingEffect` (CR 605.1b). Eladamri's Vineyard
- `AwardManaToCastingPlayerEffect(ManaColor[, int])` — adds mana to the caster's pool for an `ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL` trigger; the generic spell-cast collector carries the caster on `targetId` without making them a chosen target. Tangleroot
- `AwardManaOfColorsEffect(List<ManaColor>)` or `(List<ManaColor>, int/DynamicAmount amount)` — add `amount` mana (default 1), each chosen individually from a **fixed list** (single-color list auto-adds, no prompt). Dual/tri producers like Manaforge Cinder (`List.of(BLACK, RED)` = "Add {B} or {R}"). With `amount > 1` each mana's color is picked separately from the same list, re-prompting per pick — filter lands: Fire-Lit Thicket `(List.of(RED, GREEN), 2)`; Burnt Offering `(List.of(BLACK, RED), new XValue())` after `SacrificeCreatureCost(true)`
- `AwardOneManaOfEachColorAmongControlledEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — "For each color among permanents you control, add one mana of that color." Adds one mana of **every** color found at once (no choice; contrast `AwardManaOfColorsAmongControlledEffect` which picks one). Bloom Tender = `PermanentTruePredicate`
- `AwardManaOfColorsLandsCouldProduceEffect(ManaColorLandScope, PermanentPredicate)` — add one mana of any color a land in scope matching the predicate could produce. `OPPONENTS` + `PermanentIsLandPredicate` = Fellwar Stone ("a land an opponent controls"); `CONTROLLER` + basic-land predicate = Star Compass ("a basic land you control")
- `AwardManaOfTypeUntappedLandCouldProduceEffect()` — add one mana of any type (colorless included) the land untapped for this ability's single-permanent `UntapMultiplePermanentsCost` could produce. Benthic Explorers ("Untap a tapped land an opponent controls: Add one mana of any type that land could produce")
- `AwardManaOfTypeSacrificedLandCouldProduceEffect()` — add one mana of any type (colorless included) the land sacrificed for this ability's `SacrificePermanentCost` could produce. Squandered Resources ("Sacrifice a land: Add one mana of any type the sacrificed land could produce"); source records the sacrificed land's card at payment
- `MayTapLandsYouDontControlForSpellsUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — SPELL slot; until EOT, controller may tap lands they don't control for spell-only mana via `GameService.tapForeignLandForMana(...)` (Piracy)
- `MayPayLifeForColorlessManaUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — SPELL slot; until EOT, controller may pay 1 life to add one unrestricted `{C}` any time they could activate a mana ability, via `GameService.payLifeForColorlessMana(...)` (repeatable, requires life dz?A 1) (Channel)
- `RemoveCountersForManaEffect(ManaColor, CounterType)` — mana ability for the storage-land cycle: "Remove any number of [type] counters from this permanent: Add [color] for each counter removed this way." At resolution it prompts the controller for how many counters (0..the count present) to remove via a `RemoveCountersForManaChoice`, then removes them and adds that much mana (the counter removal is the cost but is modelled together with the mana). Implements `ManaProducingEffect`. Pair with `EntersTappedEffect` + `MayNotUntapDuringUntapStepEffect` + an `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED ConditionalEffect(new SourceIsTapped(), new PutCountersOnSelfEffect(CounterType.STORAGE))`. Bottomless Vault uses `(ManaColor.BLACK, CounterType.STORAGE)`
- `DoubleManaPoolEffect()` — double mana pool
- `ManaReflectionEffect()` — STATIC: tapping a permanent for mana produces twice as much of that mana (Mana Reflection). Applied in the mana-ability resolution via `GameQueryService.manaProductionMultiplier`; multiple stack multiplicatively (2^count)
- `NakedSingularityManaEffect()` — STATIC (`TwistBasicLandManaColorsEffect`): Plains→{R}, Islands→{G}, Swamps→{W}, Mountains→{U}, Forests→{B} when tapped for mana. Global via `GameQueryService.twistedLandManaColors`
- `RealityTwistManaEffect()` — STATIC (`TwistBasicLandManaColorsEffect`): Plains→{R}, Swamps→{G}, Mountains→{W}, Forests→{B} when tapped for mana; Islands unchanged. Global via `GameQueryService.twistedLandManaColors`
- `AllLandsProduceChosenColorUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — triggered: the upkeep player chooses a color, then until end of turn every land tapped for mana produces that color instead of any other (amount unchanged). Hall of Gemstone (`EACH_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`). Turn-scoped via `GameData.allLandsFixedManaColorThisTurn`
- `LandManaProducesOneChosenColorUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — until end of turn, the effect controller's land taps for mana each produce one mana of a color chosen separately for that tap, replacing both the normal type and amount. Used by Harvest Mage; turn-scoped via `GameData.playersWithLandManaChoiceReplacementThisTurn`
- `ReplaceLandManaWithColorEffect(ManaColor)` — STATIC (`LandManaProducesFixedColorEffect`): lands produce that color instead of any other type (amount unchanged). Infernal Darkness (`BLACK`). Global via `GameQueryService.fixedLandManaColor`
- `ReplaceBasicLandManaWithAnyColorEffect()` — STATIC (`BasicLandManaProducesAnyColorEffect`): basic lands controlled by the effect's controller produce one chosen color instead of their normal mana type; amount is unchanged and the choice is made as the mana ability resolves. Pulse of Llanowar
- `LandsOfSubtypeProduceFixedManaColorUntilEndOfTurnEffect(CardSubtype, ManaColor)` — turn-scoped, subtype-scoped version of the above: a land of that subtype tapped for mana produces that color instead of any other type until end of turn (amount unchanged). Chaos Moon's even branch (`MOUNTAIN`, `COLORLESS`). Recorded in `GameData.landSubtypeFixedManaColorThisTurn`, read by `GameQueryService.fixedLandManaColor(gameData, permanent)` where it takes precedence over the static scan; cleared by `TurnCleanupService`
- `NonbasicLandsProduceFixedManaColorUntilEndOfTurnEffect(ManaColor)` — turn-scoped, nonbasic-land-scoped version: a nonbasic land tapped for mana produces that color instead of any other type until end of turn (amount unchanged). Pale Moon (`COLORLESS`). Recorded in `GameData.nonbasicLandsFixedManaColorThisTurn`; cleared by `TurnCleanupService`
- `LandsOfSubtypeAddExtraManaUntilEndOfTurnEffect(CardSubtype, ManaColor)` — turn-scoped, symmetric "whenever a player taps a land of that subtype for mana, that player adds an additional mana of that color". Chaos Moon's odd branch (`MOUNTAIN`, `RED`). Recorded in `GameData.extraManaOnLandSubtypeTapThisTurn`, applied by `TriggerCollectionService.checkLandTapTriggers`; cleared by `TurnCleanupService`. For the permanent static version use `AddManaWhenLandOfSubtypeTappedForManaEffect`
- `AddProducedManaWhenSnowLandTappedEffect()` — symmetric ON_ANY_PLAYER_TAPS_LAND: any snow land tapped for mana pays its controller one extra mana of the produced type. Winter's Night
- `TappedSnowLandDoesntUntapEffect()` — symmetric ON_ANY_PLAYER_TAPS_LAND: any snow land tapped for mana doesn't untap during its controller's next untap step. Winter's Night
- `TargetPlayerLosesAllUnspentManaEffect()` — targeted player empties their mana pool (all buckets incl. persistent); pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter` (Mana Short)
- `DrainTargetPlayersLandManaEffect()` — targeted player taps a mana ability of each untapped land they control (mana into their own pool), then loses all unspent mana and the controller adds an equal amount; pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter` (Drain Power). Fixed single-color lands exact; any-color producers contribute colorless
- `DrainDefendingPlayerLandManaDelayedColorlessEffect()` — defending player (`targetId`) activates a mana ability of each land, loses all unspent mana; controller queues that many `{C}` at next main phase this turn (`AddManaAtNextMainPhase`, mandatory, thisTurnOnly). Non-targeting; wrap in `MayEffect(SequenceEffect.of(..., AssignNoCombatDamageEffect()))` for Pygmy Hippo. Shares land-activation path with Drain Power via `LandManaDrainSupport`
- `AwardHasteGrantingManaEffect(ManaColor, int)` — unrestricted mana carrying the rider "if that mana is spent on a creature spell, it gains haste until end of turn" (Generator Servant `{T}, Sacrifice this creature: Add {C}{C}.`). The mana lands in the regular pool plus a `ManaPool.hasteGrantingMana` tag on that subset; `ManaPool.remove` spends tagged mana first, `SpellCastingService` snapshots the tag around payment and records the card in `GameData.spellsGrantedHasteOnEntry`, and `BattlefieldEntryService` consumes that to give the entering permanent HASTE until end of turn
- `AwardUncounterableGrantingManaEffect(ManaColor, int)` — unrestricted mana carrying the rider "if that mana is spent on an instant or sorcery spell, that spell can't be countered" (Boseiju, Who Shelters All `{T}, Pay 2 life: Add {C}.`). Tagged subset of the regular pool (`ManaPool.uncounterableGrantingMana`), spent first by `ManaPool.remove`; `SpellCastingService` snapshots the tag around payment and adds the instant/sorcery to `GameData.spellsMadeUncounterable`
- `AwardRestrictedManaEffect(ManaColor, int, ManaRestriction)` — restricted mana (`ManaRestriction`: `SpellTypes(Set<CardType>)`, `ArtifactSpells()`, `ArtifactAbilities()`, `SubtypeSpells(CardSubtype)`, `SubtypeCreatureSpells(CardSubtype)`, `KickedCosts()`, `XCosts()`, `CumulativeUpkeepCosts()`, `LegendarySpells()`). `ArtifactSpells()` = cast artifact spells or activate artifact abilities (Grand Architect → `artifactOnlyColorless`). `ArtifactAbilities()` = activate artifact abilities only, not cast artifact spells (Soldevi Machinist → `artifactAbilityOnlyColorless`). `XCosts()` = colorless mana spendable only on spells/abilities whose cost contains {X} (Rosheen Meanderer); routes to the `xCostOnlyColorless` pool bucket, usable for any generic portion of an {X} cost. `SubtypeSpells(CardSubtype)` = Myr-only colorless mana (Myr Reservoir). `SubtypeCreatureSpells(CardSubtype)` = mana spendable only to cast creature spells of that subtype (Gnarlroot Trapper → `subtypeCreatureMana` bucket, the same one Cavern of Souls uses; Giada → ANGEL). `CumulativeUpkeepCosts()` = mana spendable only to pay cumulative upkeep (Adarkar Unicorn); routes to `cumulativeUpkeepOnlyColorless` / `cumulativeUpkeepOnlyColored`. `LegendarySpells()` = colorless mana spendable only to cast spells with the legendary supertype (Untaidake, the Cloud Keeper); routes to `legendarySpellOnlyColorless`, spell-only (can't pay activation costs)
- `NoteManaSpentForActivationEffect()` — notes on the source the type + amount of mana spent to pay this activation cost (Ice Cauldron); stored in `gameData.notedMana`
- `AddNotedManaForLastExiledCardEffect()` — mana ability: adds the source's noted mana, spendable only to cast the card the source last exiled (Ice Cauldron); routes to `ManaPool.exiledCardOnlyMana` keyed by that card's id
- `AddNotedManaEffect()` — mana ability: adds one mana of the source's last noted type (`gameData.notedMana`), unrestricted (Jeweled Amulet)

## Copy / clone

- `MakeTargetCreaturesCopiesOfChosenCreatureUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — choose a creature on the battlefield, then make any number of target creatures controlled by the spell's controller copies of it until end of turn (Polymorphous Rush). Pair with `target(TargetFilters.creatureYouControl(), 0, 99)` and the Strive additional-target cost; the handler pauses for the choice and uses the shared temporary-copy cleanup effect
- `CopyPermanentOnEnterEffect(PermanentPredicate, String)` + overloads — Clone-style. The `(filter, typeLabel, CardColor, CardSubtype, boolean removeManaCost)` overload carries the Vizier-of-Many-Faces embalm exception: an embalm token's final copy is recolored / given the subtype / stripped of mana cost, applied only when the entering permanent is a token. The `(filter, typeLabel, DynamicAmount)` overload adds Altered Ego's "except it enters with X additional +1/+1 counters" (counters only when copying; decline → 0/0 with none). The `(filter, typeLabel, Set<CardSubtype>, Map<EffectSlot, List<CardEffect>>)` overload adds Phantasmal Image's "except it's an Illusion in addition to its other types and it has 'When this creature becomes the target of a spell or ability, sacrifice it'"
- `MakeTargetCopyOfTargetCreatureUntilNextTurnEffect()` — **two targets**: target Shapeshifter (`targetIds[0]`) becomes a copy of target creature (`targetIds[1]`) until the controller's next turn (Shapesharer). Wire via the multi-target `ActivatedAbility` ctor
- `BecomeCopyOfTargetCreatureUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — source permanent becomes a copy of target creature until end of turn (Tilonalli's Skinshifter); `BecomeCopyOfTargetCreatureEffect()` — same, retaining the granting ability (Cryptoplasm). Pass the granting `EffectSlot` when the source trigger is not `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` (Artisan of Forms uses `ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL`)
- `HaveFullTextOfTopCreatureCardInGraveyardEffect()` — while the controller's graveyard top is a creature card, the source uses that card's full text and characteristics while retaining its own discard ability (Volrath's Shapeshifter)
- `ChooseCreatureOnEnterEffect()` + `EnchantedCreatureIsCopyOfChosenCreatureEffect()` — Aura: "As this Aura enters, choose a creature. Enchanted creature is a copy of the chosen creature." (Metamorphic Alteration). ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD + STATIC; the copy is applied and reverted by `AuraCopyService` and lasts only while the Aura stays attached
- `BecomeCopyOfTargetLandEffect()` — source permanent becomes a copy of **target land** permanently, re-granting the activated ability that produced it ("except it has this ability"). Thespian's Stage. Wire as an `ActivatedAbility` with `TargetFilters.land()`
- `BecomeCopyOfDyingCreatureEffect()` — source permanent becomes a copy of a creature that just died (last-known info from the graveyard), retaining its own death-copy trigger ("except it has this ability"). Cemetery Puca. Place in `ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES` wrapped in `MayPayManaEffect` for the "you may pay {1}" gate
- `BecomeCopyOfEnteringCreatureEffect()` — source permanent becomes a copy of another creature as it enters, retaining its own enters-copy trigger ("except it has this ability"). Unstable Shapeshifter. Place in `ON_ANY_OTHER_CREATURE_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD`, mandatory
- `BecomeCopyOfCreatureCardInOpponentGraveyardEffect()` — source permanent becomes a copy of a creature card put into an opponent's graveyard from anywhere, keeping its own name, gaining legendary + hexproof, and retaining its own trigger. Lazav, Dimir Mastermind. Place in `ON_CREATURE_CARD_PUT_INTO_OPPONENT_GRAVEYARD_FROM_ANYWHERE` wrapped in `MayEffect`
- `EachOtherCreatureBecomesCopyOfTargetCreatureUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — every creature on the battlefield **except** the target becomes a copy of the target creature until end of turn (Mirrorweave). Single target; pair with a nonlegendary-creature `PermanentPredicateTargetFilter`. Each copy reverts at cleanup via a per-permanent `BecomeCopyOfTargetCreatureUntilEndOfTurnEffect` floating effect
- `CopySpellEffect()` or `(StackEntryPredicate)` — copy target spell; for "copy twice if cast from a graveyard" add `ConditionalEffect(new CastFromZone(Zone.GRAVEYARD), new CopySpellEffect())` (Increasing Vengeance). Full form `(StackEntryPredicate spellFilter, boolean tokenWithHaste, boolean sacrificeAtEndStep, boolean copyForTargetController)`: for "copy target **creature** spell; the copy gains haste and is sacrificed at the beginning of the end step", use `new CopySpellEffect(null, true, true, false)` — the copy becomes a token, gains `HASTE`, and its permanent is registered in `GameData.delayedActions` (a `DelayedPermanentAction(SACRIFICE_AT_END_STEP)`) (drained by `StepTriggerService.handleEndStepTriggers` via `removePermanentToGraveyard`). `tokenWithHaste` also suppresses the "choose new targets" retarget prompt. Use `CopySpellEffect.forTargetSpellController()` when the target spell's controller controls the copy, as with Meletis Charlatan. Filter which spells are targetable via the mode's `target(...)`/`ChooseOneOption` filter, not `spellFilter`. To make a spell uncopyable, set `card.setCantBeCopied(true)` — honored by every copy handler. See Choreographed Sparks.
- `EachOtherPermanentMatchingPredicateBecomesCopyOfTargetPermanentUntilEndOfTurnEffect(targetPredicate, affectedPredicate)` — every other permanent matching `affectedPredicate` becomes a copy of the target permanent until end of turn. Both predicates are evaluated with the spell controller as source controller; use a controlled-artifact predicate for Masterful Replication. Each copy reverts at cleanup through the shared temporary-copy floating effect
- `CopySpellEffect()` or `(StackEntryPredicate)` — copy target spell; for "copy twice if cast from a graveyard" add `ConditionalEffect(new CastFromZone(Zone.GRAVEYARD), new CopySpellEffect())` (Increasing Vengeance). Full form `(StackEntryPredicate spellFilter, boolean tokenWithHaste, boolean sacrificeAtEndStep)`: for "copy target **creature** spell; the copy gains haste and is sacrificed at the beginning of the end step", use `new CopySpellEffect(null, true, true)` — the copy becomes a token, gains `HASTE`, and its permanent is registered in `GameData.delayedActions` (a `DelayedPermanentAction(SACRIFICE_AT_END_STEP)`) (drained by `StepTriggerService.handleEndStepTriggers` via `removePermanentToGraveyard`). `tokenWithHaste` also suppresses the "choose new targets" retarget prompt. Filter which spells are targetable via the mode's `target(...)`/`ChooseOneOption` filter, not `spellFilter`. To make a spell uncopyable, set `card.setCantBeCopied(true)` — honored by every copy handler. See Choreographed Sparks.
- `CopyThisSpellForTargetControllerEffect()` — copies the resolving spell for the controller of the permanent it targets, with a "choose new targets for the copy" prompt; pair with `MayPayManaEffect(cost, …, MayPayPayer.TARGET_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER)` for "that creature's controller may pay {C}. If the player does, they may copy this spell" (Chain Stasis)
- `CopySpellForEachOtherControlledCreatureEffect()` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL: when an instant/sorcery targets only the source creature, the caster copies it for each other creature they control that the spell could target (Mirrorwing Dragon)
- `CopySpellForEachOtherSubtypePermanentEffect(CardSubtype)` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL: when an instant/sorcery targets only a single permanent of that subtype, copy for each other matching permanent the spell could target (Precursor Golem)
- `CopyThisSpellIfConditionEffect(Condition)` — "When you cast this spell, copy it if <condition>. You may choose new targets for the copy." Place in the `ON_SELF_CAST` slot (the spell's own cast trigger); the copy is created with an optional choose-new-targets prompt only when the condition holds at resolution. Used by the SOS Infusion copy cycle (e.g. Lumaret's Favor with `new GainedLifeThisTurn()`)
- `RegisterNextRedInstantSorceryCopyEffect()` — mana-ability rider: copy the next **red** instant/sorcery cast before mana drains (Pyromancer’s Goggles, paired with `AwardManaEffect(ManaColor.RED)`)
- `CopyNextInstantOrSorceryCastThisTurnEffect()` — one-shot delayed trigger: "when you next cast an instant or sorcery spell this turn, copy that spell" (Chandra, the Firebrand −2). Turn-scoped `GameData.pendingNextInstantSorceryCopyThisTurnCount`, cleared at end of turn
- `DrawOnCreatureSpellCastThisTurnEffect()` - repeating delayed trigger: "whenever you cast a creature spell this turn, draw a card" (Glimpse of Nature). Turn-scoped `GameData.creatureSpellCastDrawsThisTurn`, cleared at end of turn
- `DrawOnCreatureEntersThisTurnEffect()` - repeating optional delayed trigger: "whenever a creature enters this turn, you may draw a card" (Beck). Stores one source card per registration in `GameData.creatureEntersDrawSourcesThisTurn`, fires for every creature including tokens and opposing creatures, and clears at end of turn
- `CopyControllerCastSpellOnSpellCastEffect(CardPredicate, TapMultiplePermanentsCost)` — ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL: copy cast instant/sorcery; optional tap cost wraps `MayPayTapPermanentsEffect` + `CopyControllerCastSpellEffect` (Aziza, Mage Tower Captain). Pass `null` tap cost and wrap in `MayEffect` for a free "you may copy that spell" (Swarm Intelligence)
- `CopyControllerCastSpellOnSpellCastEffect.withIntervening(CardPredicate, Condition)` — free copy trigger with a source-relative condition checked when the spell is cast and again when the copy trigger resolves (Pyromancer Ascension)
- `CopyControllerCastSpellOnSpellCastEffect(CardPredicate, TapMultiplePermanentsCost)` — ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL: copy cast instant/sorcery; optional tap cost wraps `MayPayTapPermanentsEffect` + `CopyControllerCastSpellEffect` (Aziza, Mage Tower Captain). Pass `null` tap cost and wrap in `MayEffect` for a free "you may copy that spell" (Swarm Intelligence). `CopyControllerCastSpellOnSpellCastEffect(CardPredicate, Zone)` = mandatory copy restricted to spells cast from that zone, e.g. `Zone.LIBRARY` for "whenever you cast an instant or sorcery spell from your library, copy it" (Melek, Izzet Paragon)
- `StormEffect()` — Storm keyword. Place in the `ON_SELF_CAST` slot; at cast time queues a `StormCopyEffect` that copies the spell once per spell cast before it this turn (all players). Each copy may choose new targets (Dragonstorm)
- `CascadeEffect()` — Cascade keyword. Place in the `ON_SELF_CAST` slot; queued as a plain triggered ability via the generic self-cast branch, then `CascadeEffectHandler` digs the library until a nonland card with mana value **strictly less** than the cascade spell's (`entry.getCard().getManaValue()`), may cast that one card for free, and puts the rest on the bottom in a random order. Reuses the `CAST_WITHOUT_PAYING` LibrarySearch flow (shared with Sunbird's Invocation), so the free cast + bottoming + AI handling come for free (Ardent Plea). For a permanent that **grants** cascade ("The first spell you cast each turn has cascade", Maelstrom Nexus) put the same `CascadeEffect()` in the `GRANT_CASCADE_TO_FIRST_SPELL` marker slot — `checkSpellCastTriggers` detects it by presence and, on the caster's first spell of the turn, queues it keyed to that spell
- `CopyControllerActivatedAbilityTriggerEffect(String manaCost)` — ON_CONTROLLER_ACTIVATES_NONMANA_ABILITY: "whenever you activate a non-mana ability, you may pay `manaCost` to copy it" — snapshots the ability once it's on the stack, wraps `MayPayManaEffect` + `CopyControllerActivatedAbilityEffect`; single-target copies are retargetable (Rings of Brighthearth)
- `CopyControllerActivatedAbilityTriggerEffect(null, null, true)` — same slot, equipped-creature variant: "whenever an ability of equipped creature is activated, if it isn't a mana ability, copy that ability" — `manaCost == null` = free and mandatory (no `MayPayManaEffect`) (Illusionist's Bracers)
- `CopyTargetTriggeredAbilityEffect()` — activated ability: copy target triggered ability on the stack (may choose new targets). Pair with `StackEntryPredicateTargetFilter(TRIGGERED_ABILITY + ControlledBy)`. Spec `SPELL_ON_STACK`. Strionic Resonator
- `ChangeTargetOfTargetSpellWithSingleTargetEffect(boolean creatureTargetsOnly)` — redirect spell (no-arg = any single target; `true` = creature-to-creature only, Meddle)
- `ChangeTargetOfTargetSpellUnlessControllerPaysEffect(int payAmount)` — Divert-style redirect; the targeted spell's controller is prompted to pay the fixed generic amount, otherwise the existing single-target retargeting flow is used
- `ChooseNewTargetsForTargetSpellEffect()` — choose new targets
- `PsychicBattleEffect()` — global target-choice trigger: reveal each library's top card and, on a unique highest mana value, let that player change any legal target(s); ties do nothing
- `PsychicBattleRetargetEffect(UUID spellCardId, int targetIndex)` — internal may-choice payload used by `PsychicBattleEffect`

## Turn / phase

- `ControllerExtraTurnEffect(DynamicAmount)` evaluates the number of granted turns from the stack entry amount context, allowing activation-time X values such as Sage of Hours.

- `TimesifterEffect()` — each upkeep, every player with a library card exiles their top card; the greatest mana value wins an extra turn, and tied winners repeat until one remains
- `SkipDrawStepThisTurnEffect()` — skips the controller's draw step in the current turn; repeated applications in that turn collapse to one skip. Used by Elfhame Sanctuary after its optional basic-land search

- `ControllerExtraTurnEffect(int)` / `ControllerExtraTurnEffect(int, boolean skipUntapStep)` — extra turns (non-targeting); `skipUntapStep=true` makes each granted turn skip its untap step (Savor the Moment)
- `RegisterLoseGameAtEndStepEffect()` — schedules "at the beginning of your next end step, you lose the game" (Last Chance, Glorious End); fires at the controller's own next end step, skipping the scheduling turn's end step and every opponent's end step
- `ExactLifeLoseGameThenAdjustLifeEffect(exactLife, lifeDelta)` — each player at exactly `exactLife` loses simultaneously, then (if game continues) each gains (`lifeDelta`&gt;0) or loses (`lifeDelta`&lt;0) that much life. All lose ⇒ draw. Triskaidekaphobia `(13, 1)` / `(13, -1)` modes inside `ChooseOneEffect` on `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`
- `ExtraTurnEffect(int)` — target extra turns
- `ControlTargetPlayerNextTurnEffect()` / `(true)` — control target player during their next turn (Mindslaver); `true` = also grant that player an extra turn after the controlled turn (Emrakul, the Promised End; scheduled when control activates)
- `AdditionalCombatMainPhaseEffect(int)` — additional combat + main phase pairs (Relentless Assault)
- `AdditionalCombatPhaseEffect(int)` — additional combat phase(s) with NO additional main phase (Finest Hour: "After this phase, there is an additional combat phase")
- `SkipNextEffect(SkipKind, SkipRecipient)` / `(SkipKind)` (= `CONTROLLER`) — the one-shot "skips their next …" family. `SkipKind` = `TURN` (Chronatog, Meditate) / `UNTAP_STEP` (Yosei, the Morning Star) / `DRAW_STEP` (Ivory Gargoyle) / `COMBAT_PHASE` (Blinding Angel, False Peace, Empty City Ruse, Stonehorn Dignitary), each queued on its own per-player `GameData.skipNext*Count` and drained by the turn engine. `SkipRecipient` = `CONTROLLER` (no targeting) / `DAMAGED_PLAYER` (ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER bakes the damaged player in as `targetId`; no targeting) / `TARGET_PLAYER` (the only value with a benign `PLAYER` spec). Skipping proceeds past the turn/step/phase as though it didn't exist, so a skipped untap step means no phasing and no untap-restriction choice (Storage Matrix, Static Orb), and a skipped draw step means no turn-based draw and no draw-step triggers. Not the static markers `SkipDrawStepEffect` / `PlayersSkipUntapStepEffect`, nor `SkipNextUntapEffect` (marks individual permanents; the step still happens)
- `TargetPlayerChoosesOneEffect(List<ChooseOneOption>)` — resolution-time modal choice made by the stack entry's `targetId`, rather than its controller; reuse `ChooseOneEffect` options and the shared mode-choice interaction. Fatespinner
- `SkipChosenStepOrPhaseEffect(SkipStepOrPhaseKind)` — records that the affected player skips every remaining draw step, main phase, or combat phase instance this turn. The turn engine consumes the turn-scoped selection and clears it at cleanup. Fatespinner
- `EndTurnEffect()` — end the turn

## Animate / transform

- `AnimatePermanentsEffect(power, toughness, subtypes, keywords, color, cardTypes, GrantScope, EffectDuration, filter)` (+ int-P/T sugar ctors) — one/many permanents become creatures. Scope SELF (manland/self, UEOT), TARGET (single, UEOT via Elvish Branchbender / UNTIL_YOUR_NEXT_TURN via Xenic Poltergeist / PERMANENT via Tezzeret / WHILE_SOURCE_ON_BATTLEFIELD via Awakener Druid; **multi-target reads `targetIds` for "up to N target" abilities, both UEOT — Fendeep Summoner — and PERMANENT — Nissa, Sage Animist's −7**), OWN_LANDS (Sylvan Awakening), ALL_LANDS (every land on the battlefield, both players — Natural Affinity), OWN_PERMANENTS+filter (The Antiquities War). Wire multi-target via the multi-target `ActivatedAbility` ctor (per-position filters, minTargets/maxTargets). P/T `DynamicAmount` (`XValue`/`CountersOnSource`/`TargetManaValue` — "P/T each equal to its mana value", Xenic Poltergeist); null P/T = printed. The animation flag alone grants creature-ness (no CREATURE cardType needed); for a noncreature artifact it becomes an artifact creature
- `SetCardTypesUntilEndOfTurnEffect(Set<CardType>, GrantScope)` — one-shot layer-4 replacement that makes the selected permanent have exactly the listed card types until cleanup. Use with `AnimatePermanentsEffect` when the oracle says the object becomes a creature and loses another card type, as with Sarkhan, the Dragonspeaker.
- `GrantSupertypeUntilEndOfTurnEffect(CardSupertype, GrantScope)` — one-shot layer-4 supertype grant stored as a floating effect until end of turn. Use `GrantScope.SELF` for an untargeted activated ability's source or `TARGET` for an explicit permanent target; `GameQueryService.hasEffectiveSupertype` and the legend rule include the grant. Genju of the Realm combines it with `AnimatePermanentsEffect(8, 12, [SPIRIT], {TRAMPLE})`
- `AnimatePermanentsEffect.crew()` — vehicle crew (printed P/T, +CREATURE)
- `AllLandsAreCreaturesEffect(power, toughness[, CardSubtype requiredSubtype[, CardColor color]])` — STATIC global: lands (both players') are fixed-P/T creatures that are still lands while the source is out. No subtype = every land (Nature's Revolt = 2/2); with a land subtype = only lands carrying it (Living Lands = all Forests become 1/1). Lands are colorless (CR 202.2), so pass `color` only when the oracle text names one (Kormus Bell = all Swamps become 1/1 **black**). Reverts when it leaves; anthems/lords see the animated lands
- `AnimateNoncreatureArtifactsEffect([boolean losesAllAbilities])` — STATIC global: every noncreature artifact becomes a creature with P/T = its mana value (March of the Machines). Pass `true` to also strip the artifacts' abilities in layer 6 (Titania's Song); no-arg / `false` = animate only
- `AnimateControlledEnchantmentsEffect(int minEnchantments)` — STATIC: while the controller controls at least `minEnchantments` enchantments, each OTHER non-Aura enchantment THEY control is a creature with base P/T = its mana value (Starfield of Nyx, `5`). Enchantment sibling of `AnimateNoncreatureArtifactsEffect`, same L4 type change + L7B base P/T under one timestamp; unlike it, permanents that are already creatures are not skipped. The threshold is a field, not a `ConditionalEffect` wrapper — the layered pass only admits conditional wrappers in layer 4, so wrapping would drop the 7b base P/T
- `AnimateNonAuraEnchantmentsEffect()` — STATIC: each OTHER non-Aura enchantment on the battlefield is a creature in addition to its other types and has base P/T equal to its mana value (Opalescence). Applies across both battlefields, preserves abilities, and uses the same L4 + L7B layered animation as Starfield of Nyx
- `AllPermanentsUpkeepSacrificeUnlessPayEffect(PermanentPredicate filter, String manaCost[, boolean excludeSource])` — STATIC global marker: grants every matching permanent "At the beginning of your upkeep, sacrifice this permanent unless you pay {N}." Read by `StepTriggerService` (not the layer system) — one `ForcedCostOrElseEffect` pay-or-sacrifice trigger per matching permanent the active player controls, sourced at that permanent. Grant is global regardless of who controls the enchantment (Energy Flux artifacts `{2}`, Pendrell Mists creatures `{1}`, Aura Flux other enchantments `{2}`); `excludeSource` excludes the permanent carrying the grant
- `AllPermanentsUpkeepSacrificeUnlessPayEffect(PermanentPredicate filter, String manaCost, int lifeAmount)` — STATIC global marker: grants every matching permanent "At the beginning of your upkeep, sacrifice this permanent unless you pay [cost]." Read by `StepTriggerService` (not the layer system) — one `ForcedCostOrElseEffect` pay-or-sacrifice trigger per matching permanent the active player controls, sourced at that permanent. Grant is global regardless of who controls the enchantment (Energy Flux artifacts `{2}`, Pendrell Mists creatures `{1}`, Vile Consumption creatures 1 life)
- `TransformSelfEffect()` — transform DFC
- `ExileSelfAndReturnTransformedEffect(thenEffect?)` — "exile this permanent, then return it transformed under its owner's control", right now (Jace, Vryn's Prodigy). The optional `thenEffect` is an "If you do, …" rider resolved only when the transform happened (Liliana, Heretical Healer). The permanent comes back as a NEW object; a planeswalker back face enters with its starting loyalty. `ExileSelfAtEndOfCombatAndReturnTransformedEffect()` is the end-of-combat sibling (Kytheon, Hero of Akros)
- `TransformSelfAndAttachToCreatureDamagedPlayerControlsEffect()` — combat-damage "you may transform; if you do, attach to target creature that player controls" (wrap in `MayEffect`)
- `TransformEnteringCreatureEffect([boolean optional])` / `TransformTargetPermanentEffect()` — may/must transform the entering creature (Vildin-Pack Alpha); see counters section above
- `TransformAllEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — transform all matching
- `PreventTransformEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — STATIC: permanents you control matching the predicate can't transform (e.g. Immerwolf)
- `EnchantedCreatureCantTransformEffect()` — STATIC Aura: enchanted creature can't transform (Bound by Moonsilver); sibling of Pacifism-style markers, checked via `hasAuraWithEffect` in `isTransformPrevented`

## Static restrictions / taxes
- `ReduceActivationCostEffect(DynamicAmount amount)` — CostEffect placed in an ActivatedAbility's effect list; reduces the generic mana of the activation cost by the evaluated amount at activation time. Use a counting amount for "This ability costs {1} less to activate for each …" (Nemesis of Mortals)

- `GrantNoMaximumHandSizeEffect(NoMaximumHandSizeDuration)` — controller has no maximum hand size. `UNTIL_NEXT_TURN` is cleared when that player's turn begins (Enter the Infinite); `REST_OF_GAME` never expires (Praetor's Counsel, Tamiyo the Moon Sage, Wrenn and Seven)

- `EntersTappedEffect()` — enters tapped
- `ReturnSourceToHandAtNextCleanupEffect()` — schedules "return this permanent to its owner's hand at the beginning of the next cleanup step" (Thawing Glaciers); swept by `TurnCleanupService`
- `ReturnSourceToHandAtNextUntapEffect()` — schedules "return this permanent to its owner's hand during its controller's next untap step" (Undiscovered Paradise); swept by `UntapStepService`; on mana abilities applied inline in `doResolveManaAbility`
- Conditional enters-tapped (check/fast/slow lands): `ConditionalReplacementEffect(condition, new EntersTappedEffect())` where the condition is the **negated** unless-clause (true ⇒ enters tapped), evaluated at entry against the entering permanent's controller (the permanent isn't on the battlefield yet, so counts exclude it). Check land = `ControlsPermanentCountAtMost(0, PermanentHasAnySubtypePredicate)` (tapped unless you control a matching permanent); fast land "unless N-or-fewer other lands" = `ControlsPermanentCount(N+1, new PermanentIsLandPredicate())`; slow land "unless N-or-more other lands" = `ControlsPermanentCountAtMost(N-1, new PermanentIsLandPredicate())`. **Never add a per-cycle enters-tapped record.**
- `RevealSubtypeOrEntersTappedEffect(subtype)` — STATIC: "you may reveal a [subtype] card from hand as it enters; if you don't (or can't), it enters tapped" (Lorwyn dual lands, e.g. Ancient Amphitheater = GIANT)
- `MayPayLifeOrEntersTappedEffect(lifeCost)` — STATIC: "you may pay N life as this enters; if you don't (or can't), it enters tapped" (shock lands, e.g. Blood Crypt = 2 life)
- `IgnoreLegendRuleWhenExactlyTwoSameNameEffect()` — STATIC: "if there are exactly two permanents named ~ on the battlefield, the legend rule doesn't apply to them" (Brothers Yamazaki). Implements the `LegendRuleExemptionEffect` capability interface, which `LegendRuleService` reads; the count spans every player's battlefield, and every same-named permanent in the violating group must carry an active exemption for the rule to be skipped
- `IgnoreLegendRuleEffect()` — STATIC: "the legend rule doesn't apply" globally while its source is on the battlefield. Implements `GlobalLegendRuleExemptionEffect`, which `LegendRuleService` reads. Used by Mirror Gallery
- `SacrificeOtherPermanentsWithSameNameOnEnterEffect()` — STATIC: "if this would enter, instead sacrifice each other permanent with the same name you control, then it enters" (Sheltered Valley self legend rule; only your own copies, and it always enters)
- `SacrificePermanentAsEntersOrGraveyardEffect(filter, description)` or `(filter, count, description)` — STATIC: "if this would enter, sacrifice one or exactly `count` [filter] permanents instead; if you do it enters, if you don't it goes to its owner's graveyard". Count greater than one uses a multi-permanent choice where none declines and any nonempty selection must contain exactly `count` permanents (Balduvian Trading Post = one untapped Mountain; Lotus Vale = two untapped lands)
- `DiscardCardAsEntersOrGraveyardEffect(filter, description)` — STATIC: "if this would enter, you may discard a matching card instead; if you do it enters, if you don't it goes to its owner's graveyard". Uses the normal filtered discard prompt with a decline option (Mox Diamond = a land card)
- `SacrificePermanentsToUntapSelfEffect(count, PermanentPredicate, description)` — resolution: all-or-nothing "sacrifice `count` [matching]. If you do, untap this creature." Untaps only when the controller controls dz?A`count` matching permanents (auto-selects which to sacrifice). Wrap in `MayEffect` for the "you may" variant (Leviathan — UPKEEP_TRIGGERED, `count=2`, Island filter)
- `NoMaximumHandSizeEffect()` — no max hand size (static; controller only)
- `ReplaceManaDrainWithColorlessEffect()` — the controller's unspent mana becomes colorless instead of draining at step/phase boundaries; persistent mana remains in its current color until it would drain (Kruphix, God of Horizons)
- `PlayersHaveNoMaximumHandSizeEffect()` — players have no maximum hand size (static; all players while source is on the battlefield; Anvil of Bogardan)
- `ReduceControllerMaxHandSizeEffect(int reduction)` — controller's maximum hand size reduced by N (static; Thought Devourer). Implements `ControllerMaxHandSizeEffect`
- `ReduceOpponentMaxHandSizeEffect(int reduction)` — each opponent's max hand size reduced by N (static; Jin-Gitaxias). Implements `OpponentMaxHandSizeEffect`
- `SetOpponentMaximumHandSizeEffect(int maximumHandSize)` — the chosen opponent's max hand size is set to a specific value (Cursed Rack — 4; modeled as the controller's opponent(s)). Static; folded in timestamp order with reductions by `TurnCleanupService.getMaxHandSize`. Implements `OpponentMaxHandSizeEffect`
- `SetControllerMaximumHandSizeEffect(int maximumHandSize)` — your max hand size is set to a specific value (Recycle — 2). Static; folded in timestamp order by `TurnCleanupService.getMaxHandSize`. Implements `ControllerMaxHandSizeEffect`
- `DiscardToTopOfLibraryInsteadEffect()` — static replacement (Library of Leng): the controller's effect-caused discards go on top of their library instead of the graveyard. Checked in `GraveyardService.discardCard()`; modeled as always keeping the card. Pair with `NoMaximumHandSizeEffect()`
- `IncreaseSpellCostEffect(CardPredicate, DynamicAmount, CostModificationScope)` / flat-int sugar — matching spells cost the evaluated amount more (ALL = everyone — Thalia, Chill, Gloom, Feroz's Ban, Thorn of Amethyst, Irini Sengir; SELF = yours only — Derelor `CardColorPredicate(BLACK)`, 1, "{B} more" modeled as +1 generic; OPPONENT = opponents' only — Aura of Silence, artifact-or-enchantment, 2). The amount is evaluated against the source permanent, enabling Hum of the Radix's `PermanentCount(PermanentIsArtifactPredicate(), CONTROLLER)` tax. Tax-side mirror of `ReduceCastCostForMatchingSpellsEffect`, same scope enum
- `MinimumSpellCostEffect(int)` — static battlefield floor on a spell's mana component, evaluated after other cast-cost increases and reductions and active only while the source permanent is untapped (Trinisphere, 3)
- `IncreaseCostOfSpellsTargetingThisSpellEffect(int)` — spell-self (STATIC): while this spell is on the stack, spells that target it cost N more (symmetric; spells only, not activated abilities). Kaervek's Torch, 2
- `IncreaseSpellCostExceptOnControllersTurnEffect(int)` — every spell costs N more (symmetric), except during the spell's controller's own turn (Defense Grid, {3})
- `IncreaseOpponentCostForTargetingControlledPermanentEffect(PermanentPredicate, int[, boolean taxesActivatedAbilities])` — opponent spells/abilities targeting your matching permanent cost more; the two-argument form taxes both, while `false` taxes spells only and source-relative predicates use the taxing permanent as source
- `IncreaseActivatedAbilityCostEffect(PermanentPredicate, int)` — activated abilities of permanents matching the predicate cost N more generic mana to activate (STATIC, symmetric; e.g. Gloom with white-enchantment predicate, 3). Taxes ability activation, not spells; folded in by `AbilityActivationService` via `CastingCostService.getActivatedAbilityActivationTax`
- `ReduceEquipCostEffect(int)` — equip abilities of Equipment permanents controlled by the source's controller cost N less generic mana to activate (STATIC, controller-scoped; Auriok Steelshaper). Applies only to equip abilities and is floored to the ability's generic mana portion
- `AdditionalSacrificePerManaSymbolEffect(ManaColor, PermanentPredicate, String, boolean taxesSpells, boolean taxesAbilities)` — STATIC symmetric marker (Drought): spells and/or activated abilities cost an additional "Sacrifice a [matching permanent]" for each mana symbol of that color in their mana/activation cost (hybrid/Phyrexian of that color count once). Queried by `CastingCostService.getImposedSacrificeRequirement*`; spell payment via `PlayCardRequest.imposedSacrificePermanentIds`; ability payment synthesizes `SacrificeMultiplePermanentsCost` into the interactive permanent-choice path
- `ReduceGraveyardCardActivatedAbilityCostEffect(CardPredicate, int)` — activated abilities of *graveyard cards* matching the predicate cost N less generic mana to activate, controller-scoped ("in **your** graveyard"; STATIC). E.g. Embalmer's Tools with `CardTypePredicate(CREATURE)`, 1 — also cheapens Embalm/Eternalize. Read from the activating player's battlefield by `CastingCostService.getGraveyardActivatedAbilityCostReduction` and threaded into `AbilityActivationService`'s graveyard-ability payment as a negative additional cost (floored to the generic portion). Distinct from `IncreaseActivatedAbilityCostEffect`, which taxes *permanent* abilities
- `ReduceActivatedAbilityCostEffect(PermanentPredicate, int)` — activated abilities of matching permanents cost N less generic mana to activate, symmetrically for all players (STATIC). E.g. Heartstone with `PermanentIsCreaturePredicate()`, 1. Read from every battlefield by `CastingCostService.getActivatedAbilityActivationCostReduction`; colored mana requirements are untouched and the total cost cannot be reduced below one mana
- `IncreaseOwnCastCostUnlessRevealSubtypeEffect(int amount, CardSubtype)` — spell-self (STATIC): costs `amount` more unless you can reveal a matching-subtype card from hand (other than the spell). Lorwyn "reveal a creature-type card or pay {N}" cycle (Goldmeadow Stalwart: Kithkin/{3})
- `ReduceOwnCastCostEffect(DynamicAmount)` — **THE spell-self cost reduction.** `Fixed(N)` for a flat amount; a counting amount for "for each …" (Ghoultree `CardsInGraveyard(CardTypePredicate(CREATURE), CONTROLLER)`, Emrakul `CardTypesAmongCardsInGraveyard()`, Blasphemous Act `PermanentCount(PermanentIsCreaturePredicate, ANY_PLAYER)`, Draco `Scaled(BasicLandTypesAmongControlledLands(), 2)` = Domain, {2} per basic land type, Hollow One `Scaled(CardsDiscardedOrCycledThisTurn(), 2)` = {2} per card cycled/discarded this turn). Conditional reductions wrap it: `ConditionalEffect(condition, ReduceOwnCastCostEffect(Fixed(N)))` — Metalcraft (Stoic Rebuttal), ControlsPermanent (Academy Journeymage / Wizard's Retort / Wizard's Lightning / Lookout's Dispersal), OpponentControlsMoreCreatures (Avatar of Might), CardsLeftGraveyardThisTurn (Wilt in the Heat). **Never add a per-variant record for this.** Exception: when the gating condition would read effective P/T, do **not** use `ConditionalEffect` on `STATIC` — the static-bonus path evaluates the condition and computing effective toughness recurses. Instead fold the gate into a threshold amount so it's only evaluated by the cost handler at cast time: `ReduceOwnCastCostEffect(new FixedIfControlledCreaturesTotalToughnessAtLeast(M, N))` — "costs {N} less if creatures you control have total toughness M+" (Orysa, Tide Choreographer).
- `ReduceOwnCastCostForSharedCardTypeWithImprintEffect(DynamicAmount)` — controller's spells sharing a card type with the imprinted card cost less (Semblance Anvil)
- `ReduceCastCostForMatchingSpellsEffect(CardPredicate, DynamicAmount|int, CostModificationScope)` — matching spells cost less (SELF = yours, OPPONENT = opponents', ALL = everyone's — Arcane Melee; e.g. CardSubtypePredicate, CardIsHistoricPredicate, CardAnyOfPredicate). The amount is evaluated against the source permanent — `CountersOnSource(PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE)` gives "for each +1/+1 counter on this creature" (Herald of War)
- `ReduceCastCostForChosenSubtypeSpellsEffect(int amount)` — source controller's creature spells of the source permanent's chosen creature subtype cost `amount` less; pair with `ChooseSubtypeOnEnterEffect` (Urza's Incubator); changeling creature cards qualify
- `ModifyFlashbackCostEffect(int amount, CostModificationScope)` — modifies only flashback costs, with positive amounts increasing and negative amounts reducing the generic portion; SELF/OPPONENT/ALL scope follows the source permanent's controller (Catalyst Stone, -2 for you and +2 for opponents)
- `ReduceOwnCastCostIfTargetingStackEntryEffect(StackEntryPredicate, int)` — this spell costs less if first target is a spell on the stack matching the predicate (kept — target-gated)
- `ReduceOwnCastCostIfTargetingPermanentEffect(PermanentPredicate, int, boolean controlledByCaster)` / sugar `(predicate, amount)` = false — this spell costs less if its first target matches the predicate; `false` = any controller (Ajani's Response), `true` = a permanent **you control** (Savage Stomp, "a Dinosaur you control"). Kept — target-gated
- `ReduceOwnCastCostPerTargetEffect(PermanentPredicate, int)` — this spell costs N less for every chosen target permanent matching the predicate; repeated target occurrences count separately. Kept as a target-gated exception and resolved by `CastingCostService.computeTargetBasedCostReduction` (Battlefield Thaumaturge)
- `ReduceActivationCostPerCounterEffect(CounterType, int reductionPerCounter)` — CostEffect placed in an ActivatedAbility's effect list; reduces the generic mana of the activation cost by N per counter of the given type on the source (floored at 0). Applied in `AbilityActivationService`. Used by Diary of Dreams (page counters)
- `IncreaseActivationCostPerCounterEffect(CounterType, int increasePerCounter)` — CostEffect mirror of the above; **raises** the generic activation cost by N per counter of that type on the source, counted at activation time. Use with a printed `{0}` cost for "{X}: … X is the number of [type] counters on this permanent" (Chromatic Armor, sleight counters)
- `LimitSpellsPerTurnEffect(int maxSpells, SpellLimitScope)` — max spells per turn; EACH_PLAYER = every player (Rule of Law, Arcane Laboratory), CONTROLLER = the source's controller only (Colfenor's Plans), ENCHANTED_PLAYER = the enchanted player only (Curse of Exhaustion, a Curse Aura). Lowest applicable limit wins
- `EnchantedPlayerCantActivateNonManaNonLoyaltyAbilitiesEffect()` — enchanted player can activate only mana/loyalty abilities (Curse Aura, Overwhelming Splendor)
- `IgnoreSourceAuraEffectsUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — NORMAL: the source Aura's static effects are ignored until end of turn (Volrath's Curse); flags `auraEffectsIgnoredThisTurn`, read by `GameQueryService.hasAuraWithEffect`, cleared by `resetModifiers()`
- `IgnoreSourceDampingEngineEffectUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — NORMAL: the source Damping Engine restriction is ignored until end of turn; flags the source permanent and is cleared by `resetModifiers()`
- `GrantExtraLoyaltyActivationToPlaneswalkersEffect()` — one-shot: each planeswalker you control gets one extra loyalty activation this turn (The Chain Veil). Stacks with itself and with the static `AllowExtraLoyaltyActivationEffect()` (Oath of Teferi)
- `CantSearchLibrariesEffect(boolean payableToIgnore)` — can't search (static); `true`=pay {2} to ignore (Leonin Arbiter, no-arg default), `false`=absolute (Mindlock Orb)
- `CantSearchLibrariesEffect()` — can't search (static)
- `OpponentSearchesTopCardsInsteadEffect(int count)` — static: if an opponent of this permanent's controller searches ANY library, they search only the top `count` cards of that library instead (Aven Mindcensor, `count=4`). Passive rules-modifier read at search time by `LibrarySearchSupport.sendLibrarySearchToPlayer`; if none of the top N match, the search finds nothing
- `CantCastSpellTypeEffect(Set<CardType>)` / `(Set<CardType>, boolean appliesToAllPlayers)` — can't cast spells of these types (static). Default controller-only (Steel Golem); pass `true` for symmetric "these spells can't be cast" (Aether Storm creature clause)
- `CantCastSpellsSharingColorWithMostRecentSpellEffect()` — symmetric static restriction: players can't cast a spell sharing a color with the most recently cast spell this turn; colorless spells share no colors (Mana Maze). Enforced through `CastingPermissionService`
- `EnchantedPermanentControllerCantCastSpellTypeEffect(Set<CardType>)` — Aura static; the **enchanted permanent's controller** can't cast spells of these types (Brand of Ill Omen: "Enchanted creature's controller can't cast creature spells")
- `NoncreatureSpellsCantBeCastEffect(int minManaValue, boolean restrictXSpells)` — global/symmetric: no player can cast a noncreature spell with mana value >= `minManaValue`, or (if `restrictXSpells`) with `{X}` in its cost (static, Gaddock Teeg `(4, true)`)
- `OpponentsCantCastSpellsWithManaValueAtMostEffect(int maxManaValue)` — opponents of the source's controller can't cast spells with mana value ≤ `maxManaValue` (static, Brisela `(3)`; chosen X counted at cast time)
- `OpponentsCantCastSpellsOfChosenColorEffect()` — opponents of the source permanent's controller can't cast spells of its chosen color (static, Iona, Shield of Emeria). Pair with `ChooseColorOnEnterEffect()`; colorless spells and the controller are unaffected
- `OpponentsCantCastOrActivateDuringYourTurnEffect()` — during the controller's turn, opponents can't cast spells or activate abilities of artifacts/creatures/enchantments (static, Grand Abolisher; mana abilities included, lands unaffected)
- `PlayersCanCastSpellsOnlyDuringOwnTurnEffect()` — players can cast spells only on their own turns (static, Dosan the Falling Leaf; abilities unaffected; symmetric)
- `PlayersCanCastAndActivateOnlyDuringOwnTurnEffect()` — players can cast/activate only on their own turns (static, City of Solitude; mana abilities included, all zones; symmetric)
- `PlayersCantCastInstantsOrActivateNonManaAbilitiesDuringCombatEffect()` — during combat, no player can cast instant spells or activate non-mana abilities (static, Hand to Hand; symmetric; `GameQueryService.isCombatActionLockActive`)
- `CantCastAdditionalNonartifactSpellsEffect()` — global/symmetric: each player who has already cast a nonartifact spell this turn can't cast additional nonartifact spells; artifact spells and each player's first nonartifact spell are unrestricted (static, Ethersworn Canonist; enforced in `CastingPermissionService.isAdditionalNonartifactSpellRestricted`)
- `AlternativeCostForSpellsEffect(String, CardPredicate)` (or `(String, CardPredicate, CounterType manaValueCapCounter, boolean oncePerTurn)`) — alternative cast cost; optional counter-based mana-value cap + once-per-turn (As Foretold) + `fromHandOnly` in the 5-arg form for "cast spells from your hand without paying their mana costs" (Omniscience) + `appliesToAllPlayers` in the 6-arg form for "any player may cast … without paying their mana costs" (Aluren). Also honoured inside a `CreateEmblemEffect` payload (Tamiyo, Field Researcher −7) — `CastingCostService.findEmblemFreeCastSource` scans `GameData.emblems`; an emblem has no counters and no per-turn use tracking, so the counter-capped and once-per-turn variants are skipped there Static factory `AlternativeCostForSpellsEffect.genericEqualToManaValue(filter)` makes the alternative cost generic mana equal to the spell's own mana value (Kentaro, the Smiling Cat) — resolve it via `manaCostFor(spellManaValue)`, never by reading `manaCost()`.
- `PlayersCantCastSpellsFromZonesEffect(Set<Zone> zones)` — no player can cast from any zone in `zones` (static, global; only `GRAVEYARD`/`LIBRARY` enforced — Ashes of the Abhorrent passes `Set.of(GRAVEYARD)`, Grafdigger's Cage passes `Set.of(GRAVEYARD, LIBRARY)`)
- `TargetPlayerCantCastSpellTypesThisTurnEffect(Set<CardType>)` — target player can't cast spells of those types for the rest of this turn (spell; declare a player target via `target(...)`). Moonhold ({W} clause, `CREATURE`), Abeyance (`INSTANT`, `SORCERY`)
- `TargetPlayerCantActivateNonManaAbilitiesThisTurnEffect()` — target player can't activate abilities that aren't mana abilities for the rest of this turn, loyalty abilities included (spell; declare a player target via `target(...)`). Abeyance
- `TargetPlayerCantPlayLandsThisTurnEffect()` — target player can't play lands for the rest of this turn (spell; declare a player target via `target(...)`). Moonhold ({R} clause)
- `ControllerCantPlayLandsEffect()` — static; the source's controller can't play lands. Aggressive Mining
- `PlayersCantPlayLandsIfPermanentCountEffect(int minimumCount, PermanentPredicate filter)` — static global restriction; no player may play lands while at least `minimumCount` matching permanents are on the battlefield. Lands entering via effects remain allowed. Limited Resources uses `(10, PermanentIsLandPredicate)`
- `TargetPlayerCantCastCreatureSpellsThisTurnEffect()` — target player can't cast creature spells for the rest of this turn (spell; declare a player target via `target(...)`). Moonhold ({W} clause)
- `WardOfBonesEffect()` — static; each opponent controlling more creatures/artifacts/enchantments than the controller can't cast spells of that type (compared independently), and each opponent controlling more lands can't play lands. Controller never restricted. Ward of Bones
- `DampingEngineEffect()` — static; a player who controls more permanents than every other player can't play lands or cast artifact, creature, or enchantment spells. The source's once-per-turn sacrifice ability can ignore its own restriction until end of turn. Damping Engine
- `CardsCantEnterBattlefieldFromZonesEffect(CardPredicate filter, Set<Zone> zones)` — cards matching `filter` (null = all) can't enter the battlefield from any zone in `zones`; blocks reanimation/undying/library-search-to-battlefield (static, global; only `GRAVEYARD`/`LIBRARY` enforced — Grafdigger's Cage passes `CardTypePredicate(CREATURE)` and `Set.of(GRAVEYARD, LIBRARY)`)

## Choose / name

- `ChooseCardNameOnEnterEffect()` / `(List<CardType> excludedTypes)` / `(List<CardType> excludedTypes, HandAccess)` / `(..., boolean nonbasicLandOnly)` — choose card name ETB. `nonbasicLandOnly=true` offers only nonbasic land card names (Alpine Moon). `HandAccess.LOOK_AT_OPPONENT_HAND` = look first, name anything (Sorcerous Spyglass); `HandAccess.REVEAL_OPPONENT_HAND` = each opponent reveals their hand and the name must be one of those cards (Alhammarret, High Arbiter)
- `SpellsWithChosenNameCantBeCastEffect()` / `(boolean opponentsOnly)` — STATIC; spells with the chosen name can't be cast. Default symmetric (Nevermore); `true` = opponents only, controller may still cast (Gideon's Intervention). Pair with `ChooseCardNameOnEnterEffect()`
- `ChooseCardNameOpponentsCantCastUntilNextTurnEffect()` — SPELL; choose a card name, until your next turn opponents can't cast spells with that name (Comply). Stamps `GameData.opponentsCantCastNamedSpellsUntilControllerNextTurn`
- `YouAndOpponentChooseCardNamesOnEnterEffect()` — ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD; controller then opponent each name a card (no basic land names); stamps `chosenName` + `secondChosenName` (Null Chamber)
- `SpellsAndLandsWithChosenNamesCantBePlayedEffect()` — STATIC, symmetric; spells with either chosen name can't be cast and lands with those names can't be played (Null Chamber). Pair with `YouAndOpponentChooseCardNamesOnEnterEffect()`
- `ReduceCastCostForChosenNameSpellsEffect(int amount)` — STATIC; spells you cast whose name equals the source permanent's chosen name cost `amount` generic less (Council of the Absolute = 2); pair with `ChooseCardNameOnEnterEffect(...)`
- `PlayerHasProtectionFromChosenNameEffect()` — STATIC; controller has protection from the chosen card name (Runed Halo); pair with `ChooseCardNameOnEnterEffect()`
- `PreventDamageFromChosenNameEffect()` — STATIC; prevent all damage (combat + noncombat) to the controller AND permanents they control from sources with the chosen name — damage only, not full protection (Gideon's Intervention). Pair with `ChooseCardNameOnEnterEffect()`; queried by `GameQueryService.isDamageFromChosenNamePreventedForController`
- `ReduceSpellDamageEffect(int amount)` — STATIC replacement; every spell deals its damage minus `amount` to permanents and players (Benevolent Unicorn). Global, all spell types; combat/abilities/fight-bite unaffected, not gated on `isDamagePreventable`, stacks. Queried by `GameQueryService.getSpellDamageReduction` from `DamageSupport`
- `DelayingShieldDamageReplacementEffect()` — STATIC replacement marker; damage to the controller is replaced by delay counters on the carrying permanent, including damage that cannot be prevented. `DamageSupport` applies it to noncombat and combat player damage. `DelayingShieldUpkeepEffect()` removes all delay counters, then expands into one `ForcedCostOrElseEffect(PayManaCost("{1}{W}"), LoseLifeEffect(1))` decision per counter.
- `ReplaceDamageAboveThresholdEffect(int threshold, int replacementDamage)` — STATIC replacement; any source that would deal at least `threshold` damage to a permanent or player deals `replacementDamage` instead. Covers combat and noncombat damage, is not prevention, and applies each matching instance once per damage event. Queried by `GameQueryService.applyDamageReplacementEffects` from `DamageSupport` and `CombatDamageService` (Divine Presence = `(4, 3)`)
- `PreventDamageFromInstantAndSorcerySpellsEffect()` — STATIC; prevent all damage dealt by instant and sorcery spells (Energy Storm). Global; combat/abilities/fight-bite unaffected. Queried by `GameQueryService.isDamageFromInstantOrSorcerySpellPrevented` from `DamageSupport`
- `PreventDamageFromTargetSpellEffect()` — targeted resolution effect; marks the targeted instant or sorcery spell so all damage it deals is prevented for the rest of the turn. Benign `SPELL_ON_STACK` spec; the card's `StackEntryPredicateTargetFilter` narrows it to instants and sorceries (Hidden Retreat). `PreventDamageFromTargetSpellEffect.withLifeGain()` adds life for each damage prevented (Hallow)
- `PreventDamageToSelfFromTargetingSpellsEffect(PermanentPredicate condition)` — STATIC marker: prevents all damage dealt to the source permanent by spells that target it. The optional condition is evaluated at damage time; use `PermanentControllerControlsPermanentPredicate(PermanentIsCreaturePredicate, true)` for "as long as you control another creature" (Bronze Horse). Damage from abilities and spells that do not target the permanent is unaffected
- `BoobyTrapEffect()` — STATIC marker; chosen player reveals draws + name-match sac/10-damage trigger (Booby Trap), detected in DrawService
- `CounterDrawReplacementEffect.counterType()` / `StudyCounterDrawReplacementEffect()` — STATIC marker capability; the controller may put the named counter on the source instead of drawing. Pursuit of Knowledge uses `STUDY_COUNTER` and the existing `ReplaceSingleDrawEffect` may-choice flow
- `RevealFirstDrawDrawOnBasicLandEffect()` — STATIC marker; controller reveals the first card they draw each turn, and if it's a basic land a "draw a card" trigger goes on the stack (Rowen), detected in DrawService (only the turn's first draw is revealed)
- `BreathstealersCryptDrawReplacementEffect(int lifeCost)` / `()` = 3 — STATIC marker; every draw is revealed; creature draws: discard unless pay life (Breathstealer's Crypt), detected in DrawService; mayfx `BreathstealersCryptDrawReplacementHandler`
- `OpponentExtraDrawsRedirectedEffect()` — STATIC marker; opponents' draws other than their first draw-step draw are skipped and the controller draws instead (Notion Thief), detected in `DrawService.resolveDrawCard`
- `ChooseColorOnEnterEffect()` — choose color ETB; `ChooseColorOnEnterEffect(CardColor.BLACK, CardColor.RED)` restricts the pick ("choose black or red", Mangara's Equity); `ChooseColorOnEnterEffect(2)` collects two distinct colors into `Permanent.chosenColors` ("choose two colors", Tablet of the Guilds)
- `ChooseColorForSourceEffect()` — choose a color on **resolution** and store it as the source permanent's chosen color (re-choice; Chromatic Armor). Place last in an ability's effect list — it pauses for input
- `BecomeChosenColorEffect()` — STATIC self-scoped layer-5 color setter: the source permanent becomes its chosen color, replacing its other colors. Pair with `ChooseColorForSourceEffect()` when the choice happens on resolution (Puca's Eye)
- `AllNonlandPermanentsAreChosenColorEffect()` — STATIC layer-5 color setter: all nonland permanents (any controller, incl. source) become the source's chosen color, replacing other colors. Pair with `ChooseColorOnEnterEffect` (Shifting Sky)
- `ControlledNonlandPermanentsAreColorEffect(CardColor)` — STATIC: every nonland permanent you control (source included) becomes that color, replacing its others (CR 105.3). Celestial Dawn (WHITE)
- `AllPermanentsGainChosenColorEffect()` — STATIC layer-5 additive color grant: all permanents (any controller, including lands and the source) gain the source's chosen color *in addition to* their other colors (not replacing). Pair with `ChooseColorOnEnterEffect` (Painter's Servant). Battlefield permanents only — does not recolor spells on the stack or cards in other zones
- `BecomeAllColorsUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — self-scoped layer-5 color set: the source permanent becomes all five colors until end of turn (no target/choice). Floats a `BecomeChosenColorsUntilEndOfTurnEffect` with every color on the source (Scrapbasket `{1}:`)
- `SetChosenColorUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — target permanent becomes the color of your choice until end of turn; `SetChosenColorUntilEndOfTurnEffect(false, false)` is the self-scoped form with no target. Used by Distorting Lens, Blind Seer, and Rainbow Crow
- `BecomeColorlessUntilEndOfTurnEffect(boolean targeted)` — layer-5 color set: a permanent becomes colorless (empty color set, CR 105.3) until end of turn. `false` = self-scoped, no target (Raging Spirit `{2}:`); `true` = `benign(PERMANENT)` target (Ersatz Gnomes `{T}:`). Until-end-of-turn sibling of the static `BecomeColorlessEffect`, whose `GrantScope.ALL_PERMANENTS` form covers every battlefield permanent, including the source (Thran Lens)
- `BecomeColorlessIndefinitelyEffect(boolean targeted)` — the permanent becomes **colorless indefinitely** (CR 611.2b); floats `BecomeColorlessUntilEndOfTurnEffect` with `EffectDuration.PERMANENT`. `false` = self-scoped, `true` = targets a permanent. Indefinite sibling of `BecomeColorlessUntilEndOfTurnEffect`; use it (not `SetTargetColorEffect(null)`) inside an activated ability, since `SetTargetColorEffect` also declares a spell-on-stack target and would make the ability ask for one. Xathrid Gorgon
- `BecomeChosenColorsIndefinitelyEffect([boolean targeted])` — controller chooses one or more colors; the permanent becomes those colors **indefinitely** (floats `BecomeChosenColorsUntilEndOfTurnEffect` with `EffectDuration.PERMANENT`).
